# Progress against design 06 section 14

## Done

**Step 1 Foundations (done)**: env-driven client + ConfigError + EnvBanner; BrowserRouter with basename from `BASE_URL`; folder layout; `data/` core (client, AppError, queryClient, keys); i18n provider (ar/en, RTL/LTR, persisted); Tailwind brand tokens + Manrope/Tajawal; `ui/` kit; scripts `dev, dev:local, build, lint, test, preview`. CI file not written (no CI exists yet).

**Step 2 Auth shell (done)**: AuthProvider, RequireAccess (+ ProjectAccess), RoleHome, `/login`, `/no-access`, shells (App, FullBleed, Public, Founder, Network, Auth) as layout routes, `*` NotFound, ErrorBoundary (root + route errorElement), legacy table + boot shim. Route table follows 06 section 2; unbuilt pages are `ComingSoon` with the step number.

**Step 3 Shared libs with unit tests (done)**:
- `lib/time/tz.js`: one explicit IANA zone per project (`DEFAULT_TIMEZONE` Africa/Cairo), `isValidTimeZone`, `toInstant(date, time, tz)` (DST-safe, fixes CON-1), `wallClock/dateInTz/timeInTz/todayInTz` (fixes CON-10), `minutesOfDayInTz/secondsOfDayInTz`, `formatInTz` (ar/en, Western digits), `describeTimeZone`, `listTimeZones`.
- `lib/time/slots.js`: `ADVANCE_NOTICE_OPTIONS`, `DEFAULTS`, `parseHHMM/formatHHMM/to12h`, `slotsBetween` + `slotsFrom` (the two as-is `calculateDaySlotTimes` unified), `roundMinutes/slotsPerRound` (01-7), `isWithinNotice`, `currentSlotIndex`, `secondsUntilSlotEnd`, `resolveDayConfig` (DUP-1/2/11-18).
- `lib/time/format.js`: `formatDate/Time/DateTime/CalendarDate/Relative` driven by lang + project zone.
- `lib/links.js`: `parseRef/isUuid/isSlug/canonicalRef`, `appBaseUrl`, `publicPath/buildPublicUrl` (agenda/form/landing/booking/view/verify), `buildInviteUrl`, `buildStaffInviteUrl`, `buildShareUrl` (07-13 copy-only for Facebook/LinkedIn), `staffPath` deep links.
- `lib/drive.js`: `isDriveUrl`, `extractDriveId`, `driveImageUrls` (thumb/full candidate lists), `driveDirectUrl`, `driveViewUrl`.
- `lib/export/xlsx.js`: lazy `import('xlsx')` wrapper (`exportRows`, `parseWorkbook`, `toSheetRows`, `safeCell` formula-injection guard, test hook `_setLoader`). `xlsx@0.18.5` added as a dependency; it is NOT in any chunk until a page imports the wrapper.
- `lib/contact.js`: e-mail helpers (`isEmail/normalizeEmail/extractEmail/maskEmail`), `displayName/firstName` (07-5 first logical unit incl. Arabic particles), phone validation to E.164 (`validatePhone`, EG/SA/AE rules from the old `phoneValidation.js`, generic fallback, `SUPPORTED_COUNTRIES`, `formatPhone`) with translation keys instead of literals.
- `lib/constants.js` (staff roles, transportation, visibility, program types, profile palette, image policy), `lib/image.js` (browser WebP full/thumb + path convention), `lib/bilingual.js`, `lib/avatar.js`.

**Step 4 Registry + NodeHost (done)**: manifests for the 10 DB node types, registry with memoised lazy components, `NodeHost` (3 modes, unknown/newer type, error isolation, skeleton), `useNodeConfig`, `useInputPort`, `useSavedStateRefresh`, `invalidateNodeOutputs`, dev-only Note node with `/dev/nodes` playground.

**Step 5 Programs & projects (done)**:
- Programs list wired to `v_programs` (derived completion), cover thumbnails, archived toggle, settings link, **Create program** modal (name ar/en, description ar/en, type, year, visibility, cover; the cover is uploaded after `create_program` because the bucket policy keys on the program id).
- Program overview (`/programs/:id`): cover, description, projects grid, **Create project** modal (name ar/en + timezone, RPC `create_project`), links to canvas / details / settings.
- **Program Settings** (`/programs/:id/settings`): edit fields, immediate cover upload/replace/remove (old files removed), projects list with per-project completion checkbox (`complete_project`/`reopen_project`) and **Complete all** (`complete_all_projects`, decision 01-11), archive/restore.
- **Project Details** (`/projects/:id/details`): name/description, ONE explicit timezone (picker over `Intl.supportedValuesOf`), visibility, location ar/en, map link (validated), offline days, transportation (provided / self_arranged / none), founder-page toggles (`show_description`, `show_details`, nothing shown by default).
- `data/storage.js`: `uploadImage(file, {ownerId, kind})` (resize in browser, full + `_t` thumb, immutable cache headers), `publicImageUrl/publicThumbUrl`, `removeImage`. New `ui/` pieces: `BilingualField`, `ImageField`, `ColorSwatches`, `Avatar`.

**Staff management + staff invitations (08 section 14, done)**:
- `/admin/staff` (permission `staff:manage` = super_admin; `/team` redirects there): staff list from RPC `list_staff` (profile + roles + program memberships), change roles, program access (program-level membership), remove (roles + memberships only), invite by e-mail (roles, programs, e-mail language). The invitation link is shown ONCE with a copy button because e-mail delivery is not wired yet (the e-mail is queued in `email_outbox`). Invitations list with status and revoke.
- `/invite/staff/:token` (AuthShell, public): validates through the anon RPC (shows only e-mail + inviter + expiry), asks ONLY full name + password + colour, signs up with the e-mail from the invitation and `staff_invitation_token` in user metadata (the DB trigger never grants founder on that path and applies the invitation atomically), then calls the idempotent `accept_staff_invitation` and lands on `/` (RoleHome). A signed-in user can accept with the current account (e-mail must match; mismatch explained).
- `/profile` (any signed-in user): name, profile colour, UI language, change password. `UserMenu` shows the avatar in the chosen colour and a Profile item.
- DB side: migration `db/migrations/m24_staff_management_and_invitations.sql` + `db/verify/v24_staff_management.sql` (48 role-switch checks). Details in `db/MIGRATION-LOG.md`.

**Staff vs founder exclusivity (08 section 15, done)**: `db/migrations/m25_staff_founder_exclusivity.sql` + `db/verify/v25_staff_founder_exclusivity.sql` (22 checks). The guarantee is a `user_roles` trigger (SQLSTATE `23514`, token `staff_founder_exclusive` in message + hint); `set_staff_roles` re-checks first; `apply_staff_invitation` answers `reason: 'founder_account'` for founder accounts. Client: `data/errors.js` maps server tokens to specific message keys (`errors.staffFounderExclusive`), the roles modal shows the error inline + toast (and a hint on founder accounts), the invite page shows `invite.reason.founder_account`. Details in `db/MIGRATION-LOG.md`.

**Step 6 Canvas (done)**: `@xyflow/react@12.8.4` in one lazy chunk (`app/canvas/ProjectCanvas.jsx`, only on `/projects/:id`). Tool library (registry groups, search ar/en/key, booking presets as two items, drag onto the canvas or tap "+" = add at the viewport centre, collapsible on phones); nodes/edges from `get_project_graph`; add (`add_node`, default name `"<Type> N"`, overlap-free placement), move (`move_nodes`, optimistic + 800 ms debounce, no revision bump), rename (`save_node` with the rev token; conflict shown), duplicate (`add_node` + extension options + config copy), delete (ConfirmDialog with the connection count; soft delete; Undo in the toast calls `restore_node`, edges come back with the node), connect with client validation mirroring 02 2.5 + DAG per family (`canvasModel.js`) and the server as authority (its message is shown), edge inspector (audience public/platform/participants/pms, enabled, remove with Undo = reconnect with the same config), node inspector (summary, status saved/saving/error, "updated just now" flash, ports + counterparts, View/Edit/Settings links), the `view_host` system node locked with a link to the public project page, minimap + controls + fit view, Delete/Backspace + Esc, save indicator, empty state, saved-state refresh (revision poll -> graph refetch + flash), RTL (sidebar right / inspector left in Arabic; ports stay inputs-left/outputs-right), touch (bottom-sheet inspector, 14 px handles, `connectionRadius` 28).

**Step 7a Lists node + shared people/entity layer (done, 2026-10-12)** (01 section 3.3, 02 section 3, 04 section 5.3; resumed after an interrupted build: the inherited `data/lists.js`, `data/people.js`, `lib/entities.js`, `shared/entities/{EntityCard,EntityGrid,EntityForm,EntityPicker,i18n,manager.logic}`, `ui/Badge`, `ui/ExpandableText`, query keys, the `useNodeConfig` `extraPatch` and migration `m30` were reviewed, lint-fixed (a11y click handler, set-state-in-effect, autoFocus, the non-existent lucide `Linkedin` icon) and kept):
- `data/people.js`: THE way to read/write Athar Network experts (`network_experts`) and canonical `companies` (`listNetworkExperts/getNetworkExpert/createNetworkExpert/updateNetworkExpert`, `listCompanies/getCompany/createCompany/updateCompany`, hooks `useNetworkExperts`, `useCompanies`, `usePeopleMutations`); every row is normalised to ONE `EntityView` (`lib/entities.js`: `{ id, kind, title{en,ar}, subtitle, organization, bio, imagePath, links[], email }`), so no component ever sees `expert_id || id` or the three company vocabularies again.
- `data/lists.js`: `resolve_list` paging (`listItems(nodeId, {filter, kind})` -> `ListItemView[]`: `{ id, itemId|null, virtual, source{kind,id,state}, missing, entity, overlay, status, sortOrder, excluded, card, contact }`), every m06c/m30 write RPC, `materializeItem` (= `ensure_item` on first edit of a virtual item), `useListItems/useListMeta/useViewLink/usePublicList/usePublicTab`, `useListMutations(nodeId, { onChanged })` (each success invalidates `['node', id]` and calls `onChanged` = `invalidateNodeOutputs`), `ensureViewLink`.
- `shared/entities`: `EntityCard` (ONE card for experts/companies/generic, bilingual with `dir="auto"`, per-item field visibility, reserved image aspect, 44 px link targets, `ExpandableText` bio), `EntityGrid` (1/2/3 columns, single item centred + expanded), `EntityForm` (bilingual fields per kind, `ImageField` upload through a page-provided `onUpload`), `EntityPicker` ("Add from the Athar Network" / "Add an existing company": search, add by REFERENCE with duplicate guard, create-and-add), `EntityManager` (table + cards, search/filter/sort, select-all + bulk status/hide/show/export, inline status + notes, up/down + HTML5 drag reorder persisted through `reorder_items` and disabled while filtered, per-row edit/hide/detach/remove, source/missing/hidden badges, loading/error/empty states), `ItemEditor` (manual fields or read-only source summary + overlay: status, notes, custom fields, per-item "show on card"), `ImportItemsModal` (Excel -> manual items through `lib/export/xlsx.parseWorkbook`), `export.js` (Excel columns incl. custom overlay fields; import header aliases).
- `nodes/list`: `manifest` (components, `items` resolver -> `data.js resolveItems`, `contributesViews 'list-tab'`, `showToParticipants: true`), `listConfig.js` (config shape + normalise/validate), `Edit` (manager wired to the RPCs: add manual, pick expert/company, import/export Excel, every action with toast + Undo for hide, ConfirmDialog for remove/detach, connected-sources chips from the graph), `View` (PM preview = the same `ListTab`), `Settings` (name ar/en + visibility + show-to-participants + config in ONE `save_node`, card fields, accent, expandable, hide-title-on-single, statuses vocabulary, custom overlay fields, public link create/copy/open), `ListTab` (the contributed view, see contract below), `i18n`.
- Public route `/view/:nodeRef/:viewKey` is live: `NodeViewPage` dispatches `list-tab` -> `PublicListPage` (`get_public_list`, audience decided by the DB, load-more on a full page, `document.title`), other keys stay ComingSoon.
- DB: `db/migrations/m30_list.sql` + `db/verify/v30_list.sql` (23 checks) - details in `db/MIGRATION-LOG.md`.
- `NodeFrame` got `showSave`, `actions`, `subtitle`, `bodyClassName` (Edit screens whose writes are row-level saves hide the config Save bar).

**Step 7e Booking node + the public booking page (done, 2026-10-13)** (01 section 3.5 / 3.6 / 6.3, 02 section 3.8 + 02-12, 04 sections 3.9 / 5.6 / 5.8, 07 sections 1.5 / 1.6 / 2, 08 decisions 01-1 / 01-7 / 01-12 / 01-15 / 02-12 / 07-3 / 07-6 / 07-10; as-is docs/04 booking features kept without their bugs: C-3 empty shared link, BUG-9 deleting a booked slot, UTC/Cairo/locale mix, three calendar paths; resumed after an interrupted build: the inherited files were reviewed, the lint/test failures fixed and the missing docs written):

- `data/booking.js`: PM reads of the admin payload (`booking_settings` + providers + slots + bookings, needed columns only, ONE `Promise.all`), `get_booking_extras` (calendar jobs, outbox rows, invitations, usable Google connections, names for previews: ONE PM call), the node's public link (kind `booking`, `ensureBookingLink`; the migrated nodes carry one link per legacy provider id and any of them opens the page), provider overlay upsert/update/delete, bulk `add_booking_slots`, block/delete slots, `cancel_booking` / `restore_booking` / `reschedule_booking` / `retry_calendar_job`, the public trio `get_public_booking_page` / `create_booking` / `cancel_booking` and the hooks `useBookingAdmin/useBookingExtras/useBookingLink/usePublicBooking/useBookingMutations`.
- `nodes/booking/bookingConfig.js` (pure, unit-tested): config normaliser (`page {title,intro}`, `rules.max_bookings_per_email`, `slotDefaults`, `emails.{booking_confirmation,booking_cancellation}` ar/en + cc/admin; tolerant of the migrated `{}` / `emails.booking_confirmation.{cc,admin}` shape), `DEFAULT_TEMPLATES` (= `app_private.booking_default_template`), placeholder catalogue (07 section 1.5 set), `validateBookingConfig` (shared link required + https, notice >= 0, max > 0, templates), `planSlots` (wall clock in the PROJECT zone -> ISO instants through `lib/time/slots` + `toInstant`), `splitPlan`/`overlaps` (overlap preview; the DB GiST exclusion decides), `groupByDay`, `slotState`, `syncState` (calendar state from `integration_jobs`: `synced | legacy | pending | waiting_connection | processing | failed | dead | cancelled | none`), `emailIndex`, `mergeProviders` (list items by reference + overlay rows, orphans last), `providerEntity`, `sampleVars`, `bookingColumns` (Excel).
- Edit (row-level saves, no Save bar, `NodeFrame showSave={false}`): **Providers** (the connected list's items by reference with avatar/name/subtitle from the resolver, "takes bookings" creates the `booking_providers` row lazily after `materializeItem` for a virtual item, booking e-mail defaults to the item's contact e-mail, per-provider notice override, remove only without slots, orphan/missing badges, no orphan flash while the list loads), **Availability** (provider select, day from the connected Agenda's `schedule` days or a date picker, from/to/duration/gap defaults from Settings, live preview "N slots will be created (M overlap ...)", generate, single slot, slots grouped by day with free/booked/blocked/past, block/unblock, delete free slots with Undo (re-created), "show past days"), **Bookings** (filters upcoming/past/cancelled/all with counts, booker/provider/when/status, calendar sync badge from the jobs + "waiting for a Google connection" hint, last e-mail chip per key with status, open Meet link, cancel with ConfirmDialog + Undo (`restore_booking`), reschedule to a free slot of the same provider, retry failed sync, invited companies list with booked marks, Excel export through `lib/export/xlsx`).
- Settings (ONE explicit Save = node columns + `config` + `children.settings` in the same `save_node`; `app_private.save_children_booking` validates): preset, who can book (derived: open link vs invited companies), name ar/en, visibility + show-to-participants (off), default advance notice, max bookings per booker (mentor mode), Meet mode unique/shared + shared link (empty = inline error and server error, C-3), Google connection select over `google_connections` ("Not connected" option and badge; never blocks a booking), confirmation + cancellation templates through the new shared `shared/email/TemplateEditor` (ar/en, placeholder chips, live preview with a sample booking, cc/admin), page title/intro ar/en, generator defaults, public link create/copy/open.
- View = the PM preview through the SAME `BookingPageView` + the SAME RPC (audience pm, bookings disabled); interview-mode preview shows providers/slots without an invitee.
- Public page `/book/:nodeRef/:hostRef?` (`app/pages/PublicBookingPage.jsx` + lazy `BookingPageView`): mobile-first and bilingual; provider cards (reference data from the list) -> a compact provider header -> day pills -> free slot buttons in the PROJECT zone (+ the visitor's own time only when it differs for an offered slot) -> details form (name, e-mail, company; prefilled from the logged-in profile or from the invitation; interview mode shows the company as fixed text) -> success screen (Meet link when known at once, "will follow by e-mail" otherwise, the tokenised cancel link once, copy button). States: invitation required, already booked (shows the booking), not available (+ sign-in for anonymous), slot taken / too soon (message + the stale slot disappears: the payload is refetched), per-e-mail limit. `?invite=<token>` reaches `get_public_booking_page` and `create_booking`; `?cancel=<id>&token=<t>` opens a one-step cancel confirmation; legacy `/book/legacy/<provider id>` maps to the provider host. Errors come from the m34 hint tokens through `data/errors.js`.
- Shared kit added: `shared/email/TemplateEditor.jsx` (+ `i18n`), `lib/email.js` (`renderTemplate` = the DB renderer, `sanitizePreviewHtml`, `splitAddresses`, `normalizeEmailTemplate`, `validateEmailTemplate`; also used by `selectionConfig.js`), test setup `asyncUtilTimeout: 5000`.

**Step 7f Mentoring Distribution node + its three surfaces (done, 2026-10-13)** (01 section 3.7, 02 sections 4 / 12, 03 section 9.4, 04 sections 3.10 / 3.17, 08 decisions 01-7 / 02-9 / 02-12 / 02-13; as-is docs/05 section 4 features kept WITHOUT the section 9 bug list: publish resetting config, `validateSessionMove` arg mismatch, sessions without ids, preset shadowing, ignored `break_slots`, per-mentor window only on regenerate, slots-per-day ignoring gaps, ineffective End Time, UTC "today", the unauthenticated timer channel):
- `nodes/distribution/matching.js` (pure, deterministic given a seed, 16 unit tests): THE ONE matching algorithm. One cell per (round, mentor) where the mentor is on the day, inside their window/allowed days and under their per-day maximum; each round is ONE optimal assignment (Hungarian algorithm: maximise the filled cells first, then fairness = companies with fewer meetings first, mentors with fewer filled cells first, then a seeded jitter), a company is in one room per round, a mentor never meets the same company twice in a day, `uniquePairing` never repeats a pair over the node (history = locked/untargeted days + pins), 8 attempts with derived seeds keep the best (fewest empty cells, then most even load), every empty cell is EXPLAINED (`no_companies | not_enough_companies | already_met`). Locked days are never targets; scope `all` regenerates every unlocked day, scope `unlocked` keeps the manual overrides ("pins") and fills around them. Reaches the theoretical maximum on the throwaway data (9 companies x 6 mentors: 54/54 possible pairs, 0 repeats) and handles the migrated size (17 mentors, 71 companies, 28 rounds) and 30 x 100 x 36 in well under a second.
- `nodes/distribution/distributionLogic.js` (pure, 20 tests): payload -> model (`normalizePayload`), node config (`timer {alertSeconds, sounds}`), the Setup draft + `draftToChildren` (the `save_node` children), `effectiveDay` / `totalFromEnd` / `windowFromAgendaSlot` / `pickMentoringSlot` (slots per round gap-aware through `lib/time/slots`; the End field sets the total), `toMatchingInput` / `toReplaceRows`, `validateTimetable` (company/mentor double booked, repeated pair, outside window, over max, beyond round, mentor not on the day, company missing, empty cells), `buildMatrix`, the timer maths (`serverOffsetMs`, `timerRemaining`, `formatMMSS`), `liveRound` (a timer round wins, else the wall clock of the PROJECT zone), `roundPairs`, `myMeetings` (now/next/past/undated), `mentorMeetings`, `distributionSheets` (Excel).
- `data/distribution.js`: PM payload / rules / timer state, the write RPCs, `materializeCandidates` (one `ensure_items` per source list for the still-virtual mentors/companies), the two view links (`getDistributionLinks` / `ensureDistributionLink`, kind `view`, keys `matrix` | `live`), the public reads and hooks (`usePublicDistribution`, `usePublicTimer` = a tiny RPC polled every 5 s only while the page is visible).
- Edit (tabs): **Setup** (round: slot, ONE per-day gap, default start, unique pairing; days: date from the connected Agenda `schedule` BY REFERENCE (`agenda_day_id`, "add the agenda days", the window taken from the agenda's mentoring session) or an own date (02-12 fallback), start / end / total (computed rounds and end), per-day slot/gap override, reorder, remove with a confirmation, locked days read-only; mentors: the connected list by reference as a mentor x day table + per-mentor availability (window from/until, max per day, room/table), mentors no longer in the list stay visible; a note when a Booking node is fed by the same mentors list (01-7 warning only); companies: the connected list), ONE explicit Save = `save_node` + `children` (virtual items are materialised first); **Timetable** (Generate = every unlocked day from scratch; Regenerate unlocked = keeps the manual pins; per-day "Regenerate this day"; optional fixed seed; a confirmation before replacing sessions; the result card with fairness numbers and the explanation of every empty cell; publish toggle that touches nothing else; per-day lock; the matrix with drag-and-drop swap/move and a click dialog (assign / swap with the company in another room / clear), each manual edit ONE validated RPC + an Undo toast = the inverse change; issues line with the stored-data validation; Excel; print), **Live** (the PM remote + the board). Generate saves a dirty Setup first (as-is BUG-13).
- Settings: name ar/en, visibility private / platform / public, show-to-participants (stored; the founder page is step 8), publish status + toggle, timer alert window + sounds (node config), the two public links (Matrix, Live) created lazily, one `save_node`. View = the PM preview of the three surfaces through the SAME components.
- Surfaces: (a) `MyScheduleTab` = the Agenda tab `my-meetings-tab` ("My schedule / Who do I meet next", mobile-first: company picker with search, or the signed-in founder's own company preselected via `company_founders`, now / next card with mentor, room, time and minutes to go, the full list, a mentor-centric mode; the choice is remembered per device); (b) `MatrixTab` = the Agenda tab `sessions-tab` AND the public `/view/:ref/matrix` (mentors x rounds per day, today's day first, current round marked, company highlight, print landscape, every day printed); (c) `LiveBoard` on `/view/:ref/live` (big countdown from the server timestamps, round X of N, who meets whom now and next, alert colour + tick/alarm sounds the viewer enables, full screen; without a timer round it follows the project-zone clock). `TimerControls` (set round, start/pause, +-10 s / +-1 min, reset, previous/next, follow the clock) is rendered only in the staff editor and on the live page when the DB says the viewer is a PM. `app/pages/DistributionViewPage.jsx` + `app/shells/PublicWideShell.jsx` (the 1200 px container) + routes `view/:nodeRef/matrix|live` (static segments outrank the generic `view/:nodeRef/:viewKey`); the legacy `/view/<event id>/mentoring/matrix|live` links redirect there and the DB resolves the old project id.
- DB: `db/migrations/m35_distribution.sql` + `db/verify/v35_distribution.sql` (85 checks) - details in `db/MIGRATION-LOG.md`.

- DB (`db/migrations/m39_founder.sql` + `db/verify/v39_founder.sql`, 52 checks; see `db/MIGRATION-LOG.md`): `list_my_programs(include_withdrawn)`, `get_founder_program_page(project)` (replaces the m06c stub: the header + the ORDERED list of ENABLED blocks, built by CALLING `list_founder_agendas` / `list_founder_libraries` / `list_founder_landings` / `list_my_certificates`), `get_founder_block(project, type, node, after)` (lazy payloads of the agenda days, the mentors / companies lists and My schedule, re-checked), `app_private.my_participation`, `founder_project_dates` and **`link_my_participations`** (a participant created from a Selection response is linked to the founder ACCOUNT whose VERIFIED e-mail is the response `founder_email`: without it a founder "accepted through selection" could never see the program, see F4).
- `data/founder.js` (`useMyPrograms`, `useFounderProgramPage`, `useFounderBlock`, `getFounderBlock`; query keys `myPrograms` / `founderProgram` / `founderBlock`), `app/pages/founder/`: `FounderHomePage` (`/alumni/founder/dashboard`: greeting + the active programs + "See all"), `FounderProgramsPage` (`/alumni/founder/programs`: `ProgramCard` per project = cover with the dark overlay + program and project names (ar / en), status badge accepted / attended / withdrawn, completed badge, dates, location, the whole card is the target through ONE link; mobile-first one column, two from `sm`; explained empty state; skeletons; error + retry; `withdrawn` hidden, see F3), `FounderProgramPage` (`/alumni/founder/programs/:projectId`: ONE request, `ProgramHeader` (back link, cover = the project / agenda cover, names, status + completed chips, dates + the project zone), a phone `SectionNav` (sticky chips, shown from three blocks up, scroll-to + marker through IntersectionObserver), then the blocks in SERVER order, an unknown block type is ignored; "nothing to show yet" explanation when the PM enabled nothing; `withdrawn` = header + status card only; not a participant = "not available" with no hint that the project exists), `programLogic.js` (pure: `splitPrograms`, `visibleBlocks`, `nextUp` in the project zone, `safeMapUrl`, `statusTone`), `i18n.js` (`founder.*`, ar / en).
- **Block registry** `app/pages/founder/blocks/index.js` (`BLOCKS`: `description, agenda, mentors, companies, details, library, landing, schedule, certificate`, each a lazy chunk) + `BlockFrame` (anchor, heading, `ErrorBoundary` + `Suspense`: a block that crashes or whose query fails shows ITS OWN error + retry, the page stays) + `QueryBlock`: `DescriptionBlock` (expandable), `AgendaBlock` (the "Program Agenda" button of `FounderAgendaButtons` + a "Happening now / Next up" line from the agenda days, times each in a bidi isolate), `PeopleBlock` (mentors / companies through the SAME `ListTab` with the new COMPACT `EntityCard` row: avatar / logo beside the text; "Show more" follows the cursor), `LocationBlock` (location + "Open in maps" (new tab, `noopener`, http(s) only), in-person days (plural ar / en), transportation provided / on your own), `LibraryBlock` / `LandingBlock` / `CertificateBlock` (the existing `FounderLibraryButtons` / `FounderLandingButtons` / `FounderCertificateButton`, which got an optional `items` prop so the page's rows are used without a second request), `MyScheduleBlock` (`MyScheduleTab`, the founder's own company preselected).
- Routes: `alumni/founder/dashboard`, `alumni/founder/programs`, `alumni/founder/programs/:projectId` are real; `alumni/founder/programs/:projectId/library` redirects to the Program page (`FounderProgramRedirect`); the agenda route of step 7c is unchanged. Guards: `RequireAccess area="founder"` (a PM / alumni manager opening the founder area is bounced to their own home, a founder opening a staff route to the founder home; `app/guards/founderArea.test.jsx` + the route-table test).
- Shared edits: `EntityCard` `dense` is now the compact row (the prop existed but nothing used it) and `EntityGrid` keeps dense cards at one column on a phone / two from `sm`; `ListTab` forwards `dense`; the Selection Participants hint now says a founder is linked automatically by the e-mail of the application.

**Design system applied (08 section 16, 2026-10-12)** - the athareg.com identity (`web/design-system/`) on the whole of `web/src` with the owner's palette (blue primary, yellow accent/CTA with black text, black as a secondary touch); README "Design system" is the reference. What changed:
- Foundations: `styles/index.css` rewritten (semantic HSL tokens + raw palette, `[data-surface="dark"]` scope, `:lang(ar)` typography overrides, base headings in Hepta Slab, `.site-header`, `.nav-link`, `.panel`, `.hover-grow`, overlays, React Flow controls in the palette); `tailwind.config.js` extended from `tailwind.tokens.js` (named type sizes, radii, shadows, spacing, containers, `xs`/`header` screens); `index.html` loads Hepta Slab 800 / Manrope 400-700 / Questrial / Tajawal and the brand favicon; `public/brand/` holds the logo (full + 150x50), favicon and hero pattern (`lib/brand.js` resolves them under `BASE_URL`).
- Kit: `Button` (variants/sizes in `ui/buttonStyles.js`, `buttonClass()` for links), `Field`/`PhoneField`/`SearchBar`/`BilingualField`/`ImageField` on the site input spec, `Badge` tones re-mapped, `Modal`/`ConfirmDialog` 15px + black backdrop, `Toast` tone bar, `States` (black spinner, dashed surface empty state), `Img` overlay/shadow/rounded props, `Avatar`/`ColorSwatches` with black marks on light swatches, `ExpandableText` in Questrial, brand FA icons under `ui/icons/brand/`.
- Chrome and shells: `SiteHeader` + `SiteFooter` + `NavBar` + `UserMenu` + `LanguageSwitch` + `BrandMark`; all six shells rebuilt on them (gradients and the noise overlay removed everywhere); Login / staff invite / NotFound / NoAccess / ConfigError / crash screen on the hero pattern with slab titles and the yellow CTA.
- Pages and nodes: Programs cards = cover + black 0.7 overlay + white title (ink band when there is no cover) with Badge status pills; Program overview/settings, Project details, Profile, Staff (+ invitations, modals), NodePage mode nav, `NodeFrame` (15px panel, yellow "unsaved" chip, 25px body padding), canvas chrome (`NodeCard` 7px + black selection, `ToolSidebar` rows, `NodeInspector`, `FAMILY_COLORS` from the palette), `EntityCard`/`EntityGrid`/`EntityManager` (15px cards, `#f2f2f2` table header), `ListTab` and the public list page, the Form Builder (builder, responses, settings, cover picker) and `FormRenderer` (the site's `#f2f2f2` form card, slab title, 6px yellow submit, success in green). Every `hover:bg-accent` (which turned yellow with the new tokens), `shadow-premium`/`shadow-sm`, ad-hoc emerald/amber chips and gradient text were replaced.
- Profile/list accent palette (`lib/constants.PROFILE_COLORS`) is now the site palette (9 colours; stored values outside it still render).
Deviations from `components-map.md` (written for the black-dominant site scheme before the owner chose blue-primary): `primary` tokens are blue, the CTA is the `cta`/`accent` yellow (so `Button primary` = yellow fill, `secondary` = blue fill; the map's "secondary = black" became the `outline` variant + `[data-surface="dark"]`); focus rings and nav hover are blue, not black; the header nav collapses below `lg` (1024px) instead of the site's 922px because the staff nav carries up to 7 links (links tighten to `px-3 text-base` until `xl`); the user name hides between `lg` and `xl`; the logo is 120px under `sm` and the language switch is icon-only under 480px so a signed-in phone header fits 375px; the canvas family colours leave yellow out (unreadable on the `#f8f8f8` canvas); `--muted-foreground` is `#666` instead of the site's `#7a7a7a` (AA on white at small sizes) and error text uses a darker red than the `#f56c6c` border; the mobile menu panel has no enter animation; the bundle keeps one Hepta Slab weight (800) and the browser matches 700 to it.

- **Withdrawn replaces "Hide" (m40, decision 17.1; 2026-10-13)**: the PM marks a founder / company / expert as WITHDRAWN (a labelled, reversible, manual action: confirmation with an optional PM-only reason, Undo toast, "Reinstate"); nothing is automatic, no e-mail is sent. The fact has ONE home: for the two IDENTITY kinds of list items (a form-response applicant, a canonical company) it is the project-level `participants` row (`status = 'withdrawn'`, so the Selection board, every list fed by a stage, the Participants page, Distribution, Booking, Agenda tabs, the founder blocks and the certificate eligibility all DERIVE from it and a withdrawal made on ANY of them is the same fact); for anything else (an Athar Network expert, a manual item) it is `list_items.withdrawn_at / _by / _reason`. Server side ONE predicate `app_private.is_withdrawn(project, form_response_id, company_id, item_flag)` is called by 17 consumers patched in place (see `db/MIGRATION-LOG.md`); the resolver takes `include_withdrawn` and returns `withdrawn` + `withdrawal {at, reason, source}` to the PM managers only, the public / platform / founder payloads drop the person. UI: `shared/entities/WithdrawDialog.jsx` + `useWithdrawFlow.jsx` (the ONE interaction used by the Lists manager, the Selection board and the Participants page), `EntityManager` (red "Withdrawn" chip + reason, "Mark as withdrawn" / "Reinstate" row + bulk actions, a presence filter All / Active only / Withdrawn / Hidden (legacy)), Selection board ("Withdrawn (n)" pill, kept out of the stage counts, drawer box with Reinstate, e-mail recipients skip them), Participants page (the status select offers accepted / attended only; Withdrawn is its own action), Booking (provider row + invitee chips), Distribution (PM Setup / Timetable / Matrix chips, warnings in the validation, the live board announces nobody withdrawn), Certificates (Issue tab chip + "withdrawn after issue" flag). The old Hide is gone from the UI; the 2 migrated hidden experts show a "Hidden (legacy)" chip with two ways out (mark withdrawn, restore).

## Deviations from 06 (and why)

1. No `data/*.hooks.js` split: hooks sit next to functions in `programs.js`/`canvas.js`/`staff.js` (fewer files at this size). Consequence for tests: pages are tested with full module mocks that re-implement the hooks.
2. `.env.development` (committed) not used: the root `.gitignore` ignores `.env.*`, so `dev:local` runs mode `development` and reads the git-ignored `.env.local`. `dev:live` not created (needs the owner's go-ahead).
3. Roles come from `user_roles` directly: the migrated DB has no `get_user_roles` RPC.
4. `/profile` lives inside PublicShell (any signed-in user), not a staff shell.
5. Node `ports` shape follows the DB (`families[]`, `kind`, `cardinality`, `required`) instead of 06's single `type` string.
6. Note node has no `node_types` row, so it is demonstrated only with the in-memory adapter.
7. Legacy `/view/:id/<tab>` rules shadow a new `/view/:nodeRef/:viewKey` whose viewKey equals `experts|startups|library|join-athar`; new view keys must avoid these.
8. Missing translations of the skip link and the English "planned for" text on placeholders (placeholders are temporary).
9. Vite chunking: react/router and supabase-js are the heavy vendor chunks; they load on every page.
10. `vitest`/`@vitejs/plugin-react` pinned to versions compatible with Vite 7; `testTimeout` raised to 15 s (jsdom per file under load).
11. Staff admin route is `/admin/staff` (the 06 table had `/team` for "project managers / roles admin"; `/team` now redirects). Permission name `staff:manage` added to `lib/permissions.js`.
12. Program cover upload needs the program id (bucket policy), so "create with cover" is two steps (RPC, then upload + update); a failed upload leaves the program without cover and tells the user.
13. The invitation e-mail is only queued (design 07 worker is stage 3); the admin copies the link. The e-mail link origin is passed by the client (`appBaseUrl()`, validated `https?://` server-side) because the DB does not know the app URL.
14. Program visibility is editable but no public program page exists yet (step 9).
15. In dev (StrictMode) the "area not available" toast can appear twice on a redirect (double effect run); production shows one.
16. Canvas (step 6): no explicit "Save layout" button (06 3.1): positions autosave 800 ms after a drag and the top bar shows saving/saved/failed; a separate button would save nothing more. Edge `audience` offers the four DB values (`public|platform|participants|pms`, 04 3.4) rather than 02-14's three. Edge undo re-creates the edge through `connect` with the same config (no edge tombstone exists), so an edge whose target was deleted meanwhile cannot be restored. Undo for node delete lives in the toast (8 s) and uses `restore_node`; there is no multi-step undo stack. The `view_host` node is locked (not deletable) but draggable (its position is layout only). Duplicate copies `config`, `visibility`, `show_to_participants` and the extension option the RPC accepts (list subkind, booking mode) but not children (questions, days, cards): those are node-owned data of step 7 editors. The client cannot see a list's subkind (not in `get_project_graph`), so list `items` -> kind-specific inputs are accepted client-side and decided by the DB. Keyboard edge creation is not available (06 says the inspector pickers cover it; not built yet). `COPY_WORD` ("copy"/"نسخة") is a bilingual constant, not a dictionary key, because names are stored in both languages at once. The dev `note` type is not in the canvas sidebar (no `node_types` row; it stays a `/dev/nodes` playground).
17. Toast got an optional `action` ({label, onClick}) for Undo.
18. Lists (step 7a): the 01 section 3.3 config proposal is trimmed to what the DB resolver honours: `config.card {showFields, accent, expandable, hideTitleOnSingle}`, `config.statuses[]` (the list's own status vocabulary; `list_items.status` stays free text for lists, the UI offers the vocabulary; the column is hidden when empty) and `config.overlayFields[]` (custom PM-only columns, 02 section 3.4). `columns[]` with a per-column `source/ref` mapping, `sort.mode`, `manualAdd`, `networkImport` and `titleOverride` are not implemented: the resolver maps form answers with fixed canonical keys (`company_name`, `founder_name`, `logo`, `bio`, `founder_email`, `phone`), the tab title is the node name, manual add / network pick are always allowed for the matching kind, and `display_override` (02-2) has no UI yet. Per-item card visibility is written as `overlay.card.show {image,title,subtitle,organization,bio,links}`; the migrated legacy keys (`show_photo`, `show_name`, `show_description`, `show_links`...) are read through one mapping (`lib/entities.cardShowOf`); legacy keys without a card field (`show_role`, `show_stage`, `show_founder`, `show_location`, `show_governorate`) are ignored because the resolver's `display` has no such fields.
19. Lists: overlay edits are immediate row-level saves (02 section 6), not a draft: the Edit screen has no Save button (`NodeFrame showSave={false}`); the DB bumps the revision on every write, so downstream views refresh through the normal poll. Only Settings uses the draft + explicit Save.
20. Lists: the list's own public page uses a NEW public link kind `view` / `view_key 'list-tab'` (m30 `get_public_list`), created on demand from Settings (the DB has no view links for lists; the migrated lists are reachable through the project host tabs). Public pagination: the DB always returns a `next` cursor, so "Load more" is shown only when the last page was full (100 rows).
21. Lists: `delete_list_item` (m30) hard-deletes a manual item or a pick ROW (never the source record); a form-fed row can only be hidden (`exclude_item`), matching 02 section 3.6. "Detach to manual" is one-way (ConfirmDialog, no Undo) because the source link is dropped by the RPC.
22. Lists: `EntityPicker` lists `network_experts` for `kind = expert` (team members behind a checkbox); creating a new expert from the picker writes `network_experts` directly under RLS (PM/super_admin insert policy), creating a company writes `companies` with `origin_project_id` = the project and `created_by` = the caller. "Update network profile" (explicit edit of the shared record) is not in the list UI: it belongs to the Athar Network page (step 7, `/network/experts`), the list only links by reference.
23. Lists: Excel import (as-is feature) creates MANUAL items only (never network/company records); columns are matched by header aliases (`shared/entities/export.js IMPORT_ALIASES`). Excel export goes through `lib/export/xlsx` (first consumer of the lazy chunk).
24. Lists: images uploaded from a list (manual item photo/logo, new network profile photo) are stored under the PROJECT folder (`<project id>/photo|logo/`) because the storage policy keys on project/program membership; a network profile photo therefore lives in the project where it was created (the registry page may later re-home it under `global/`).
25. Public route `/view/:nodeRef/:viewKey` is now a real page (`NodeViewPage`) that dispatches on the key; legacy keys (`experts|startups|library|join-athar`) stay shadowed by the legacy redirects (deviation 7).
26. Form Builder: public submission goes through the anon-callable RPC `submit_public_form` (m31) instead of 04 §5.9's "edge function after CAPTCHA/rate limit then service-role `submit_form_response`": no edge function exists in the new stack yet, so the RPC itself carries the honeypot, a per-(form, ip) rate limit (20/10 min from the PostgREST headers) and the full server-side validation; `submit_form_response` stays service-role only. No CAPTCHA (needs the owner's provider choice, 03 OD-5).
27. Form Builder: respondent file uploads use a GUARDED anon INSERT policy on the private `form-uploads` bucket (`app_private.form_upload_allowed`: form open, public/platform with an enabled link, has a file question, per-form hourly cap, strict path/extension) instead of 03 §9.2's signed upload URL from an edge function. Same reason; swap when the edge function ships. Files are read by PMs through signed URLs (existing policy).
28. Form Builder: the 01 §3.2 config proposal is trimmed: `isActive/opensAt/closesAt/maxResponses/oneResponsePerEmail/cover` live in the `forms` row (04 §3.7, saved as `children.form`), `name` is the node name, `sections/questions` are relational; `config` keeps `description`, `thank_you {message{en,ar}, image_path, show_image}`, `social {5 platforms + custom[]}`, `share {title, description, image_path}` and `notify_pm`. The link preview (`share`) is stored in `config`, not yet copied to `public_links.og_*` (no OG renderer exists); `export.googleSheetUrl` became `forms.export_sheet_id/tab` (04). Question `validation` is written as `{minLength,maxLength,min,max,pattern,customError{en,ar},allowed_countries}`; the legacy `minValue/maxValue`/string `customError` are read by both the client and `form_check_answers`.
29. Form Builder: the `view` output port ("Apply now" tab, 01 §3.2 0..1) is not implemented: the DB `node_types.form_builder.ports` row has only `responses` and `link`, and embedding a form inside the Agenda page is a later Agenda-builder decision. The "Other" choice is stored as the free text itself (no `__other__`/`__other_text__:` sentinels, docs/04 §4.3); migrated answers that carry the sentinels are shown verbatim.
30. Form Builder: the thank-you tokens are `{name} {company} {email} {phone}` (plus `{{...}}` and the Arabic legacy tokens) replaced by string substitution; `{name}` = founder_name role, falling back to the company name (A-14 fixed: no RegExp from labels, no first-key fallback). Instagram is a source-tracking channel only (no share intent exists, A-13).
31. Form Builder: assigning a semantic role to an existing question changes its key (DB CHECK `key = semantic`); m31 re-keys the stored answers in the same save so nothing is lost (this touches `form_responses` of that form only when the PM saves that change). Response `status` is `submitted | reviewed | spam` (04), edited from the table/drawer; the as-is `approved/rejected` values belong to Selection.
32. Form Builder: the PM notification e-mail is only queued (`email_outbox`, one row per project manager, no sender connection yet), like staff invitations; the Google Sheet append is only an `integration_jobs` row (`sheets_append`) carrying ids, never a key or the answers (docs/04 S-1/S-5 are the worker's concern).
33. `FormRenderer` is a node component imported by `app/pages/PublicFormPage.jsx` (same pattern as `ListTab` for the public list page); the honeypot field is an off-screen text input named `website` (aria-hidden, tabindex -1).
34. Agenda: the 01 §3.1 config proposal is trimmed: `days[]` are relational (`agenda_days/agenda_slots`, saved as `children.days`), `tabOrder[]` became the per-edge `order_index/label/is_enabled/audience` of 02 §4.2 (edited in the Agenda Settings, written with `update_edge`), `importers` is Excel only (01-9; the migrated `legacySheetUrl` is kept in the config but never rendered), `seo` is not stored (the page sets OG meta from the project name/cover and the header title; `public_links.og_*` has no renderer yet, same as forms), `defaultLang` is the site language switch. `header.font*` controls are dropped (the design system fixes the fonts; stored legacy keys are kept untouched), `header.cover.imageUrl` is the PROJECT cover (01-2), `backgroundImageUrl` (page background) is not rendered (kept as `backgroundPath`), `footerPath` is rendered under the agenda tab.
35. Agenda: there are TWO hosts, both served by the same page and RPCs: the implicit `view_host` (project page; the 16 migrated links `/agenda/<name-slug>` live on it and list the agenda node as a tab through its edge) and an Agenda node with its own `agenda` link (01 §3.1 "each Agenda node is its own view page": the built-in agenda tab first, then the edges into its `tabs` port). The Agenda Settings therefore edits its own tab bar AND, when the node is a tab of the host, the project page's tab bar; the project page link is created from the same screen (`ensure_public_link(null,'project')`). The canvas inspector's "Open the project page" link (`/agenda/<host node id>`) only resolves for migrated projects (legacy_ref); a new project needs the link created from the Agenda Settings first - left as is (step 6 code).
36. Agenda: tab deep links use derived slugs (`agendaLogic.tabsWithSlugs`), not edge ids: `agenda`, the English list title (`experts`, `companies`, matching the legacy `/view/:id/experts|startups` redirects for the usual names; `startups` -> `companies` already in the legacy table), fixed words for library/landing/distribution, `-2` suffixes on collisions, `<view_key>-<n>` when the title has no Latin letters. Renaming a list changes its slug (the first tab is the fallback for an unknown slug).
37. Agenda: the host page refetches `get_agenda_view` on focus and every 60 s while visible and the agenda tab ticks its "now" marker every 30 s (as-is polled every 30 s; no socket, 02-5). The tab bar is not sticky (the day pills are, under the site header).
38. Agenda: removing a session needs no confirmation (nothing is written before Save; the toast offers Undo); removing a day asks (it carries its sessions). The Excel import never writes: it merges into the draft (append by day name/date or replace) and the PM saves. Migrated `legacySheetUrl` values (6 projects) are not offered as a sync source (01-9).
39. Agenda: `list_founder_agendas` is the founder exposure; the step-8 founder Program page must render `app/pages/founder/FounderAgendaButtons` (nothing is rendered while no node is exposed). `audience` of a founder on a PUBLIC agenda is `public` (the DB answers `participant` only for platform items): same content, so it does not matter for the agenda.
40. Selection: the 01 §3.4 config proposal is trimmed: `transitions` is always `free` (the as-is state diagram allowed every move; the reason modal + origin stage cover the hold flow), `sheetImport` is kept in the config but has no UI (01-9 / 02 §9: an inbound Sheet importer belongs to the Form node when wanted), `stage:<key>` dynamic output ports became the fixed DB ports `approved/rejected/holding/interview/all` (the stages of a type feed them: `app_private.selection_port_statuses`), and there is no `participants` output port (the Participants page reads `list_participants`; a `rows:participant` port exists in the DB families for Certificates). Reviewer data: `selection_reviews` is ONE decision record per item (as migrated), per-reviewer scores/notes are a NEW table `selection_scores` (04 §3.5 only had the single review); `tech_status` stays a free overlay column (editable in the item editor).
41. Selection: templates are stored per key as `{subject, body_html, subject_ar, body_html_ar, cc[], admin[], enabled}` (the migrated keys plus the Arabic pair; 01 §3.4 has one template per key). The node-type defaults exist twice on purpose (client preview `DEFAULT_TEMPLATES`, server `app_private.selection_default_template`) so a stage sends out of the box; a stage override replaces the default pair for that language (the Arabic side starts empty and falls back to English at send time). `project_manager_name` = the acting PM's profile name (07: `sender_name` is an alias). `cc` + `admin` are both carried as CC (the as-is admin addresses were CC too); the real sender identity is the worker's (07-3), so `sender_connection_id` is null on queued rows (same as m24/m31).
42. Selection: the virtual-membership rule means a never-touched applicant has no status and is shown in the `inbox` stage; it leaves the stage through an output port only once a PM decided (the DB resolver filters on `status`). "Hide" (`exclude_item`) = withdraw the applicant from this stage (and from every downstream stage); detach/remove are not offered on a stage. Bulk status changes of decision stages ask for ONE reason for the whole batch.
43. Selection: `{{booking_link}}` is resolved only at send time from the stage's Booking node (config override, else the first enabled edge from the stage to a `booking` node): the invitation token is per applicant AND per Booking node, revoked and re-issued on every (re)send, 30 days (`create_invitations` semantics). The public booking page route accepts `/book/:ref?invite=<token>` (host segment optional); the Booking builder must read `?invite` and pass it to `get_public_booking_page` / `create_booking`.
44. Participants: `list_participants` lists virtual "eligible" rows ONLY for stages whose "accepted -> participants" toggle is on (02 §7.3 step 1); the m06c body listed every selection node's accepted items, which would have turned the 48 migrated accepted applicants into participants without a PM decision. The "at most one enrolling stage per project" rule (02 §7.3) is not enforced (the DB would need every selection config; the Settings hint says "last stage only"). A founder ACCOUNT cannot be added by hand yet (no user search RPC; `assign_participant(p_user)` exists): manual add = a company record; the founder link happens from the Alumni area (03-8).
45. Participants: `mark_all_attended` only touches materialised rows, so the page enrols the eligible stages first (`enroll_accepted` per stage) and then calls it; "unmark absentees" = the row's status select back to `accepted`. Withdraw keeps the row (`withdrawn`), reinstate sets `accepted` again; `request_withdrawal` (founder side) only shows as a flag here.
46. The "open original response" link is offered when exactly one form feeds the chain (the resolver row carries no form id); with several forms the drawer still shows every answer but no deep link.

47. Booking: the 01 §6.3 config proposal is trimmed to what the DB honours: the node's own facts are the `booking_settings` row (mode, default notice, Meet mode + shared link, organizer connection; saved as `children.settings` through `save_node`, validated by `app_private.save_children_booking`), `nodes.config` keeps `page {title,intro}`, `rules.max_bookings_per_email`, `slotDefaults` and `emails.{booking_confirmation,booking_cancellation}`. Not implemented: per-day slot config (`resolveDayConfig` is available in `lib/time/slots` but slots are materialised rows, 04 §3.9), a per-booker "max per booker" for interview mode (an invited company books exactly once), a custom field list on the booking form (name, e-mail, company only), the `booking-tab` out port renders nothing yet (placeholder view, the host shows "not available yet").
48. Booking: the tokenised links are two different things. The INVITATION token (interview mode, 01-15) is issued only by the Selection stage's send (`booking_invitations`, hashed, 30 days); the CANCEL token is created by `create_booking` per booking (hash in `bookings.cancel_token_hash`, the clear value returned once and put in the confirmation e-mail's `{{cancel_link}}`). The m06d HMAC token is still accepted when the DB setting `app.settings.booking_hmac_key` exists (the migrated bookings have no hash and no HMAC key locally, so they cannot be cancelled by token; PMs cancel them in the Bookings tab).
49. Booking: there is NO edge function in this step. The booking row, the `integration_jobs` rows (`calendar_create` on booking, `calendar_update` on reschedule, `calendar_cancel` on cancel/PM cancel, idempotency keys per booking) and the `email_outbox` rows (confirmation, cancellation, re-confirmation on reschedule/restore; null sender, worker resolves it, 07-3) are written by the RPCs in ONE transaction; a booking is never blocked or rolled back by Google. Without a Google connection the job stays `pending` and the UI says "Waiting for a Google connection" (not an error). `release_integration_job` (service role) is added for the worker to hand a job back without counting a failure. The design/contract for the worker is in "Handover for calendar sync" below.
50. Booking: confirmation e-mails are queued in the language of the page the booker used (`bookings.lang`), with the node's template for that language, else its other language, else the node-type default (same defaults in `bookingConfig.DEFAULT_TEMPLATES` and `app_private.booking_default_template`). `{{meet_link}}` is the real link when it is known at booking time (shared mode) and a sentence "the meeting link will follow by e-mail" otherwise; unique-link mode gets its link from the worker (07-6: created WITH the calendar event, the booking page does not wait for Google). `cc` + `admin` addresses of a template are both carried as CC (as Selection, deviation 41).
51. Booking: the PM "Cancel" is undoable (`restore_booking`, only while the slot is still free: it re-confirms, queues a new `calendar_create` and a new confirmation e-mail, `round` 2 in the idempotency key) - the as-is flow had no undo. A reschedule keeps the booking row and its Meet/event ids (`calendar_update`), moves it to a free slot of the same NODE (not only the same provider) and re-sends the confirmation; the UI offers the same provider's free slots.
52. Booking: slot generation is client-side planning (`lib/time/slots` + `toInstant` in the project zone) sent as ISO instants to `add_booking_slots` (max 500 per call, overlap skipped by the GiST exclusion, never an error); the "overlap" count in the preview is advisory. The old per-day "calculateDaySlotTimes" duplicates are gone. Slots are wall-clock times in the PROJECT zone (07-10); the public page shows the project time and, only when it differs, the visitor's local time.
53. Booking: `get_public_booking_page` hides slots inside the advance-notice window and booked/blocked slots (view `v_booking_slots_open`), returns at most 500 open slots, never returns the provider's booking e-mail or any Meet link, and answers `invitation_required` / `already_booked` / `open`; a project manager previewing an interview page without a token sees providers and slots without an invitee. The audience comes from the link (anon: public nodes; participants: platform; PMs: everything); a private node answers `null` (no existence leak).
54. Booking: the template editor is the new shared `shared/email/TemplateEditor`; the Selection E-mails tab still carries its own copy of the editor around its send panel (same shape: ar/en, placeholder chips, preview). Migrating Selection onto the shared component is a refactor left for later (both already share `lib/email.js` helpers and the DB renderer).
55. Booking: dev server port. `npm run dev:local` binds 5174, which Windows reserves on this machine (excluded TCP range 5141-5240, `preview_start` refuses it); the browser walk-through used `vite --mode development --host 127.0.0.1 --port 5400`. `.claude/launch.json` was not changed (outside `web/`).

56. Distribution: the 01 section 3.7 config proposal is trimmed to what 04 stores. The node's own facts are the `distributions` row (slot, ONE gap, default start, unique pairing, publish flag, seed) + `distribution_days` (+ `distribution_day_mentors`) + `distribution_mentor_rules`, saved as `children` of `save_node` (`save_children_distribution`); `nodes.config` keeps only `timer {alertSeconds, sounds}`. `breaks[]`, `generator.mode`, `totalRoundMin` per day as a node-wide value, `controlRoles` and a `uniquePairingScope` are not stored (01-7: per-day gap only, scope = the node, timer control = project managers only by construction).
57. Distribution: "Generate" and "Regenerate unlocked" run the SAME function. Both skip locked days (01-7); the difference is the manual pins: Generate replaces every session of the unlocked days (confirmation names how many), Regenerate unlocked keeps the sessions the PM edited by hand (`is_manual_override`) as fixed cells and fills around them. Every click uses a new seed unless a fixed seed is chosen; the seed used is stored in `distributions.seed`.
58. Distribution timer: 03 section 9.4 proposed private Realtime channels with Realtime Authorization; this build uses NO Realtime at all. The state is ONE row of server timestamps (`distribution_timers`), written only by the PM RPC, read by visitors through a tiny RPC polled every 5 s while the page is visible (and the full payload only refetched when the polled revision is newer), and every screen computes the remaining time locally from `ends_at` and the server clock offset (design 08 P2: no persistent sockets for visitors). Cost: a control action reaches the screens within about 5 s. If sub-second sync is ever needed, a private channel can carry a "refresh now" ping on top of the same rows.
59. Distribution availability: a mentor's days are expressed ONCE, by the per-day mentor lists (the mentor x day table); `distribution_mentor_rules.days` (index list, migrated values `{0}`, `{}`) is still honoured by every generation and validation and shown as a "migrated restriction" with a Clear button, but the UI no longer edits it. `room` is a NEW nullable column on the rule (a rule row can exist only to carry a room): the as-is model had no room, the owner's "which room do I go to" needs it. Companies have no per-day availability (04 has no table for it): every non-hidden company of the connected list takes part in every day; hide an item in the list to leave a company out.
60. Distribution ports: the DB row keeps `sessions-tab` and `my-meetings-tab` (m02), not 01's `view-matrix` / `view-live` / `sessions`. The matrix and "My schedule" are Agenda tabs (the host maps edge `from_port` -> `view_key`); the LIVE board is a standalone page (`/view/:ref/live`, link kind `view`, key `live`) because it is a TV screen, not a tab. No `rows:session` output port was added (nothing consumes it yet). The existing `my_meetings(project)` RPC (m06c) is untouched; the founder program page (step 8) can use the Agenda tab route or that RPC.
61. Distribution privacy (02-9): "My schedule" is a tab of a node whose visibility the PM chooses; anyone who may see the node (public: everyone with the link; platform: participants) can pick any company or mentor. The "log in as a founder" requirement and a per-company private link / QR of 02-9 are NOT built: the signed-in founder's own company is preselected (via `company_founders`), the others stay one tap away. A founder whose company is only a form-response item (no canonical company) is not recognised and uses the picker.
62. Distribution public routes live in the new `PublicWideShell` (1200 px) as static routes `view/:nodeRef/matrix|live`; `NodeViewPage` does not dispatch them. Public payloads include company names (and mentor photos) because the matrix is the point of the node; contact data and PM-only keys never leave the DB. The publish flag is enforced by the RPC (as-is CON-17 was UI-only): an unpublished node answers visitors `published: false`, the Agenda host hides its tabs' data.
63. Distribution dates: a day without a date (the 6 migrated ones) renders with its times and no date and is never "today": the live board needs a PM-chosen timer round for it and "My schedule" lists it in order without now/next. All "today" / "now" logic goes through `lib/time` with the project zone; a round that crosses midnight (the migrated 23:34 + 60 min day) shows its end as 12:34 AM.
64. Tooling: executing a PM-guarded SECURITY DEFINER function under `set local role anon` segfaults the local Postgres container (also seen in m33, it recovers by itself): v35 proves anon denial through the privilege catalog and the browser walk-through used PostgREST (`permission denied`). Windows excludes port 5174: the walk-through used `vite --port 5400`.

65. Landing: the 01 section 3.9 config proposal is trimmed to what the DB and the renderer honour. `nodes.config` keeps `nav`, `hero {title, body, coverPath}`, `cardsSection {enabled, title, body, layoutColumns}`, `personalize {enabled, title, body, labels}`, `share {channels}`, `seo`, `analytics {enabled}`; cards / presets / frames are rows (`save_node` children). NOT built: `theme {primary, accent, font}` (the platform tokens fix the palette and fonts, decision 16), `languages` / `defaultLang` (the site language switch decides), `hero.cta`, `personalize.panelPattern` (the dot pattern is fixed), `personalize.crop` / `output` (constants: 4:5, zoom 1-3.5, 1080 x 1350 PNG), `seeMore.enabled` (replaced by "shown only when the text overflows"; the per-card clamp lines stay), per-card `channels` (the channel list is page level), `frame.layer` (frames are always drawn above the photo; `composeToCanvas` supports `below` for a later switch).
66. Landing share texts are language-NEUTRAL: `landing_share_presets` has one `body` per (card, platform) and no language column (04 3.11), so the PM writes the text once (bilingual if wanted). The built-in default ("card title + link") IS built in the visitor's language. Telegram: `url` = the link and `text` = the message WITHOUT the link (the link stays in its own parameter; with the link also in the text Telegram would show it twice); a preset without `{link}` still carries the link on its own line (every channel). Facebook / LinkedIn: link-only intent + "copy the text first" (07-13). X is an optional channel (off by default, owner's list has six). On OUR form pages the shared link gets `?source=<platform>` so the form analytics count shares by channel; typed external URLs are never touched.
67. Landing own page: the link is kind `view` + view_key `landing` (`/view/<slug>/landing`, as the Distribution views), not kind `landing` (`/join/<ref>`): both resolve in `get_public_landing`, `/join/:nodeRef` is routed to the same page, `ensure_public_link` creates the `view` one from Settings. The legacy `/view/<event id>/join-athar` already redirects to the host tab `/agenda/<id>/landing` (the migrated landing node has the edge `landing-tab -> project page tabs`).
68. Landing frames: stored AS IS (PNG, lossless alpha) in the existing public-read `public-assets` bucket under `<project>/landing/<uuid>.png` (the project-folder write policy applies) instead of a new `landing-frames` bucket (07 6.1): no bucket / CORS change was needed (the page loads frames through `fetch` -> bitmap, with an `<img crossOrigin>` fallback, so the export canvas is never tainted). Validation is client side (PNG, <= 5 MB, 4:5, >= 1080 x 1350, a transparent window and visible artwork); the DB checks the project folder prefix and the count (8). The photo window found in the alpha channel is stored and drawn as a safe-zone in the editor; it is NOT used to clip the photo (07 6.2: the photo is cover-fitted to the whole canvas and the frame is drawn on top, as the owner's HTML pipeline).
69. Landing photo crop: pan is stored as a fraction of the viewport and clamped so the photo always covers the frame (the HTML let the photo be dragged off the frame, leaving white), pinch-zoom and keyboard control were added (HTML: mouse, touch drag, wheel, slider); the export is `canvas.toBlob('image/png')` (the HTML used `toDataURL`, whose quality argument is ignored for PNG) and the download name is `<page slug>-athar.png`. A "Share picture" button (Web Share API with the file) appears only where the browser can share files (the route to Instagram on phones).
70. Landing analytics: a NEW anon RPC `track_landing_event` (m37) instead of `track_node_event` (forms keep theirs): closed event / platform vocabulary, the card must belong to the node, the node's switch is honoured, project managers are not counted, 120 events / 10 min per (node, hashed caller) with the excess dropped silently, the caller is never stored (a one-way bucket hash in a private counter table), and as an Agenda tab the landing node id travels with the host's ref (the DB checks the edge). `tab_view` is sent once per page view and never while the payload is loading. A click that leaves the page in the same tab waits at most ~400 ms for the request.
71. Landing targets (decision 01-13): a card's button and share target are EITHER a typed URL (whitelisted: http(s), mailto, tel, /path, #anchor; `javascript:` / `data:` refused by the client AND `save_children_landing_page`) OR a node id bound through the `links` input (`cta_node_id` / `share_node_id`): the DB resolves the node's enabled public link at read time (`cta_link` / `share_link` = `{kind, slug, view_key}`, only for a non-private node), so nothing is copied and a changed address stays right. The share target defaults to the button target, then to the page itself. A bound node needs its public link created in ITS Settings first (the select lists only connected nodes whose link exists).
72. Landing layout: decided from the component's OWN width (`compact` < 768 px via ResizeObserver), not from media queries, so ONE renderer is right as a full page, as a tab of the 750 px agenda page (the cards become the phone carousel there) and in the editor's phone-sized preview. The page's own sticky nav is hidden in tab mode (the host's tab bar navigates). `prefers-reduced-motion` switches off the wiggle / pulse / smooth scroll.
73. Landing: a fresh node's config stays `{}` and the editor offers the HTML's copy as an explicit "Use the starter template" (never applied silently, so a migrated node is never padded with text its owners did not write). The migrated node keeps exactly what it has: hero title from the legacy `title`, hero cover, 4 cards (3 covers, "Join Now" with `#`), no share buttons (`share_enabled` false), personalize off.
74. Landing SEO: title / description / share image live in `config.seo` (not copied to `public_links.og_*`: no OG renderer exists yet, same as forms and the agenda); the page sets `document.title` and the meta tags in the browser (invisible to link-preview crawlers until the OG function ships, docs/04 A-37).
75. Shared CSS: `html, body { overflow-x: hidden }` made `body` a scroll container, so `position: sticky` NEVER worked in the app (the site header included); now `overflow-x: hidden; overflow-x: clip` (clip hides the overflow without a scroll container; older Safari keeps `hidden`). Also a gotcha for every builder: `cn()` = tailwind-merge does not know the custom font-size tokens (`text-hero`, `text-page-title`, `text-section-title`, `text-md`, `text-nav`...) and treats them as TEXT COLOURS: in one `cn(...)` call a size token and a `text-<colour>` class cancel each other (the later wins). Keep size tokens in a plain `className` string or add them to a tailwind-merge config (not done here).
76. Landing `save_children_landing_page` was re-created (the m06b body + validation): a card keeps its id (upsert by id, removed cards deleted by id, `node_events.card_id` goes NULL for a removed card), URLs / cover / frame paths / platform vocabulary / text lengths / counts (24 cards, 8 frames) are checked and refused with `22023` + hint `landing_invalid` (`detail = <field>:<reason>`, message in `landing.err.invalid`). `get_public_tab` is patched IN PLACE (only its landing branch, now `app_private.landing_public_payload`), the same technique as m36, so a re-run of either migration keeps the other's branch.

Library (deviations, numbered L1.. to stay independent of the parallel Landing builder's numbers):
- L1. The 01 section 3.8 config proposal is trimmed to what the DB and the viewer honour: the resources and sections are rows (`resources`, `library_categories`, saved as `children`), `nodes.config` keeps `layout` (grid|list), `groupBy` (auto|category|type|none) and `searchEnabled`; `includeCentralPool` is NOT built (L2).
- L2. The central pool (`resources.node_id is null`, `library_selections`, the `/library` platform page, still `ComingSoon`) is not built: no node in the migrated data references it (all 26 resources are node resources) and the page belongs with the Form library admin page. The read side is ready (the payload and the file policy already include a node's `library_selections` references; nothing writes them yet).
- L3. Per-resource visibility (`public | participants | hidden`) is NEW (default `public`: the migrated rows stay visible). It combines with the NODE visibility: private serves nobody, platform serves participants only, public serves everybody the `public` resources and participants also the `participants` ones; `hidden` is PM-only everywhere. `show_to_participants` is the founder Program page exposure only (like Agenda/Booking: `list_founder_libraries`); it does not gate the data, because the host tab of a platform node is already visible to participants through `tab_visible`.
- L4. Draft + ONE Save (like Agenda) instead of row-level saves: the removal Undo, the order, the sections and the visibility are one coherent edit that must bump the revision once (the brief's expected_rev). Files are uploaded immediately (they cannot live in a draft); the editor tracks the files uploaded in the session and removes them again when they are never saved (after Save: the unreferenced ones; leaving the editor: all unsaved ones) and removes the OLD file of a replaced file after the save. A removed resource keeps its row (soft delete) and its file (restorable, purged later): removing a resource and saving is therefore not a storage cleanup.
- L5. Files are NEVER public. The `resources` bucket stays private; visitors get 300 s signed URLs minted at click time. Because Storage runs the SELECT policy for signing, the same policy also lets an allowed caller read the object through `/object/authenticated/...` with their own key: it is exactly the set of files the caller may see, so nothing leaks beyond the payload, but a permanent "permalink" to a PUBLIC file does exist for anyone holding the anon key (the signed URL only bounds shared links). If the owner wants links to expire for public files too, the files would have to be served through an edge function.
- L6. Uploaded images: the file in the private bucket is the WebP (full 1600 px) with a `_t` thumbnail NEXT to it (the policy accepts the thumbnail of an image resource only); an optional COVER image is a separate public image in `public-assets` (covers are public by definition: the editor says so). The card shows the cover, else the signed thumbnail of an image file, else a type icon.
- L7. Types stay the four of the as-is table (`readable` = documents, `audible`, `visual` = video/images, `link`); the editor suggests one from the file or the link host and the PM may change it. Office documents (docx/xlsx/pptx) were added to the bucket's allowed types (the brief's "docs").
- L8. **m31 defect repaired in m36**: `form_uploads_insert_public` called `app_private.form_upload_allowed` after its EXECUTE was revoked from anon/authenticated; Postgres checks EXECUTE for every function of an RLS expression when the statement starts, so EVERY `storage.objects` insert by an authenticated user failed (`permission denied for function`; on this local image that error path segfaults the server, which is also what the crashes of deviation 64 were: any permission error reaching the top level). The policy now calls the public wrapper `public.form_upload_allowed`. This unblocks program/project covers, list photos, the Agenda cover, Landing images and (for the first time) anonymous form file uploads. v31 stays green; the Form Builder step should re-check its anonymous upload end to end.
- L9. `get_public_tab` is patched IN PLACE by m36 (only the library branch), not re-created, because the parallel Landing builder also needs that function: whoever re-creates it later must keep `v := app_private.library_payload(s, app_private.library_viewer(s.id, l.audience))` in the library branch (m36's self-check and v36 `C05` fail if it is lost). The payload audience is the CALLER's standing (`library_viewer`), not only the link's: `resolve_link` answers `public` for every caller of a public node.
- L10. Not built: resource analytics (downloads), per-section ordering views in Edit (the editor shows ONE ordered list with a section chip, visitors see it grouped), drag between sections, a lightbox for image resources (they open the signed file in a new tab), the `rows:resource` output port (01 section 3.8 "future Landing / Program page block": nothing consumes it).

Certificates (deviations, numbered C1.. to stay independent of the other builders' numbers):
- C1. The 01 section 3.10 / 07 section 4.3 config proposal is trimmed to what the DB honours: the template is rows (`certificate_templates` + `certificate_template_fields`, saved as `children.template`), `nodes.config` keeps `language` and `verifier { enabled, showCertificate }`. Not built: `pageSize/orientation` (taken from the image), `signature` (baked into the template image), `emailOnIssue` (07 section 1: off by default, no e-mail exists), `issueMode` (01-11: one explicit "Issue certificates" action), a separate `code` / `qr` object (they are the variables `verification_code` / `verification_qr`).
- C2. Template files: PNG / JPEG only. PDF templates are NOT supported (07 section 4.3 proposed pdf.js): converting a PDF page to an image needs pdf.js (~1 MB + a worker) which is not cheap; the editor tells the PM to export the design as an image (A4 at 150-300 dpi is 1754 x 1240 .. 3508 x 2480 px). The file is stored AS UPLOADED (no re-encoding: print quality), under `<project>/<node>/<uuid>.<ext>` (not `v<version>.<ext>`, the version is decided by the server at save time).
- C3. Versioning: a template that issued certificates reference is immutable (m06a trigger), so a change creates the NEXT version and the old file + fields stay; a template nobody references is updated in place; an unchanged save creates nothing. The Issue panel and the founder download always use the version a certificate was issued with (`certificates.template_id`); a NEW certificate uses the newest version of the node language.
- C4. Names: the 04 snapshot has `participant_name` + `company_name`. `participant_name` is the PERSON when known (the form response's founder name, else the account's profile name, else the company's primary founder) and the company name only as the last fallback; the as-is generator wanted the founder name. This differs from the m06d helper (company first), which was replaced. The Issue panel shows the printed name per row ("Name on the certificate"). A participant with no name at all gets no certificate (`no_name`).
- C5. Re-issuing: `unique (node_id, participant_id)` became a partial unique index on ACTIVE rows, so a revoked certificate can be re-issued as a NEW certificate (new code) while the revoked one stays as history. "Issue to all attended" never re-issues revoked ones (07-9: revocation is deliberate); the PM selects the participant to re-issue. Revocation is manual only; the panel flags an issued certificate whose participant is no longer `attended` and a re-opened project (decision 07-9, 01-11 "reopening is allowed").
- C6. Founder access: the m07 SELECT policy on `public.certificates` (own, attended, not revoked) is kept because the stage-1 verification relies on it; the stricter gating (project completed + the node's "Show to participants") lives in `list_my_certificates`, the render payload and the storage rule for the template image. A founder who queries the table directly can read the code + snapshot of their own certificate before the PM exposes it, but cannot sign the template image.
- C7. Public verifier: anon gets the template image through a signed URL for a VALID certificate when the node shows it (`cert_template_readable`); the object path is only ever returned inside that payload (project / node / uuid, not guessable) and the payload carries only the snapshot values the template prints (a company name is exposed only when the PM placed it). Turn "Show the certificate itself" off and the page shows facts only and the file closes. The verifier stays PUBLIC by design (07 4.5); rate limit 40 lookups / 10 min per hashed caller, no IP stored. No CAPTCHA (same open question as forms and booking, 03 OD-5).
- C8. `verify_certificate(code)` (m06d name) is kept as an alias of `get_public_certificate_verification` so the exhaustive anon grant list of m07 stays true; the route is `/verify/:code` (06 section 2) with the legacy redirect.
- C9. The `participants` input of the node is optional (it was required): nothing emits `rows:participant` yet, so a required input would have shown the node as "incomplete" on the canvas forever; the node reads the project's participants through the Issue panel RPC (the Selection handover's suggestion). No out ports (`rows:certificate`, `view`) were added: nothing consumes them; the founder block is `FounderCertificateButton`. The node's `visibility` is not used (a certificate is not a page): "Show to participants" is the only exposure switch.
- C10. Fonts: only the families `index.html` already loads (Tajawal 400-800 for Arabic + Latin, Manrope 400-700, Hepta Slab 800, Questrial 400); every Latin font falls back to Tajawal for Arabic letters, so a Latin-only font never produces tofu for an Arabic name. A bundled calligraphic Arabic font (e.g. Amiri) would be one more `<link>` family + one entry in `layout.FONTS` if the owner wants it.
- C11. Rendering limits: the canvas is capped to 4200 px on the long side for downloads (a 3508 x 2480 A4 template is native); previews draw at the displayed width x dpr. The PDF is the canvas picture (not vector, not selectable text) because the browser is the only place Arabic is shaped (06-5); `CompressionStream` exists in every current browser, an older one gets a JPEG-in-PDF.


Founder platform (deviations, numbered F1.. to stay independent of the other builders' numbers):
- F1. The block set and ORDER follow the owner's description (docs/13 section 11), not the table of 06 section 10.2: description, agenda, mentors, companies, details (location), library, landing, My schedule, certificate. 06's seven blocks gained `landing` and `schedule` (the owner's list); the certificate is last, as in 06.
