The server decides the order (a fixed order per type, nodes of one type by creation time), the client only renders it.
- F2. `mentors` / `companies` are LIST nodes by `subkind` (`expert` / `company`) that the PM exposed with "Show on the participants' Program page" AND platform / public visibility AND that hold at least one visible item (an empty list is not a block). They do not have to be connected to a tab or the host: the exposure toggle is the one switch. A `generic` list is not a block, and the accepted applicants of a Selection stage are not either (the "companies attending" are a companies list the PM curates).
- F3. Two owner decisions say different things about a `withdrawn` participant: 02-7 "hidden from the founder's Programs list" and 03-6 "sees the program card with status only". Both are honoured: the list HIDES withdrawn participations (a founder who only has withdrawn ones sees the empty state), a small collapsed "Withdrawn programs (n)" below the list shows a STATUS-ONLY card (names + red chip + a note, no cover, dates, location or link), and the Program page of a withdrawn participant is the header + a status card with NO blocks (the DB sends none). Dropping the collapsed section is one deleted component block if the owner wants the stricter reading.
- F4. **Linking a founder account to a participant created from a form response (the missing piece of "accepted through the selection process")**: `participants` from Selection carry `company_id` or `form_response_id`, and `is_participant` / the `list_founder_*` / certificate predicates match `user_id` or one of the caller's companies, so a founder accepted through a form had no way to see the program (the Participants page itself said "linking is done from the Alumni area", which is not built). 02-8 decides "link by e-mail": `link_my_participations()` (m39) sets `participants.user_id` for the caller's founder account when the e-mail of her account is VERIFIED and equals the `founder_email` of the response (once per project, idempotent, never a staff account, never unverified). It runs at the start of `list_my_programs` and `get_founder_program_page`, so a deep link to an agenda / library page BEFORE the first Programs visit still answers `forbidden` (the founder lands on the Programs list first). It relies on Supabase Auth requiring e-mail confirmation: with auto-confirm ON anyone could register an address and see that program (open question).
- F5. `FounderAgendaButtons`, `FounderLibraryButtons`, `FounderLandingButtons` and `FounderCertificateButton` got an optional `items` prop (rows from `get_founder_program_page`, same shape as their own RPCs) that skips their own request; used standalone they behave as before. `FounderCertificateButton` also takes `showHeading` (the section already carries the title).
- F6. The Library and Landing buttons open the node's PUBLIC pages (`/view/<ref>/library-tab`, `/view/<ref>/landing`, the audience decided by the DB, participants see platform pages) in the public shell, not inside the founder shell: the founder leaves the bottom tab bar for those two pages (the agenda stays inside the founder shell). Re-hosting them in the founder shell is a routing change only. The 06 placeholder `/alumni/founder/programs/:projectId/library` redirects to the Program page.
- F7. Compact mode of `EntityGrid` / `EntityCard` (`dense`, a prop nothing used): a row with a round avatar (photo or fallback glyph) or a square logo box beside the text, bio clamped to two lines, one column on a phone and two from `sm`; the 3:1 picture band it used to mean wasted 110 px per mentor without a photo.
- F8. Icons: the owner's "brand set" (`ui/icons/brand`) has seven glyphs and none for a place or a bus, so the Location block uses the functional lucide icons (`MapPin`, `CalendarDays`, `Bus`, `Footprints`) and the people sections use the brand `UsersIcon`.
- F9. `super_admin` keeps `area:founder` (06 section 8.2: "super_admin implies all"): a super_admin opening the founder area is not bounced, but she is not a participant, so every Program page is "not available" and her list is empty. A project manager / alumni manager is bounced (tested). The staff-never-founder rule is the DB's (m25).
- F10. Founder Home is lean on purpose (greeting + active programs). The bottom tab bar still points at `/alumni/founder/notifications` and `/alumni/founder/profile`, which are `ComingSoon` (step 10 with `opportunities`, `progress`, `company/:id`, the admin pages, `/signup` and `/invite/:token`).
- F11. There is NO block for Booking nodes (the owner's list of section 11 has none; the m06c stub listed every exposed node by type, bookings included). A "Book a mentor session" block would be one more branch in `get_founder_program_page` + one registry entry (the Booking handover describes the link).
- F12. The dates of a program (cards and header) come from the days of the agenda nodes the PM exposed, in the project zone (`founder_project_dates`); a program without an exposed agenda has no dates (nothing leaks from a hidden agenda). The list order is "active first, newest first by that date, else by creation".
- F13. `get_my_programs()` (m06c) stays in the DB and is unused (superseded by `list_my_programs`); stage-1 `v11` FO04 describes the old stub (see the m39 log entry).

- W1. **Where the fact lives (decision 17.1: no duplicated truth)**: identity items (form-response applicant, canonical company) are withdrawn when the PROJECT-level `participants` row says `withdrawn`; every other item carries its own `list_items.withdrawn_*`. One place per kind, never both, and `app_private.is_withdrawn` is the only reader. A withdrawal on a Selection stage therefore also withdraws the person on the Participants page, in every other stage, list, Distribution, Booking and the founder page (the owner's "leaves the program" in one click), and an applicant who was never enrolled gets a participant row only to record it (`participant_withdrawals.restore_status = 'not_enrolled'`: reinstating deletes it, so "eligible, not enrolled yet" returns exactly as it was).
- W2. **Withdrawn is not a Selection stage**: the applicant keeps the stage he had (`accepted` stays `accepted`) and shows a red chip; the board counts him apart ("Withdrawn (n)" pill) and every output port, e-mail recipient list and later stage drops him. A stage type "withdrawn" would have cut the applicant off from the stage that decided him and would not have been shared with the other lists. The Selection stage type `hold` ("On hold") is a workflow stage, not a Hide, and is unchanged.
- W3. **Reason and restore status are PM-only**: kept in `participant_withdrawals` (a founder can read his own `participants` row through the API, so the reason does not live there). Nobody is told: no e-mail, no notification (no automation).
- W4. **No automatic clean-up**: bookings, invitations and distribution sessions that already exist for a withdrawn person are NOT cancelled or deleted. The PM sees them flagged (Bookings tab, invitations, Timetable / Matrix chips, validation warnings); public / platform outputs hide them; the live board announces nobody withdrawn; the RPCs refuse NEW invitations, sessions and bookings for them.
- W5. **Default filters**: the Selection board shows "Active only" by default (its stage pills are counts of people still in the program) with the "Withdrawn (n)" pill beside them; the Lists manager shows everyone by default so the PM sees who is out, with the chip (the filter narrows to Active / Withdrawn / Hidden (legacy)).
- W6. **The old Hide is removed, not renamed**: a separate "Hidden" for pure display-hiding of one list item was NOT kept (the owner asked for Withdrawn instead of Hide; a PM who only wants an item out of a public list can remove it from the list or detach it). The 2 migrated legacy `excluded` experts stay hidden from every consumer as before and show "Hidden (legacy)" with the two exits; `exclude_item` remains only to restore them and nothing in the UI creates the flag.
- W7. The Participants page status select no longer offers "Withdrawn" (it is the explicit action next to it); `set_participant_status('withdrawn')` still works and goes through the same path (`withdraw_participant`).
- W8. The registry views (`v_company_programs`, `v_network_expert_programs`, `v_program_participations`) stay HISTORY: a withdrawn participation is still a participation, listed with its status.

## Open questions for the owner

- Staff invitation expiry is 14 days (same as alumni invitations). Confirm.
- `remove_staff` keeps the auth user and profile (re-invitable). With 08 §15 the removed account ends with no roles (closed).
- Where the staff e-mail sender identity comes from when the outbox worker ships (`startups@athareg.com` system connection per 07-3?).
- OD-1: React Flow attribution kept during the test phase (08 section 14); revisit before production.
- Program `visibility` and project `visibility` are stored; the public program/project pages that honour them are step 9.
- Canvas: should the host node be pinned (not draggable) as well as locked? Should an edge also carry a per-edge label (`label_en/ar`, 02 4.2) editable in the inspector now, or in the Agenda node's Tabs settings (step 7)? Realtime on `node_revisions` for PMs (02 5.3) vs the current focus + 30 s poll.
- Lists: should a list-level `display_override` (02-2: shorten a company name for one list) get UI now, or stay off? Should `notes` ever be public (02 section 3.4 "show notes" default off)? Today the public payload never carries overlay keys, so enabling it needs a DB change.
- Lists: the resolver maps form answers with fixed keys (`company_name`, `founder_name`, `logo`, `bio`, `founder_email`, `phone`); the Form Builder node should expose these as its canonical/semantic keys (01 section 3.2) so a form -> list connection shows the right title without a mapping UI.
- Lists: the status vocabulary is per list and free text in the DB for `list` nodes (only Selection validates stages server-side). Fine for v1?
- Lists: `delete_list_item` is a hard delete of the list ROW (manual items lose their fields). Acceptable, or should manual items be soft-deleted too ("nothing lost")?
- Forms: CAPTCHA provider for public forms (03 OD-5)? Today: honeypot + per-ip rate limit + server validation only. Should the rate limit (20 submissions / 10 min per form and ip) be different?
- Forms: should anonymous respondents be allowed to upload files at all before the edge-function flow exists (deviation 27), or should `file` questions stay PM-only/platform-only until then?
- Forms: the 5 migrated "Legacy import" forms (382 responses) keep their legacy keys (`q001`...); their `company_name` role is set, but `founder_name/founder_email/phone` are assigned on only 2 of them. Assigning the roles in the builder re-keys the answers (m31) - do you want that done now for all legacy forms (a PM action per form), so Selection/Lists get names and e-mails everywhere?
- Forms: the link preview (`config.share`) is not rendered anywhere (no OG function); keep it in config or move it to `public_links.og_*` once a share/OG function exists (docs/04 A-37)?
- Forms: a confirmation e-mail to the respondent (01 OD-12) is not built; the outbox pattern makes it a small addition once the owner wants it.
- Agenda: two hosts exist (the project page and each Agenda node's own page, deviation 35). Should new projects default to ONE of them (e.g. the project page link created automatically with the project), or is "create the link you want from the Agenda Settings" fine?
- Agenda: a tab label override is per language (`label_en`/`label_ar`); when only the English override is set, the Arabic page shows the node's Arabic name (DB `coalesce(label_ar, name_ar)`). Should an English-only override also replace the Arabic label?
- Agenda: the migrated `backgroundPath` (page background image, 1 project) and the legacy header font keys are kept but not rendered. Drop them at sign-off?
- Agenda: `presenter_item_id` (a session's presenter picked from an expert list, 04 §3.11) has no UI yet (free text only); the public payload already resolves it. Wanted in v1?
- Agenda: should the public page offer an "Add to calendar" (.ics) per day/session? Not in 01; cheap to add client-side from the `schedule` shape.
- Selection: the migrated stages have `config = {}` (or only `emails`/`sheetImport`), so they run on the default vocabulary and the default templates; saving Settings once writes the explicit `stages[]`/`passing` (no data change). Should the 8 migrated nodes be saved like that now, or left as-is until a PM opens them?
- Selection: the 48 migrated `accepted` applicants become participants only when a PM turns on the toggle on the (last) stage of each project. Do you want that done for the completed projects so Certificates can see them, and should "attended" be bulk-set for completed projects?
- Selection: the interview invitation needs a Booking node of mode `interview` connected to the stage; today only 2 migrated projects have one. Should sending be allowed without a booking link (template without `{{booking_link}}`) - it is - or should the stage warn when the Interview stage has no Booking node?
- Selection: `cc` and `admin` lists are both sent as CC (as-is). Should admin copies become BCC (needs an outbox column)?
- Selection: no per-applicant "resend from the board" (resend lives in the E-mails tab with the Resend toggle). Fine for v1?
- Participants: a manual participant can only be a company today (no founder-account picker). Needed before the founder platform ships?
- Booking: should the 28 migrated bookings with a stored `legacy_calendar_event_id` get a `calendar_owner_connection_id` pointing at the `startups@` system connection once OAuth exists (07 section 2.3 step 4), so a later cancel/reschedule reaches the right calendar? Today they show "Synced (migrated)" and a PM cancel queues a `calendar_cancel` job with no connection.
- Booking: unique-Meet mode creates the link only with the calendar worker (not built); until then a booker sees "the meeting link will follow by e-mail". Is a manual "paste the Meet link" per booking (PM edits `bookings.meet_link`) wanted as a stop-gap, or do PMs use shared mode until OAuth ships?
- Booking: a booker may book several different slots in mentor mode unless `max bookings per booker` is set (default empty = no limit, as the as-is page). Should the default for new mentor nodes be 1?
- Booking: the public page asks for name, e-mail and company only (no phone, no topic/notes). The as-is page asked the same; add a free-text "what do you want to discuss" field (stored in a new column) for mentor sessions?
- Booking: the interview invitation link is valid 30 days and one booking per invitation (decision 01-15); a rescheduling request after booking goes through the PM today (the public page offers cancel only). Should the invited company be able to reschedule itself from the same link?
- Booking: a booker who cancels frees the slot immediately; for interview mode the invitation stays valid, so the company can book again with the same link. Intended?
- Booking: CAPTCHA/rate limit for the anonymous `create_booking` (same open question as forms, 03 OD-5): today the protection is the slot lock, the per-e-mail limit and the notice window only.
- Distribution: is the split "Generate = from scratch, Regenerate unlocked = keep my manual edits" (deviation 57) what you want, or should the two be merged into one button?
- Distribution: timer latency. Screens poll every 5 s, so a PM action appears within about 5 s. Acceptable for a hall, or do you want Realtime (a "refresh now" ping over a private channel) for near-instant sync? It needs the Realtime Authorization check of 03 section 9.4 on the live project first.
- Distribution: should a founder be forced to sign in to see "My schedule" (02-9 as written), or is "visible to whoever may see the node, own company preselected" (deviation 61) fine? A per-company private link / QR is not built.
- Distribution: companies that attend only some days (no table exists, deviation 59), and a real clash check for a mentor who is in BOTH a Booking node and a Distribution node (today only a note on the Setup tab, 01-7 "warn, later").
- Distribution: room / table is one free-text label per mentor (not per day). Should it be per day, or a numbered table list?
- Distribution: the 2 migrated nodes are published and public with links on `/view/<slug>/matrix|live`; nothing was changed on them. The 6 dateless days need a date (or an Agenda day) before the live board can follow the clock for them.
- Local stack: a PM-guarded SECURITY DEFINER RPC called under `set local role anon` segfaults the local Postgres 17.6 container (pre-existing, see `db/MIGRATION-LOG.md` m33). It does not affect the app (PostgREST never executes those for anon), but it is worth checking on the live project before relying on anon role-switch tests.

- Library: should the central pool (platform-wide resources a node can reference without copying) get its admin page now (`/library`, super_admin writes, PMs pick), or stay deferred until a second program needs shared material?
- Library: a participant of a PUBLIC library sees the `participants` resources only when signed in and enrolled; anonymous visitors of the same link do not see them at all (no "sign in to see more" hint). Add the hint?
- Library: removed resources keep their files (soft delete; the 90-day purge job of 04 section 2 is not built). Is that retention right, or should removing a resource also delete its file after a grace period?
- Library: public files can be fetched with the anon key through the Storage API as long as the node and the resource are public (L5). Acceptable, or should public downloads go through an expiring link only (needs an edge function)?
- Library: a per-resource "view only, no download" and a download counter - wanted?
- Landing: share texts are one text per platform per card (no language column). Do you want one per language (a DB column + a second text per channel), or is "write it once, bilingual if you like" enough?
- Landing: Telegram gets the link in its own parameter and the text WITHOUT the link (deviation 66). Prefer the text with the link inside (the link then shows twice in Telegram)?
- Landing: link previews (title / description / image when the page is pasted into WhatsApp / Facebook) need server-side OG rendering (A-37); today only the browser sets the meta tags. Build the small OG function / rewrite rule now, so shared cards look good?
- Landing: a project manager's visits are not counted as page views (the preview is not a visit). Confirm. Analytics are ON by default for new pages and anonymous (no IP, no device); the rate limit is 120 events / 10 min per page and visitor.
- Landing: the frame's photo window is only drawn in the editor (the photo fills the whole canvas, the frame sits on top, as in the HTML pipeline). Do you want the photo CLIPPED to the window (e.g. a circular window, a transparent background outside it)? The window + shape are already stored.
- Landing: the migrated "Join Athar" node keeps its 4 cards (3 test cards with covers and "Join Now" buttons pointing at `#`, no share buttons) and no personalize section; I changed nothing. Should it be turned into the real two-card page (starter template) before the legacy link is retired?
- Landing: a card's button or share target bound to a node needs that node's public link (created in ITS Settings). Should the Landing editor offer to create the link for a connected node in one click?
- Landing: which language should a first-time visitor see? The page follows the site language switch (`?lang=`, stored choice, Arabic by default for public pages). A per-page default language (the HTML was English only) is not stored.
- Forms: the m31 upload policy repair (L8) means the Form Builder's anonymous file upload had never worked end to end; please re-test it with a real form that has a file question.

- Certificates: the name printed is the PERSON (founder name) with the company name as the fallback (C4). For a company-level participant with several founders only the primary founder is named. Should the certificate name the COMPANY instead, or one certificate per founder of the company?
- Certificates: the 48 migrated accepted applicants are not participants yet and no migrated project is completed, so nothing can be issued on real data until a PM enrols / marks attendance and completes a project. Should `attended` + completion be bulk-set for the finished projects (Selection open question)?
- Certificates: PDF templates (pdf.js, ~1 MB lazy chunk) were left out (C2). Needed, or is "export your design as a PNG" fine?
- Certificates: a public verification page that also DRAWS the certificate (default on) shows the printed values to anyone with the code. Keep it on by default, or facts only?
- Certificates: "certificate ready" e-mail (07 section 1, off by default) is not built; the outbox pattern makes it a small addition (`{{certificate_link}}` = the founder's Program page).
- Certificates: should issuing also be possible per participant straight from the Participants page ("Issue certificate" button), and should the founder be told (notification) when a certificate is issued?
- Certificates: a revoked certificate can be re-issued as a new one (new code). Intended, or should a revoked participant stay revoked unless a super_admin restores it?
- Certificates: the founder can read the row of their own issued certificate through the table before the node is shown to participants (C6). Tighten the m07 policy (needs the stage-1 verification updated)?

Founder platform, open questions for the owner:
- Linking by e-mail (F4): is "the verified e-mail of the account equals the founder e-mail of the application" the intended link, and is e-mail confirmation ON in the live Auth settings? If not, linking would have to be invitation-only (an invitation row carrying the participant, accepted by the account).
- Withdrawn (F3): keep the collapsed "Withdrawn programs" section, or hide withdrawn participations completely?
- A Booking block (F11) for founders ("Book a mentor session", opening the booking page of an exposed Booking node)?
- Should the Library / Landing pages open inside the founder shell (F6), keeping the bottom tab bar?
- Should a super_admin be able to preview the founder platform as a given founder ("view as")? Today she cannot (F9).
- The founder side has no "request withdrawal" action yet (`request_withdrawal` exists in the DB and the PM page flags it): add a button on the Program page?
- Co-founders (03-13): every founder linked to a company sees the company's participations; a form-response participant is linked to ONE account (the first to open the platform with that e-mail). Is that enough, or should every address in the application be matched?

Withdrawn, open questions for the owner:
- Scope (W1): the withdrawal is per PROJECT (participants, Selection and every consumer are project-scoped, decision 02-7). The owner's words say "a whole program". If one program groups several projects (editions / cohorts) and a company that leaves must leave ALL of them at once, say so: it is one more derivation in `is_withdrawn` (the participant rows of the sibling projects) and a PM confirmation listing them, not a new fact.
- Should the founder be told (an e-mail or a notification) when the PM marks him withdrawn or reinstates him? Today nothing is sent (W3).
- Should existing bookings / invitations / distribution sessions of a withdrawn person be cancelled for the PM in one click ("Withdraw and cancel his meetings")? Today they stay and are only flagged (W4).
- Is a separate display-only "Hidden" for a list item still wanted (W6)? It was removed on purpose.
- Phones: the Participants page is a table (desktop-first PM editor) whose action column sits behind a horizontal scroll at 375 px; a card layout would put "Mark as withdrawn" in reach. Not done (the PM editors are desktop-first).

## Next builders: the shared pieces to reuse (Lists handover)

- **Tab view contract** (Agenda `tabs` input, project host page, founder program page): a node's `manifest.contributesViews[i].component` is a lazy default export rendered by the HOST with the DB payload, never fetching itself. For lists: `const ListTab = lazy(() => import('../../nodes/list/ListTab.jsx'))` and `<ListTab data={payload.data} title={pick(payload,'title')} audience={payload.viewer} onLoadMore={fn} hasMore={lastPageWasFull} loadingMore={bool} />` where `payload = get_public_tab(ref, edgeId)` (inside the Agenda) or `get_public_list(ref)` (own page); `payload.data = { items, next, config: { kind, card } }`. `audience` is `'public' | 'participant' | 'pm'` (= `resolve_link.audience`): `missing` items are shown to `pm` only. Pagination: call the RPC again with `p_after = data.next` and append; pass `hasMore` = the last page had `PUBLIC_PAGE_SIZE` (100) rows (the DB always returns a cursor). The PM preview passes `items` (`ListItemView[]`) + `config` + `kind` instead of `data`. The same component is used by the node's View mode, so "preview = what the visitor sees" holds by construction.
- **Consuming list items** (Distribution mentors/companies, Booking providers, another List, Selection): `useInputPort('<port>')` returns `items` = `ListItemView[]` from `nodes/list/data.js resolveItems` (resolver registered on the `items` output port). Items may be VIRTUAL (`itemId === null`, `id = 'virtual:<kind>:<source id>'`); anything that must store a reference calls `materializeItem(listId, item)` from `data/lists.js` (= `ensure_item`) first and stores the returned uuid. Treat `item.missing` as inactive (02 section 3.6). Never read `list_items` directly.
- **Entity rendering/editing anywhere** (Booking provider cards, Distribution matrix names, Selection applicant cards, Athar Network page, founder "mentors present"): `shared/entities/EntityCard` + `EntityGrid` with an `EntityView` (`lib/entities.js` normalisers: `normalizeExpert`, `normalizeCompany`, `normalizeDisplay`, `normalizeListItem`); `EntityForm` for bilingual create/edit; `EntityPicker` for "add by reference" dialogs; `EntityManager` + `ItemEditor` for any curated list (Selection can pass its stages as `config.statuses`). `visibleCardFields(listShowFields, item.card)` is the ONE card-visibility rule. Labels live in the `entity.*` namespace (`shared/entities/i18n.js`, registered on import).
- **People registry**: `data/people.js` is the only module touching `network_experts`/`companies`; add functions there (e.g. `network_expert_usage`, `merge_companies`) rather than new queries in pages.
- **Public pages**: `app/pages/NodeViewPage.jsx` dispatches `/view/:nodeRef/:viewKey`; add a branch per new view key (Distribution `live`/`matrix`). `data/lists.getViewLink/ensureViewLink` show how a node creates its `view` link lazily and how Settings offers copy/open.
- **Kit**: `NodeFrame` props `showSave`, `actions`, `subtitle`, `bodyClassName`; `useNodeConfig().save(extraPatch, { children })` sends node columns (`name_en/ar`, `visibility`, `show_to_participants`) AND the node's typed children (questions/sections, days/slots, cards) in the same `save_node` call as the config - the Agenda builder should pass `{ days: [...] }` the same way (the DB writer is `app_private.save_children_agenda`). Pass `{ config }` in `extraPatch` when the screen wants to write a normalised config shape back.

## Next builders: Form Builder handover (for Agenda and Selection)

- **Consuming form responses** (Selection `applicants`, a List preview, Distribution): `useInputPort('<port>')` on an edge from `form_builder.responses` returns `items = [{ id, kind: 'form_response', source{kind,id,state}, answers, status, submittedAt, sourceTag, display{title,subtitle,email,phone,logo,bio} }]` plus (through the resolver result) `schema = { form_id, fields: [{ name, type, label{en,ar}, canonical }] }` (02 section 2.3). `display` is derived from the semantic roles (`responseDisplay` in `nodes/form-builder/formLogic.js`); `canonical` = the role. Selection should store `source: { kind: 'form_response', id }` references and call `materializeItem(listId, item)` for overlay rows exactly like Lists; never copy answers. The DB resolver (`resolve_list_rows`) already reads `answers->>'company_name'`/`founder_name`/`logo`/`bio`/`founder_email`/`phone`: a form connected to a List or Selection shows the right title/subtitle/contact as soon as the PM assigns the roles (no mapping UI needed).
- **Semantic roles are the contract**: a question with a role uses the role as its key. `SEMANTICS`, `SEMANTIC_TYPES`, `suggestSemantic`, `applySemantic` live in `formLogic.js`; the labels are `form.semantic.*`. If Selection e-mails need `{{founder_name}}`/`{{company_name}}` they read `answers[role]` directly.
- **Agenda "Apply" tab** (01 section 3.2 `view` port, optional): not built. If the Agenda builder wants an embedded form tab, add a `view` out port to the `form_builder` manifest AND to `node_types.ports` (m02 row) and render `FormRenderer` with the `get_public_form` payload inside the tab (`publicUrl` for sharing, `onSubmit` -> `submitPublicForm`). The public form already has its own page, so this is optional.
- **Public form link** (Landing page CTA): resolver on the `link` port returns `[{ kind: 'form', url, label{en,ar}, nodeId }]` once the PM created the link in Settings (`data/forms.ensureFormLink`); `withSource(url, 'landing')` adds the source tag.
- **Respondent UI pieces to reuse**: `ui/PhoneField` (country picker + E.164 validation through `lib/contact.validatePhone`), `FormRenderer` (any public form rendering), `formLogic.validateAnswers/cleanAnswers` (same rules as the server's `form_check_answers`). Error tokens of public RPCs follow the m31 pattern (`hint = token`, `detail = key:reason`) and are mapped in `data/errors.js` (`error.token`, `error.detail`).
- **Project timezone inside a node**: `graph.project.timezone` is available from `useNodeContext()` (get_project_graph carries the project); use `resolveTimeZone` + `toInstant/dateInTz/timeInTz` for `datetime-local` fields as `Settings.jsx` does.

## Next builders: Agenda handover (for Selection, Booking, Distribution, Landing, Library and the founder page)

- **Contribute a tab to the view page** (Selection `results-tab`, Distribution `sessions-tab`/`my-meetings-tab`, Landing `landing-tab`, Library `library-tab`, Booking `booking-tab`): (1) keep/declare the `view` OUT port in your manifest AND in `node_types.ports` (m02); (2) set `manifest.contributesViews = [{ key: '<port key>', title, component: () => import('./YourTab.jsx') }]` - the key must equal the out-port key because the host maps `edge.from_port` -> `view_key`; (3) the component is a lazy default export that NEVER fetches: the host (`app/pages/agenda/AgendaHost.jsx` -> `tabViews.js`) renders `<YourTab data={payload.data} title audience timezone project onLoadMore? hasMore loadingMore hideTitle />` with `payload = get_public_tab(ref, edgeId)`; (4) `get_public_tab` already has a branch per type (library/landing/distribution return whitelisted rows, m06d); extend it in a new migration if your tab needs more, and keep `data.items + data.next` if you want the host's generic "Load more"; (5) nothing else: the PM connects your port to an Agenda's `tabs` or to the project page's `tabs` on the canvas, and the Agenda Settings "Tabs" editor handles label/order/enabled/audience. Audience/visibility are enforced in the DB (`app_private.tab_visible`): a `private` node never appears to visitors even when connected. While your `contributesViews` component is still the placeholder, the host shows "not available yet" for the tab (no crash).
- **Consume `schedule`** (Booking day dates, Distribution days with `agenda_day_id`): `useInputPort('schedule')` on an edge from `agenda.schedule` returns `items = [{ id (= agenda_days.id), kind: 'agenda_day', date: 'YYYY-MM-DD'|null, name{en,ar}, sortOrder, timezone, start, end ('HH:MM' or null), startsAt, endsAt (ISO), slots: [{ id, start, end, startMinutes, endMinutes, startsAt, endsAt, title{en,ar} }] }]`. Store `id` as the reference (`distribution_days.agenda_day_id` FK, SET NULL on delete), never copy dates; `date` may be null for an undated day; always format through `lib/time` with `item.timezone`. Decision 02-12: the port is optional - fall back to the node's own day config when `status === 'idle'`.
- **Consume `link`** (Landing CTA, e-mails): the resolver returns `[{ kind: 'agenda', url, label{en,ar}, nodeId }]` once the PM created the node's link in Settings; the project page link is `data/agenda.getProjectPageLink(projectId, hostId)`.
- **Founder page (step 8)**: render `<FounderAgendaButtons projectId={projectId} />` (`app/pages/founder/FounderAgendaButtons.jsx`) in the project block; it is empty until a PM enables "Show the Program Agenda button" on an agenda node whose visibility is platform/public. The route `/alumni/founder/programs/:projectId/agenda/:tab?` exists (`?node=` picks one agenda when several are exposed).
- **Reusable pieces**: `lib/meta.setPageMeta` (title + OG for any public page), `lib/export/xlsx.exportWorkbook` (multi-sheet download), `nodes/agenda/TabsEditor` + `agendaLogic.tabPatches/sortTabs` (any node that hosts `view` edges), `agendaLogic.tabsWithSlugs/findTab` (slug <-> tab), `DayEditor`'s HTML5 drag pattern (handle props + `moveItem`).

## Next builders: Selection handover (for Booking - interview mode - and Certificates)

- **Booking, interview mode (consuming the stage's `interview` port and the invitation tokens)**: `useInputPort('bookers')` on an edge from `selection.interview` (or `approved`/`all`) returns `items = ListItemView[]` of the stage (company-shaped, `itemId` = the SELECTION item id = `booking_invitations.booker_item_id` / `bookings.booker_item_id`; `stage` = the applicant's status; `upstream = { nodeId, port }`). Store `item.itemId` after `materializeItem(stageNodeId, item)` (virtual applicants have none; in practice an applicant in the Interview stage always has a row). The invitation link the stage e-mails is `/book/<booking slug>?invite=<token>` (route `book/:nodeRef/:hostRef?`): read `?invite`, call `get_public_booking_page(ref, host, invite)` (returns `invitation_required` / `already_booked` / `open`, m06d) and pass the token to `create_booking`. The booking node's public link may already exist (created lazily by `send_selection_emails` through `ensure_public_link(node,'booking')`): reuse `data/lists.getViewLink`'s pattern with kind `booking`. Tokens are created only by the stage's send (one active per applicant per Booking node, 30 days, revoked on resend); do not generate open per-member links (01-15). The stage's E-mails tab shows which Booking node it links to (`get_selection_extras().booking`); a `config.interviewBooking.linkedBookingNodeId` override exists in the stage Settings. `{{booking_link}}` is the only placeholder the Booking node has to honour from the stage side; the booking confirmation/cancellation e-mails are the Booking node's own templates (07 §1.5 placeholders `provider_name, start_time, end_time, timezone, meet_link, booker_email`) and can reuse `app_private.render_email_template` + the outbox pattern of `send_selection_emails` (null sender, idempotency key, `email_log`).
- **Certificates (consuming participants)**: read the project's participants with `data/participants.useParticipants(projectId)` -> `ParticipantView[]` and filter `status === 'attended'` client-side for the preview; the DB `issue_certificate(s)` RPCs (m06d) re-check `participants.status = 'attended'` and `projects.completed_at`. Only MATERIALISED rows have a `participantId` (virtual "eligible" rows cannot receive a certificate: the PM enrols them on the Participants page first - offer a link to `/projects/:id/participants`). `participants_display` gives the name (company name, else the response's company name, else the profile name) and `logo_path`; the founder/company identity link (`user_id` / `company_id`) is set from the Alumni area, not from the stage. `needs_review = true` marks a participant whose acceptance was reverted upstream (02-7): warn before issuing. The `rows:participant` port family exists in the DB `node_types` but no node emits it yet: if the Certificates node wants a port, the Participants "built-in list" (02 §7.3 step 2) is the place to add a resolver (`data/participants.listParticipants`).
- **Reusable pieces**: `shared/entities/EntityManager` extensions (`extraColumns`, `cardExtra`, `extraRowActions`, `disabledActions`, `bulkExtra`, `onOpen`, `sortExtra`), `useInputPort(...).results` (per-edge resolver result incl. a form's `schema` for labelled columns), `nodes/selection/selectionConfig.renderTemplate/sanitizePreviewHtml/unknownPlaceholders` (any node with e-mail templates; keep the DB renderer `app_private.render_email_template` as the one that matters), the reason modal + drawer patterns in `nodes/selection/Edit.jsx`, `data/participants.js` hooks, the Form Builder deep link `?tab=responses&response=<id>`.

## Next builders: Booking handover (for Distribution, Certificates, Landing, the founder page and the future OAuth / calendar-sync work)

- **Providers by reference = the same pattern Distribution needs for mentors/companies**: `useInputPort('mentors')` -> `ListItemView[]`; `nodes/booking/bookingConfig.mergeProviders(items, providerRows)` + `ProvidersTab` show how to join the list items with a node-owned overlay table keyed by `list_item_id` (`itemId` after `materializeItem`), how to treat `missing` items as inactive, how to keep orphans visible and how to avoid the orphan flash while the resolver loads (render a loader while `status === 'loading'`). `providerName(row, lang)` / `providerEntity(payloadProvider)` give display names/cards; the public payload exposes only name, title, organisation, photo, bio, notice - never the booking e-mail or a Meet link.
- **Time libs**: slot math goes through `lib/time/slots` (`slotsBetween`, `roundMinutes`, `isWithinNotice`, `ADVANCE_NOTICE_OPTIONS`) and instants through `lib/time/tz.toInstant(date, 'HH:MM', projectZone)`; `bookingConfig.planSlots({date, from, to, durationMin, gapMin, tz})` is the one wrapper (returns HH:MM + ISO), `splitPlan` previews overlaps, `groupByDay(rows, tz)` groups instants by project-zone date, `hhmm(instant, tz)` formats. The agenda `schedule` input (`useInputPort('schedule')` -> `items[].date/slots[]`) feeds the day picker in `SlotsTab`; fall back to a date input when `status === 'idle'` (02-12). Distribution should reuse these instead of `calculateDaySlotTimes` clones.
- **E-mail templates**: `shared/email/TemplateEditor` (ar/en, chips, preview, cc/admin) + `lib/email.js` (`renderTemplate`, `sanitizePreviewHtml`, `normalizeEmailTemplate`, `validateEmailTemplate`, `splitAddresses`) are the shared kit; the DB side is `app_private.render_email_template` (links become anchors for `booking_link`, `cancel_link`, `meet_link`), `app_private.booking_queue_email(booking, key, cancel_link)` is the pattern for "render the node's template for the booker's language and queue ONE outbox row + email_log, idempotent per round, null sender". `app_private.booking_vars` is the placeholder set derived from stored data in the PROJECT zone (names whitespace-collapsed).
- **Public write pattern** (Distribution live board, certificates verify, any visitor action): narrow anon-callable SECURITY DEFINER RPC that resolves the link (`app_private.resolve_link('booking', ref)` -> node + audience), validates everything server-side, takes a row lock on the contended row (`select ... for update` on the slot) AND has a unique-index backstop (`bookings (slot_id) where status = 'confirmed'`), returns hint tokens (`slot_unavailable`, `slot_too_soon`, `invitation_required`, `already_booked`, `booking_limit_reached`, `booking_invalid` with `field:reason` in `detail`) mapped in `data/errors.js`. The page refetches its payload after a conflict so a stale offer disappears.
- **Founder page (step 8)**: `show_to_participants` is stored on booking nodes but no `list_founder_*` RPC exists for bookings yet; a "Book a session" button would use `buildPublicUrl('booking', ref)` from `lib/links` (the `link` out port resolver returns it) for nodes that are platform/public and have `show_to_participants = true`, and a founder's name/e-mail prefill already works (`prefill` from `useAuth`).
- **Landing / e-mails**: `useInputPort('link')` on an edge from `booking.link` returns `[{ kind: 'booking', url, label, nodeId }]` once the PM created the link in Settings (`data/booking.ensureBookingLink`). The Selection stage's `{{booking_link}}` is appended with the per-applicant token by `send_selection_emails`; Booking never generates open per-member links.
- **Handover for calendar sync / OAuth (design 07 section 2, not built)**: the DB contract is complete; the future `calendar-sync` edge function (service role, Deno) only has to run this loop and must NOT change how bookings are written. (1) `claim_integration_jobs('calendar_create'|'calendar_update'|'calendar_cancel', n)` (m06d) claims `pending` jobs whose `next_attempt_at <= now()` and sets `processing`; each job row carries `booking_id`, `connection_id` (the node's organizer connection or null) and `payload` (`{lang}` on create, `{slot_id}` on update, `{origin:'restore'}` on a restored booking). (2) `connection_id is null` -> resolve the owner: `bookings.calendar_owner_connection_id`, else `booking_settings.organizer_connection_id`, else the `system` connection (`startups@`); if none is usable call `release_integration_job(id, 'no connection')` (added in m34: back to `pending` after 15 min WITHOUT counting a failure; the UI shows "Waiting for a Google connection"). (3) Create: `events.insert` with `id` = the booking uuid without dashes (idempotent: on 409 `events.get` and adopt), `conferenceData.createRequest.requestId` = booking id and `conferenceDataVersion=1` for `meeting_link_mode = 'unique'`; shared mode puts `booking_settings.shared_meet_link` in `location`/`description` with NO hand-built conferenceData; an empty shared link is a `failed` job with a clear message (never silently unique, C-3). Start/end = the slot instants with `timeZone` = `projects.timezone`; attendees = booker (`bookings.booker_email`) + provider (`booking_providers.booking_email`, else the list item's contact e-mail) + the node's cc/admin lists, lower-cased, de-duplicated, invalid ones dropped with a note in `error`, `sendUpdates=all`. (4) Complete with `complete_job(id, result)` and write back `bookings.calendar_event_id`, `bookings.meet_link` (unique mode) and `calendar_owner_connection_id` (service role writes; the PM screens read them). (5) `calendar_update` patches start/end (+ attendees) on the same event (Meet link unchanged); `calendar_cancel` -> `events.delete` with `sendUpdates=all`, 404/410 = success. (6) `fail_job(id, message)` backs off (attempt² minutes) and goes `dead` after 5 attempts; `retry_calendar_job(booking)` (PM, already wired to the "Retry sync" button) puts failed/dead jobs back to `pending`. (7) A minute cron only retries `pending/failed` jobs through the indexed queue (no table scans, no anon key). (8) The Apps Script poller must be switched off per 07 section 2.3 BEFORE the worker runs, or both will create events. The UI already derives its state from the jobs (`bookingConfig.syncState`) and offers the connection select in Settings from `google_connections`; when OAuth ships only the select's data source (`get_booking_extras.connections`) and the worker are new. The outbox worker (`claim_email_batch`) is a separate piece and now has booking rows (null sender) to deliver.

## Next builders: Distribution handover (for Landing, Certificates and the founder page)

- **Landing**: Distribution has no `link` out port yet. If a landing card should point to the timetable add `outPort('link', ['link'], ..., { resolve })` to the manifest AND to `node_types.ports` (m02 row) and copy `nodes/booking/data.resolveLink`; the URLs are `data/distribution.getDistributionLinks(nodeId)` -> `{ matrix: { slug }, live: { slug } }` -> `buildPublicUrl('view', slug, { viewKey: 'matrix' | 'live' })` (links are created lazily from Settings through `ensure_public_link(node, 'view', 'matrix'|'live')`). The Agenda tab for a card is the existing `sessions-tab` / `my-meetings-tab` edge into the Agenda `tabs` port.
- **Certificates**: nothing is consumed from Distribution. If a template ever wants "mentoring sessions attended", `v_distribution_sessions` (m06c) is the read model (session + day + effective timing -> `starts_at` / `ends_at` in the project zone) keyed by `company_item_id`; participants and `attended` still come from `data/participants`.
- **Founder page (step 8)**: the "my meetings" content is the Agenda tab route `/alumni/founder/programs/:projectId/agenda/:tab?` (`my-meetings` slug) or the existing `my_meetings(project)` RPC; `show_to_participants` is stored on the node but no `list_founder_*` RPC for distribution exists. `MyScheduleTab` takes `{ data, audience, timezone }` (the `get_public_tab` payload) or `{ model }` and never fetches; it preselects the signed-in founder's own company from `data.my_company_ids`.
- **Reusable pieces**: `matching.js` (`hungarian`, `mulberry32`, `generateDistribution`: any "assign people to cells with history and fairness" problem), `nodes/distribution/TimeRange.jsx` (a time range that survives RTL: every time is its own bidi isolate), `dayLabels.js`, `useNow.js` (a ticking `Date`), `timerSound.js` (Web Audio ticks/alarm behind a user gesture, preference in guarded localStorage), `app/shells/PublicWideShell.jsx` (public pages that need 1200 px), `data/distribution.materializeCandidates` (virtual list items -> real ids in one `ensure_items` per source list), the **server-timestamp timer pattern** (`distribution_timers` + a PM-only control RPC + a public read RPC returning `server_now`; the client computes `ends_at - (now + offset)` and polls lightly: `timerRemaining` / `serverOffsetMs` / `usePublicTimer`), and the split used for node data that has both a draft and live rows: configuration = a draft saved with `save_node` children, sessions/rows = validated row-level RPCs with Undo = the inverse call.
- **Gotchas found**: an `sr-only` (position absolute) element inside an `overflow-x-auto` wrapper without a positioned ancestor widens the whole mobile page - give the wrapper `relative`; a scalar sub-select in the same SQL statement as a data-modifying function call sees the OLD snapshot (split such checks in tests); `normalizeDistConfig` clamps, so validate the raw typed value.

## Next builders: Resource Library handover (for the founder Program page, Landing and certificates)

- **Founder Program page (step 8), the `library` block**: render `<FounderLibraryButtons projectId={projectId} />` (`app/pages/founder/FounderLibraryButtons.jsx`; one button per library the PM exposed with Settings "Show on the participants' Program page" AND visibility platform/public; label = the node's name; empty while nothing is exposed). It links to `/view/<ref>/library-tab` (a library with its own page link) or to the tab `/agenda/<ref>/library` (a library that is a tab of the project page); both render `LibraryTab` with the audience the DB decides, so a signed-in participant also sees the `participants` resources. To embed the library INLINE in the founder page instead: `const q = usePublicLibrary(ref)` (`data/library`) and `<LibraryTab data={q.data.data} title={...} audience={q.data.viewer} hideTitle />` (lazy: `manifest.contributesViews[0].component`); `list_founder_libraries(project)` returns `[{ node_id, name_en/ar, ref, via: 'node'|'host', count }]` (`count` = visible, non-hidden resources) for the block title.
- **Tab view contract (done for the Agenda host)**: key `library-tab`, payload `data = { config {layout, groupBy, searchEnabled}, categories [{id,name_en,name_ar}], resources [{id, category_id, title_*, description_*, type, url, file_path, has_file, mime, bytes, cover_path, sort_order, visibility (PMs only)}] }`; hidden resources never arrive; `file_path` is only the Storage name to sign, never a URL.
- **Reusable pieces**: `data/library.signedResourceUrl(path, { download })` / `signedResourceUrls(paths)` (ANY node that must serve private files: certificate templates, form uploads for PMs), `nodes/library/openResource.js` (open in a new tab without popup blocking / download with a file name), `lib/resourceFiles` (accepted types and size rules shared by uploads), the storage policy pattern of m36 (anon has no USAGE on `app_private`: a policy for anon/authenticated must call a PUBLIC SECURITY DEFINER function with EXECUTE granted, never a revoked one: L8), the upload bookkeeping in `Edit.jsx useAssetSession` (unsaved uploads are removed again), `ResourceRow` / `CategoriesPanel` as examples of draft rows with inline editors, `downloadName`.
- **Landing / e-mails**: Library has no `link` out port; the page URL is `buildPublicUrl('view', slug, { viewKey: 'library-tab' })` once the PM created the link in Settings (`data/library.getLibraryLink`).
- **Gotchas found**: any permission error raised for `authenticated` / `anon` at the top level of a statement can segfault this local Postgres image (test denials inside a plpgsql `exception` block or through PostgREST, as v36 does); Storage refuses a direct `delete from storage.objects` (use the Storage API); on the SHARED Browser pane a click needs the tab fronted in the SAME batch, and every call needs the explicit `tabId` of your own tab.

## Next builders: Landing page handover (for the founder Program page, e-mails and any node that shares a public page)

- **Founder Program page (step 8), the `landing` block**: render `<FounderLandingButtons projectId={projectId} />` (`app/pages/founder/FounderLandingButtons.jsx`, data `useFounderLandings` -> `list_founder_landings(project)`, m37): one button per landing page the PM exposed with Settings "Show to participants" AND visibility platform/public AND a created public link; label = the node's name; it opens `/view/<ref>/landing?lang=` (the full-width `PublicLandingShell`, the audience the DB decides: participants see platform pages). Nothing renders while nothing is exposed. A landing that is only a TAB of the agenda / project page (no own link) is reachable through the Agenda host and `FounderAgendaButtons`; it is not listed here.
- **Consuming the landing's own URL**: `useInputPort('link')` on an edge from `landing_page.link` returns `[{ kind: 'landing', url, label{en,ar}, nodeId }]` (resolver `nodes/landing-page/data.js`, `buildPublicUrl('view', slug, { viewKey: 'landing' })`) once the PM created the link in the landing's Settings.
- **Sharing any page from any node** (reuse, do not copy): `nodes/landing-page/shareLogic.js` is pure (`fillLink`, `buildChannel(platform, { card, lang, link, sourceable })`, `nativeSharePayload`) and `ShareDialog.jsx` takes `{ card: { id, title, chip, presets }, channels, link, sourceable, track }`; a node that wants "share this page" only has to build such a card object. The form's thank-you share buttons (`FormRenderer`) still use `lib/links.buildShareUrl` (copy-only Facebook / LinkedIn, no presets): they could move onto this logic. `lib/clipboard` does not exist: `nodes/landing-page/clipboard.js` (`copyText` with the execCommand fallback, `canNativeShare`, `canShareFiles`) is the one helper to lift into `lib/` when a second consumer appears.
- **Browser-side image composition** (certificates are canvas-rendered too, decision 06-5): `photoCompose.js` has the testable pieces (`composeToCanvas` with an injected canvas, `canvasToPngBlob`, `decodePhoto` with EXIF + the 4096 px downscale, `loadFrameImage` = fetch -> bitmap without tainting, `analyzeAlpha` / `frameProblems` for validating a template with a transparent area); unit tests use a fake 2D context that records the draw calls.
- **Tab contract for a landing-like view**: `contributesViews [{ key: 'landing-tab', component: () => import('./LandingTab.jsx') }]`; the tab gets `data = { node_id, config, cards, frames }` from `get_public_tab` (the SAME payload as `get_public_landing`, built once by `app_private.landing_public_payload`) and sends analytics with the host's ref from the route params + `data.node_id` (so a view contributed to an Agenda can count its own events: `track_landing_event(ref, type, card, platform, node)` verifies the edge).
- **Public full-width pages**: `app/shells/PublicLandingShell.jsx` (site header + full-width main + footer) is the shell for marketing-style pages; `lib/meta.setPageMeta` sets title / OG; `useContainerWidth` + `isCompactWidth` (`nodes/landing-page/useContainerWidth.js`) is the pattern for components that must be right in a narrow container, not only on a narrow screen.
- **Gotchas found**: see deviation 75 (`cn()` drops custom font-size tokens next to a text colour; `overflow-x: hidden` on `html, body` kills `position: sticky`); `userEvent.setup()` installs its own `navigator.clipboard`, so a test must define its clipboard mock AFTER `setup()`; jsdom has no `ResizeObserver`, canvas or `scrollIntoView` (the renderer guards them); the first import of a lazy node chunk can exceed the 5 s `findBy*` timeout on a loaded machine (the landing test pre-imports its chunks in `beforeAll`).

## Next builders: Certificates handover (for the founder Program page and e-mails)

- **Founder Program page (step 8), the `certificate` block**: render `<FounderCertificateButton projectId={projectId} />` (`app/pages/founder/FounderCertificateButton.jsx`; data `useMyCertificates(projectId)` -> `list_my_certificates(project)`, m38). The DB already decides everything (issued, not revoked, the participant ATTENDED, the project is COMPLETED, the node's "Show to participants" is on): it renders NOTHING otherwise (no teaser, as 06 section 10.2 / docs/13). Each certificate shows the printed name, program, code, issue date, **Preview / PNG / PDF** (`shared/certificates/CertificateActions`: the render payload is fetched on click, the canvas renderer is a lazy chunk) and a "Verify online" link to `/verify/<code>`. If the page prefers the block order from `get_founder_program_page` (06 section 10.2), only the placement changes.
- **Reusing the renderer elsewhere** (an e-mail attachment worker, an alumni profile tab): `shared/certificates/renderPayload.renderPayloadToCanvas(payload, canvas, { scale })` / `downloadCertificate(payload, 'png'|'pdf')` take the payload of `get_certificate_render_payload(cert)` (PM or owner) or the `render` of the public verification; `lib/certificates/render.renderCertificate` is the pure-ish core (inject a fake canvas and `fontLoader` in tests), `lib/certificates/pdf.canvasToPdfBlob` wraps any canvas into a one-page PDF, `lib/certificates/qr` is the local QR (any "scan to open" need: draw it, never call a service).
- **Verifier**: `/verify/:code?` + `usePublicVerification(code)`; `get_public_certificate_verification` is anon-callable, rate limited (hint `certificate_rate_limited`) and answers only `status`, names, dates and `render`; build "verify" links with `buildPublicUrl('verify', code)` (the QR on every certificate encodes exactly that).
- **Alumni platform**: certificates are tied to `participants` (`participant -> project -> program`, `company_id` / `user_id`); an alumni "my certificates" tab can call `list_my_certificates()` (no project = all of them) and a manager can read the rows through the existing alumni_manager policy.
- **Gotchas found**: the shared Browser pane can be hidden, and a hidden page never runs `requestAnimationFrame` (the editor preview uses a timeout instead); `Blob.stream()` does not exist in jsdom (the PDF code builds a `ReadableStream` itself); `ui/Modal` now ignores a changing `onClose` identity; a `select count(*)` with no FROM inside a "no rows expected" test is always 1; `import.meta.glob(..., { query: '?raw' })` is a handy way to assert "no source file mentions X".

## Next builders: Founder platform handover (for the alumni pages, Booking, the e-mails and the public program pages)

- **Add a block to the Program page** (e.g. Booking): (1) a branch in `get_founder_program_page` that appends `{ type, [node_id], data }` ONLY when the PM enabled it (call an existing exposure RPC where one exists; never trust a client flag); (2) if the block needs heavy data, a branch in `get_founder_block` that re-checks participation + exposure; (3) `blocks/<Name>Block.jsx` (default export, props `{ block, projectId, project }`), registered in `blocks/index.js` (`BLOCKS[type] = { icon, Component: lazy(...) }`); (4) the keys `founder.block.<type>` + `founder.nav.<type>` in `i18n.js`; (5) a test next to `founder.test.jsx`. A type the client does not know is ignored, so the DB can ship first.
- **Reusable pieces**: `ProgramCard` / `StatusBadges` (a program as a card, a status chip), `useMyPrograms()` (ONE cached query for the Home, the list and any "my company progress" widget), `useDateRange`, `BlockFrame` + `BlockError` + `QueryBlock` (per-section error isolation for any page made of independent sections), `SectionNav` (sticky in-page chips with an IntersectionObserver marker), `programLogic.nextUp(days, tz, now)` (the next session in the project zone), compact `EntityGrid dense` for any list of people on a phone.
- **Alumni pages (step 10)**: a company's programs = `list_my_programs`; its certificates = `list_my_certificates()` (no argument = all of them); `app_private.my_participation(project)` is THE definition of "my participation" (user or one of my companies, best status). The founder shell's bottom tabs already link `notifications` and `profile`.
- **Invitations / sign-up (step 8 placeholders `/signup`, `/invite/:token`)**: not built. When an invited founder is linked to a company the program shows through `company_founders`; when she signs up on her own the e-mail link of F4 applies. Keep both: they are different participants.
- **Public program pages (step 9)**: the founder RPCs never read `projects.visibility` (a participant sees the project through the participation, 03-1); a public page must filter by it itself.
- **Gotchas found**: the shared Browser pane can shrink while you work (a 375 x 812 emulation is then scaled to fit and the screenshots come out 197 px wide: emulate 375 x 426 or the pane height instead and take several slices); `127.0.0.1` and `localhost` have separate storage, so two logged-in sessions can live side by side, and tokens of users deleted by a previous builder stay in `127.0.0.1` storage (clear `sb-*` keys before the first anonymous submit); `form_input` sets a value without React noticing - click the field and `type`; `String.replace` strings with `$$` lose a `$` (use a replacer function when patching SQL from Node); `aspect-ratio` + `max-height` on a block transfers the maximum to the width unless the width is explicit; `cn()` + custom font-size tokens (deviation 75) did not bite here because the size tokens sit in plain `className` strings.

## Next builders: Withdrawn handover (for every new list, resolver, public / founder RPC and consumer of people)

- **Rule for any NEW RPC that returns or counts people / companies / experts** (a new node, an export, a notification, a report): call `app_private.is_withdrawn(project, form_response_id, company_id, item_flag)` (or `item_is_withdrawn(item)` / `provider_is_withdrawn(provider)`), never re-implement it and never read `participants.status` or `list_items.withdrawn_at` on your own. Pattern: PM payloads CARRY the flag (`withdrawn`, `withdrawal {at, reason, source}`), public / platform / founder payloads DROP the person; mutations that would add a withdrawn person (invitation, session, booking, e-mail) refuse with `item_withdrawn` / `distribution_invalid ... :withdrawn`. Add a check to `db/verify/v40_withdrawn.sql` (the chain fixture is at the top) and a client test.
- **Resolver**: `resolve_list(list, filter)` leaves withdrawn items out by default; PM managers pass `include_withdrawn: true` (and `include_excluded: true` for the legacy hidden). A chained stage / list drops an upstream item-flagged person always and an identity-withdrawn one unless `include_withdrawn`, so the PM keeps seeing the person (with the chip) downstream. `data/lists.js` `resolveList` / `useListItems` just forward the filter.
- **Client pieces to reuse**: `useWithdrawFlow({ nameOf, onWithdraw(items, reason), onReinstate(items) })` -> `{ withdraw, reinstate, dialog }` (render `dialog` once; confirmation + optional reason + Undo toast + error toast), `WithdrawDialog`, `EntityManager` actions `withdraw` / `reinstate` (+ legacy `exclude(item, false)`), `normalizeListItem` -> `withdrawn` / `withdrawal`, `filterItems(..., { presence })`, `isLegacyHidden`, `data/lists.js` `setItemWithdrawn`, `useListMutations().withdraw / reinstate / bulkWithdraw / bulkReinstate` (they refresh every cached node query: a withdrawal changes what the whole project resolves), `data/participants.js` `withdrawParticipant`, `useParticipantMutations().withdraw / reinstate`. Booking admin: `get_booking_extras().withdrawn_items` + `mergeProviders(items, providers, withdrawnById)`; Distribution PM payload: `entityFromRow(...).withdrawn`, `withdrawnIdsOf(model)`.
- **Revisions**: `app_private.bump_withdrawal_revs()` bumps every list / selection / distribution / booking / agenda / view_host / certificates node of the project when the withdrawn-ness changes, so other PM sessions and consumers refresh by revision.
- **Gotchas found**: the shared Browser pane only draws frames while your tab is the fronted one, so start each batch with `tabs_select` on your tab (a screenshot or a coordinate click on a background tab times out); `create_invitations` refuses a withdrawn item (build invitation fixtures BEFORE the withdrawal); `bg-destructive` + white text is 2.9:1 (use `bg-destructive-text` for a filled danger control); a `Button size="sm"` is 36 px high: add `min-h-11 lg:min-h-9` when it is the only control of a phone card.

## Next

Step 8 (the founder platform) is done: Programs, the Program page with its blocks, the lean Home and the DB for them. The Alumni pages of step 10 (founder + manager, `/signup`, `/invite/:token`) were built afterwards (m49 - m51) and fixed after an independent review (m52). What remains is listed in the STATUS TABLE below: the Athar Network pages (`/network/experts`, `/network/companies`, the company record) and the central library / form library admin pages, the public Program / Project pages that honour visibility (step 9), the share / OG function, and the workers and infrastructure (outbox, Gmail OAuth, calendar sync, CAPTCHA + signed uploads, CI, deploy).
