### Low findings: fixed

- `cert_is_member` / re-issue guard (above); `reissue_certificate` for an unlinked founder = `certificate_not_linked` (v43 B07 / B08).
- Public booking (m34): `create_booking` ignores a client `company_id` unless a SIGNED-IN founder names one of his own, not withdrawn, companies; the typed company name is stored instead (v43 F01-F04).
- `participants`: the PM-internal columns (`notes`, `needs_review`, `status_changed_by`, `source`, `source_node_id`, `created_by`) are not selectable by `authenticated` (column grants); PMs read them through the SECURITY DEFINER RPCs; `v_program_participations` gets `notes` through a definer helper (v43 C06, H01-H08). **No client code selected those columns directly.**
- Certificates after a withdrawal: the public verifier answers `valid` only for an **attended** participant (a withdrawn or un-marked one reads as revoked until reinstated; the row is untouched, reversible) (v43 C20-C25). **SUPERSEDED by m44 (2026-10-14, owner decision 18.2): an issued certificate stays valid and available after a withdrawal; see "Certificates survive a withdrawal" below.**
- Tamper evidence: `authenticated` may UPDATE only `revoked_at / revoked_by / revoked_reason` of `certificates`; the template tables (`certificate_templates`, `certificate_template_fields`) are read-only (writes only through `save_node`) (v43 C07, I01-I07; v38 R11 / v03 CE05 updated: 42501 for the API role, the 23514 trigger still refuses the owner).
- Storage depth: RESTRICTIVE extension allow-list on `storage.objects` (`ext_allow_insert/update`): `public-assets` = .webp (+ .svg only under `<owner>/logo/`), `founder-assets` = .webp, `resources` = the library file types; .html / .svg / .pdf can no longer be inserted into the image buckets (v43 J01-J12, v41 X03 / Y01 updated). **Not changed (documented)**: Storage serves objects without CSP / nosniff headers and checks only the declared type (a Storage setting, not ours); the TUS / S3 upload paths need a real PM token to test before go-live.
- Concurrency: `cert_claim` takes the same participant-level advisory lock as issue / revoke (the grant is re-read under it): a claim can no longer commit an active certificate under a grant that a concurrent participant revocation just revoked. A true two-session race cannot run inside the rolled-back suite: the lock is asserted in the catalogue (v43 C09) and the revoked-participant claim = forbidden (C26).
- Verifier rate limit: the bucket is Cloudflare's header, else the LAST X-Forwarded-For hop (the nearest proxy appended it; the first entries are client-controlled), a GLOBAL bucket (1500 / 10 min) is checked first (a spoofed header can no longer evade the cap or grow the table), expired buckets are purged (v43 K01-K05). **Not changed**: the public render block still carries `template.file_path` (project / node / file UUIDs, not secrets); dropping it would stop the verifier drawing the certificate.
- `merge_companies`: a dropped WITHDRAWN participant makes the kept one withdrawn (with its reason); a dropped participant that holds a certificate GRANT gets the friendly manual-review error (v43 L01 / L02).
- Audit-FK indexes: `list_items.withdrawn_by`, `participant_withdrawals.withdrawn_by`, `distribution_timers.day_id`, the composite `resources (node_id, category_id)`.
- **Verification suite hygiene**: v39 C04, v40 C07 / C08, v42 C10 are hermetic (counted in the MIGRATED projects only, so another session's committed throwaway rows cannot fail them); the stale stage-1 / 2a gates are refreshed instead of retired: v01 (storage objects >= baseline), v02 / v10 (the designed census must be PRESENT, later builds add more; the reviewed anon-executable list of 22 functions; ST.1 / ST.3 restated for restrictive policies; V10.4 knows the 44 memberships and 3 founders and ignores the transient rate-limit counters; V02.4 is skipped on a loaded database), v03 / v11 (the fixture accounts are PMs without the founder role (m25), the template fixture is written as the owner with the person-name field and a `.webp` path, B11 / BK05 / CE07 / CE09 / M05 / FO04 expectations updated for m25 / m34 / m39 / m42, v11's "PM without membership" accounts are fixtures since m23 made every real PM a member). **All of v01 .. v43 are green**: 21 scripts + v12 / v13 reports.
- RTL: `FounderCertificateButton` puts only the CODE in the `dir="ltr"` mono span, the label stays outside (browser-checked in Arabic at 375 px, no overflow).
- Certificates visibility: the Issue panel shows a notice with a link to Settings when certificates exist but "Show to participants" is off.
- Thumbnails: full-width covers (Programs list, Program page, founder Programs) are thumbnail-then-full (lazy), not the 320 px file upscaled; 40 px rows keep the thumbnail.
- Template upload memory: the size / aspect checks read the file HEADER (`peekTemplateSize`, no bitmap; one decode that is closed at once only when the header does not say), the live preview is the STORED converted WebP (signed URL), a replaced preview bitmap is closed and its cache entry dropped.
- Duplication: ONE `newId` (`lib/id.js`, always a valid UUID v4; was five definitions + two raw `crypto.randomUUID`), ONE `formatBytes(n, lang)`, ONE `removeObjects(bucket, paths)`, `framePublicUrl = publicImageUrl`, ONE `isActiveItem` (`lib/entities.js`, ten inline copies), ONE `WithdrawnChip` (`shared/entities`, the six per-node keys are gone), the unused `IMAGE` keys and two unused constants removed, `TEMPLATE_RULES.maxBytes` = `MAX_SOURCE_BYTES` (one 25 MB ceiling). **Not done (judged not worth the risk)**: merging the three image decoders (`lib/image/canvas.js`, `lib/certificates/templateFile.js`, `nodes/landing-page/photoCompose.js`: each has its own constraints and tests) and deriving `ImageField`'s `profile` from the upload `kind` (eight call sites, the upload kinds are chosen inside data modules).
- Dead code: `variableOf` and `activePeople` deleted, three `layout.js` constants and three `data/certificates.js` helpers un-exported, the `WithdrawDialog` default export dropped. **Kept on purpose**: `claimMyCertificate` (the explicit claim RPC for the step-10 alumni pages) and the `certificates.var.participant_name` label (used dynamically for a legacy referenced template).
- Bulk Withdrawn: `public.set_items_withdrawn(uuid[], boolean, text)` (atomic, <= 500, ordered locks) + `materializeItems` (ONE `ensure_items`) + ONE refresh; verified in the browser: ONE `set_items_withdrawn` request for 2 items (and for the reinstate). All or nothing: no half-done bulk.
- Accessibility: the ImageField file input is `tabIndex=-1` (no invisible tab stop), row / select-all checkboxes sit in 44 px labels, `Button size="sm"` is 44 px below `lg`, toasts pause while hovered or focused (and resume with at least 1 s left).
- i18n: the bulk Withdrawn and "left out" sentences are plural entries with all six Arabic categories (+ English one / other), the awkward Arabic sentence is rephrased, the Issue panel translates the participant status.
- Lint / guard: `.update(` on a storage bucket handle and `createSignedUploadUrl` are ESLint errors (proved with stdin probes, nothing written to the tree) and covered by the guard test; `uploadObject` sniffs the bytes.
- Docs: README (the stale Certificates paragraph, Safari / WASM, lazy preview, the review summary) and this file.

### Deviations (and why)

- R1. The expert fix is **flag propagation** (suggested alternative 2), not a wider predicate (alternative 1): it leaves the single predicate and its 17 consumers untouched, survives deleting the row that carried the flag, and re-running m40 later does not break (a 5-argument predicate would have changed every consumer's text, so re-running the patch-based m40 after it would fail).
- R2. `link_my_participations` is stricter than the review's suggestion (which kept the flag and added PM approval): it relies on GoTrue's own semantics (`confirmation_sent_at` is set when a confirmation e-mail is sent and is not cleared by the click). **Verify once on the live project after turning "Confirm email" ON** (sign up with a real address, click the link, check that the participant links). If the semantics differ on that GoTrue version, the helper is one SQL function.
- R3. The Safari fix adds a runtime dependency (`@jsquash/webp` 1.5.0, pinned, installed with `--ignore-scripts`; `package.json` + `package-lock.json` +1 entry). It is only ever downloaded by a browser that cannot encode WebP.
- R4. `certificate_templates` / `certificate_template_fields` lost INSERT / UPDATE / DELETE for the API roles (the review only named `certificates`): the same tamper path (rewriting what an issued certificate renders). The client never wrote them directly.
- R5. The optional lossy re-encode of `public/brand/pattern.webp` was **not done**: about 80 KB saved on the login / 404 pages only, at the risk of changing the pattern's look.
- R6. `auth.role()` hardening (12 functions) is defence in depth; through PostgREST the role claim is always set.

### Open questions for the owner

1. **Live Auth setting**: turn "Confirm email" ON (Authentication > Providers > Email) before go-live; until then no founder links by e-mail (by design) and PMs link accounts through the invitation / company records.
2. **Safari / iPhone**: please upload one cover and one public-form photo from a real iPhone (Safari) and from Safari on a Mac; the fallback is proven only through the development switch in Chrome. If it is slow on a phone (a 1920 px lossy encode takes about 1 s on a desktop), the server-side `image-convert` function is the next step. **Update (m45): the dev page `/dev/image-check` + `npm run dev:lan` let the owner run this test (guide in `web/README.md`).**
3. A withdrawn participant's issued certificate now reads **revoked** on the public verifier until reinstated (no data changed). Preferable, or should withdrawing revoke them for good? **ANSWERED by the owner on 2026-10-14 (decision 18.2): the certificate stays valid and available (m44).**
4. Should a PM be able to see / approve a founder's printed name before the certificate is created (the review's "PM review of the name list")? Today the PM sees the names in the Issue panel after creation and can revoke.

### Handover

- A new consumer that shows an EXPERT must use the single predicate through the item row (nothing else to do: the flag is on every row of the expert in the project). A new code path that INSERTs `list_items` for experts is covered by the trigger.
- Reuse: `isActiveItem` (`lib/entities.js`), `WithdrawnChip`, `setItemsWithdrawn` / `materializeItems` (`data/lists.js`), `removeObjects` (`data/storage.js`), `newId` (`lib/id.js`), `formatBytes` (`lib/image/format.js`), `app_private.auth_email_verified(user)` for any feature that must trust an e-mail address.
- Local browser testing of e-mail linking: simulate the confirmation click (see README "Review fixes"). The Browser pane check list used: bulk withdraw / reinstate (one RPC each), expert withdrawn from the copy list shows withdrawn in the original list with its reason, Issue panel (Arabic, 375 px, notice, 44 px dark-red buttons), founder block (RTL code), verifier (valid, then revoked after the withdrawal), WASM upload (cover + certificate template, real VP8), native upload (no .wasm request), 22-logo list lazy loading, program card cover (1920 px, lazy), Participants page contrast.
- **Baseline after cleanup**: 12 / 12 / 12 users / profiles / roles, 4 programs, 16 projects, 82 nodes, 53 edges, 141 companies, 44 program_members, 0 participants, 0 certificates, 0 grants, 612 list_items, 384 responses, 55 slots, 30 bookings, 716 sessions, 373 storage objects, 82 node_revisions, 0 staff_invitations, 3 founders, 0 company_founders, 0 participant_withdrawals, 0 verifier rate-limit rows: **no delta** (the reviewers' stray verifier rate-limit row was removed; the three FAIL rows they left in `migration.run` were superseded by PASS rows and kept as history; my own interim FAIL rows were deleted). Orphan scan over every `project_id` column, leftover test users and storage objects: none.
- **Gotchas found**: a role-switched call of a function the role may NOT execute (for example an `app_private` helper as `authenticated`) segfaults this local Postgres image: call helpers as the owner in verify scripts; the repo's SQL files have CRLF line endings (an anchor-based edit script must keep them); a bash heredoc with backticks and `$` inside a JavaScript template breaks the tool call: write such scripts with the Write tool; `vite` re-optimises dependencies after a lockfile change (the first dev start after `npm install` is slow); `window.__forceNoNativeWebp` must be set BEFORE the first upload of the page (the probe is cached).


## Certificates survive a withdrawal (m44 + v44, 2026-10-14; owner decision 08 section 18.2): done, deviations, open questions, handover

**Owner's words (18.2)**: an issued certificate STAYS VALID AND AVAILABLE even if the participant is later marked withdrawn; withdrawal after issuing can only happen by a PM mistake, so it must not revoke or hide certificates. This replaces the m43 behaviour (the verifier answered `revoked` for a withdrawn participant, the founder lost the certificate: see the superseded notes in "Review fixes" above). The PM keeps the manual revoke.

### The rule (now true everywhere)
Once a certificate EXISTS and the PM has not revoked it, it is valid in the public verifier, listed and renderable for its founder, and readable by its owner, WHATEVER later happens to the participant's status (withdrawn, or attended -> accepted by mistake). Only `revoke_certificate` / `revoke_participant_certificates` ends it. CREATING one is unchanged: issuing, claiming and re-issuing need an ATTENDED participant + a completed project + the PM's authorisation (grant).

### Done
- **DB** (`db/migrations/m44_certificates_survive_withdrawal.sql`, nine places, each patched IN PLACE on its current definition with the m43 technique; m40 - m43 untouched): the verifier (`valid` = not revoked), `get_certificate_render_payload`, `list_my_certificates` (the claim loop that creates NEW ones keeps its attended join), `cert_template_readable` (the storage rule that signs the template for the owner), the RLS policy `certificates_select_pm_alumni_own` (own active row, no participants subquery), `get_founder_program_page` (a WITHDRAWN participant keeps the status-only header and its `blocks` now hold the ONE block he keeps, `certificate`, from the same `list_my_certificates` call), `list_my_programs` (a withdrawn row carries `certificate_count` through the new helper `app_private.my_certificate_count`: no claim, no API role executes it), `get_certificates_admin` (a linked founder with no certificate under a non-attended participant is `not_eligible`, not "claimable"), `reissue_certificate` (re-issuing creates a certificate: a non-attended participant is refused with `certificate_not_attended`, not "no longer linked"). Every patch recognises its own result (extension / removal shaped anchors), so the file is re-runnable.
- **UI decision (the least surprising)**: the withdrawn founder's Program page stays the STATUS-ONLY page (header with the names, red "Withdrawn" chip, no cover / dates / agenda) and gains the certificate block below the status card, whose text then says "your certificate stays valid and available below" (the page renders exactly the blocks the server sent, restricted or not: no client rule). The Programs list keeps hiding withdrawn programs in the collapsed section (decision 17.1 / 02-7), but a withdrawn card whose founder still holds a valid certificate shows "Only its status and your certificate are shown" and ONE full-card link "My certificate" (plural-aware, Arabic dual / plural) to that page; without a certificate the card is as before (no link). The alumni "my certificates" page of step 10 will list everything through `list_my_certificates()` (no argument), which is status-independent now.
- **Issue panel (PM)**: the row flag "withdrawn after issue" / "no longer marked attended" is information now (muted, Info icon, was red + warning triangle) and says what happens: stays valid and available to the founder (the public verifier still says valid) until the PM revokes it; reinstate the participant if the withdrawal was a mistake. The "Revoke" / "Revoke all" buttons stay; "Issue again" is NOT offered for a participant that is not attended (`canReissue` checks the status; the server refuses too); the new person state `not_eligible` reads "No certificate can be created now: the participant is not marked attended" (amber), the claimable counts exclude it.
- **Wording**: the "Mark as withdrawn" dialog says new certificates are left out and that an already issued certificate stays valid and available (en + ar); the Participants hint says the same; the old Issue-panel Arabic text that used "تلغيها" (= cancel) for revoke now uses "تُبطلها" like the rest of the module.
- Tests: `founder.test.jsx` +3 (a withdrawn card with a certificate links to it and one without does not; the same card in Arabic RTL with the dual plural; the withdrawn Program page keeps the status card AND the certificate block and nothing else, no cover / dates), `certificates.test.jsx` +1 (a withdrawn participant: issued person revocable, `not_eligible` instead of "Waiting", no "Issue again", the flag text, claimable count 0; the existing withdrawn row asserts the new flag wording), `certConfig.test.js` +1 (`canReissue` needs attended; `canRevokeAll` unchanged). v42 W01, v43 C21 / C24 updated (only the checks that encoded the superseded behaviour; each says "m44" in its text); v38 and v40 needed no change.

### Verification (local stack, dev server port 5421, own tabs, throwaway data only)
- `npm run lint` clean; `npx vitest run`: **74 files, 758 tests, all green** (the whole suite incl. the parallel work); `npm run build` green (founder chunks: `FounderProgramPage` 12.8 kB (4.8 gzip; was 12.6), `FounderProgramsPage` 2.4 kB, `CertificateBlock` 2.5 kB; `routes` 77.8 kB; vendors unchanged).
- DB: `m44` applied twice (the second run patches nothing) and in the chain m42 -> m43 -> m44 (proved: m43 alone re-run puts the attended condition back in the verifier, m44 right after restores it). `v44` **63/63**. `v44` DISCRIMINATES: after re-creating the pre-m44 definitions (m42 + m43 re-run) 17 of its checks fail; m44 makes all pass. The whole suite re-run green: v01, v02, v03 (133), v10, v11 (182), v24 48, v25 22, v30 23, v31 48, v32 28, v33 62, v34 61, v35 85, v36 73, v37 66, v38 99, v39 52, v40 107, v41 47, v42 120, v43 103, v44 63.
- **Browser** (`vite --mode development --host 127.0.0.1 --port 5421`; `127.0.0.1` = the PM, `localhost` = the founders and the anonymous visitor; a throwaway super_admin + two founders created by SQL with generated passwords that a one-shot local helper served to the page, never typed into a tool call; a program + project (Asia/Riyadh) + a Certificates node; the Browser pane is shared, every call carried my own tabId): **template** - a 2480 x 1754 PNG chosen in the editor was converted by the pipeline and stored as `.webp` (2480 x 1754), person name + company name + program + code + QR added, ONE Save (v1, 5 fields). **Issue** - "Issue to all attended (1)" -> confirm -> "Authorised 1, created 1": founder Sara holds a certificate; a second founder of the company (Hadi) was linked afterwards (no certificate). **Attended founder** - Program page shows "My certificate"; PNG download = `image/png`, 331,269 bytes, `Certificate - Sara Ali - <code>.png`. **The PM marks the participant WITHDRAWN** on the Participants page (the dialog says an issued certificate stays valid; reason typed) -> DB: participant `withdrawn`, certificate and grant untouched. **Anonymous verifier** (`localhost`, signed out) -> "Valid certificate" (Sara Ali, company, program, cohort, dates, code) and the certificate canvas drawn (the template pixel painted). **Founder after the withdrawal** - the Programs list shows only the collapsed "Withdrawn program (1)"; expanded, the status-only card reads "Only its status and your certificate are shown" with the "My certificate" link (English, phone width); the link opens the status-only page (Arabic RTL at 375 px: header, "لقد انسحبت من هذا البرنامج", "تبقى صالحة ومتاحة أدناه", the certificate block with Preview / PNG / PDF; `scrollWidth` 529 inside a 544 px pane); **the PNG download is again `image/png`, 331,269 bytes (identical)**. **A withdrawn participant WITHOUT a certificate cannot claim one**: Hadi's page = status card only (0 blocks), `claim_my_certificate` = 42501 `forbidden` (HTTP 403), `list_my_certificates` = [], 0 certificate rows, `certificate_count` 0, and nothing was created in the DB. **PM Issue panel** (English, 375 px) - the row has the red "Withdrawn" chip and the new informational flag, Sara "Certificate issued" with Preview / PNG / PDF / Revoke, Hadi "No certificate can be created now: the participant is not marked attended" (amber), no "Issue again". **Revoke still works**: the PM revoked Sara's certificate with a reason (still withdrawn) -> the verifier reads "Revoked certificate" and her page / card / list lose it (`restricted`, 0 blocks, `certificate_count` 0). **Reinstate** (Participants page) -> the Issue panel offers "Issue again" for Sara (confirmation dialog) -> a new code with origin `reissue`, and Hadi is "Waiting" again; Hadi opened his Program page and his OWN certificate was claimed (creation unchanged); the PM withdrew the participant a second time (the same RPC the UI calls) and Hadi's English status-only page showed the status card + "My certificate" block at phone width (screenshot viewed). Console: no errors except the expected 403 of the refused claim. Screenshots were viewed in the session (not saved into the repo).
- **Baseline after cleanup**: 12 / 12 / 12 users / profiles / roles, 4 programs, 16 projects, 82 nodes, 53 edges, 141 companies, 44 program_members, 0 participants, 0 certificates, 0 grants, 0 templates / fields, 0 participant_withdrawals, 612 list_items, 384 responses, 30 bookings, 82 node_revisions, 0 staff_invitations, 3 founders, 0 company_founders, 0 notifications / opportunities / alumni_invitations, 0 outbox rows, 0 verifier rate-limit rows: **no delta from my work**. The throwaway rows (3 users, their profiles / roles / identities, 1 program, 1 project, 2 nodes, 1 company, 2 founders, the participant, its withdrawal row, the template + 5 fields, 3 certificates + 1 grant) were deleted with `session_replication_role = replica` followed by a generic FK orphan scan over every FK of `public` / `app_private` (0 orphan groups); the template object was removed through the Storage API (a direct delete of a `storage.objects` row is refused) after its certificates; both browser origins signed out and their `sb-*` keys cleared, the dev server and the helper stopped, my tabs closed. **Storage objects read 371 against the recorded 373**: the only buckets that shrank are `event-assets` / `migration-archive`, which a parallel session is converting and deleting (decision 18.4); my own template object was added and removed (net 0).

### Deviations (and why)
- D1. The founder-side gates "project completed" and "node shown to participants" were NOT changed. They are the PM's own exposure switches (a re-opened project / a node switched to "not shown" still hides the certificate from the founder), not participant statuses, and v42 L11 / L12 and v38 F14 encode them on purpose. The public verifier never looked at either, so a certificate can be valid on the verifier while hidden from its founder after a re-open: see open question 1.
- D2. `claim_my_certificate` (and the claim loop of `list_my_certificates`) stay strictly attended: an explicit claim from a founder whose participant is withdrawn answers `forbidden` even when he already holds a certificate (nothing NEW can be claimed; he lists the existing one through `list_my_certificates`, which no longer needs it). v42 W02 is unchanged and still true.
- D3. The restricted Program page carries its one block in the normal `blocks` list instead of a dedicated field: the client renders whatever the server sends, a type the client does not know is ignored, and no new payload shape exists.
- D4. A new person state `not_eligible` in the Issue panel payload (additive; the panel, its tones and both dictionaries know it). Without it a withdrawn participant's late-linked founder would read "Waiting: created when he opens his page", which can no longer happen.
- D5. m44 patches by text anchors on the current definitions (the m37 / m43 technique) instead of re-creating whole functions, so a parallel migration that patches the same functions keeps working. Consequence documented in the log: m43 re-run ALONE after m44 puts the verifier's attended condition back (its anchor is the same text); the chain order restores it (proved).

### Open questions for the owner
1. A RE-OPENED project (completion removed) or a Certificates node switched to "not shown to participants" still hides the certificates from their founders (the public verifier stays valid). Should an issued certificate also survive those two PM switches on the founder side, or are they the PM's deliberate way to hide the block?
2. A withdrawn program stays in the collapsed "Withdrawn programs" section of the founder's Programs list (the card links to the certificate). Should a program with a valid certificate be shown un-collapsed instead, or is the step-10 alumni "My certificates" page enough?
3. The withdrawal dialog now says an already issued certificate stays valid. Should the PM also see how many certificates a participant holds in that dialog before confirming?

### Handover
- **Any new feature that shows an EXISTING certificate must not look at `participants.status`** (verifier, lists, alumni "my certificates", e-mails, exports); only creating one (issue, claim, re-issue) does. Use `list_my_certificates(project | null)` for "what I hold", `app_private.my_certificate_count(project)` for a count (no claim), `get_certificate_render_payload` for the drawing data.
- The withdrawn Program page: `restricted: true` + `blocks` (at most `certificate`). A new block that must survive a withdrawal has to be added to that branch of `get_founder_program_page` on purpose; everything else stays status-only (03-6).
- Client: `ProgramCard` reads `certificate_count` of a restricted row; `FounderProgramPage` renders `blocks` in the restricted state too; `certConfig.canReissue(row, person)` needs the row status; `PERSON_TONES.not_eligible`.
- **Gotchas found**: a psql dollar-quote needs the whole `$q$` (an alias `q` followed by `$` ends nothing: rename the alias); a volatile function (`list_my_certificates` claims) does not show its INSERT to the other sub-selects of the SAME statement (assert the row in a separate statement); `auth.refresh_tokens.user_id` is varchar (cast when deleting auth rows by uuid); `python` does not exist on this machine (use `node` for scripted edits) and backticks inside a double-quoted `node -e` are executed by bash (write scripts with the Write tool); the Browser pane may be created by the first `preview_start` with only a `seed` tab: create / close your own tabs by id; in this pane a click on an in-app link (without `?lang=`) showed the founder UI in Arabic although the previous load had `?lang=en` (observed, not investigated: pass `?lang=` on every navigation you screenshot).


## Old originals converted + deleted, the OG JPEG derivative and the iPhone / Safari diagnostics (m45 + v45, 2026-10-14; owner decisions 08 sections 18.4 / 18.5 / 18.6): done, deviations, open questions, handover

**Owner's words**: 18.4 convert ALL the old raster originals to WebP and delete the old ones; 18.5 the owner asked how to help test WebP on an iPhone / Safari (answer: a dev diagnostics page + a LAN run script); 18.6 build a JPEG derivative for link previews (LinkedIn / WhatsApp), WebP stays the stored and displayed format.

### Done
1. **Old raster originals: converted, then deleted (local stack, two explicit steps)**. `db/scripts/convert-images.mjs` has three modes: dry run (default), `--apply` (step 1: EVERY raster of EVERY bucket to its WebP sibling `<dir>/<base>.webp`, thumbnails only where the bucket carries them, audit row in `app_private.image_conversions`, every reference rewritten incl. `migration.asset_result.archive_path`, `storage_dangling_refs()` proven empty, nothing deleted) and `--delete-originals` (step 2, a separate run, `--dry-run` previews: only originals with a verified WebP sibling, no reference left and an unchanged sha256, removed THROUGH THE STORAGE API after the manifest `local-db/backups/deleted-originals-manifest.csv` (git-ignored) is on disk). Run locally on 2026-10-14 after a checkpoint `local-db/backups/pre-m45.dump`: **133 conversions** (57 references rewritten, all in `migration.asset_result.archive_path`), **133 originals deleted + 18 stale metadata rows + 2 non-images removed (153 manifest rows)**, nothing skipped.

| Bucket | Before (objects / bytes) | After (objects / bytes) |
|---|---|---|
| `event-assets` | 112 / 72,911,628 | 92 / 14,467,565 (76 WebP of 3,405,336 B: -94% on the converted files) |
| `migration-archive` | 58 / 25,409,163 | 58 / 2,552,240 (57 WebP of 2,407,738 B: -90%) |
| `public-assets` | 198 / 4,917,684 | 198 / 4,917,684 (untouched) |
| `resources` | 5 / 10,354,348 | 5 / 10,354,348 (untouched) |
| `og-images` (new) | - | 18 / 1,034,794 |
| **Total** | **373 / 113,592,823** | **371 / 33,326,631** |

   The metadata sum shrank by 80.3 MB; the files on disk by about 76.4 MB (the 18 stale rows declared 3.86 MB of files that were already missing; WebP copies add 5.81 MB, JPEG derivatives 1.03 MB). **Recoverable**: the 76 `event-assets` originals (and the 2 non-images) are still in `local-db/export/storage/event-assets/` (sha256 re-checked against the manifest: all equal) and on the live project; the 18 stale rows (`event-assets/experts/c1522ab6-.../*.jpeg`, listed in `db/MIGRATION-LOG.md` m45) have no file anywhere; the 57 `migration-archive` originals exist ONLY in the manifest (path, size, sha256): stage 2b fetched them from Google (56 `drive.google.com`, 1 `lh3.googleusercontent.com`) and the source URLs are intact in `migration.asset_map` / `asset_result.old_path`. Reference proof: a scan of every text / json / jsonb / array column of `public`, `legacy`, `migration`, `app_private` for each deleted path finds 0 hits in `public` and 0 in `archive_path`; the only mentions left are provenance URLs of the OLD project (82 rows in 15 `legacy.*` columns, `migration.asset_map` / `asset_refs` / `asset_result.old_path`), which are not storage references and are never rewritten. `storage_dangling_refs()` = 0, `nonwebp_image_objects` = 0. v41 D02 now says "no raster in ANY bucket".
2. **The OG JPEG derivative**. `m45` adds the PUBLIC bucket `og-images` (`image/jpeg` only, 1 MiB; world-readable; written only by a manager of the owner folder or a super_admin under `global/`, only for names `<owner>/<kind>/<uuid>.jpg`), extends the WebP-only rule to every other bucket, and adds `app_private.og_image_path` (+ service-role-only `public.og_image_path`), `og_source_refs()`, `og_missing_refs()`, `og_orphans`. Browser: the profiles `cover` and `landing` carry `og: true`; `convertToWebp` also returns `og` (1200 x 630, cover crop, white behind alpha, progressive mozjpeg via `@jsquash/jpeg` (a 252 KB `.wasm`, 60 KB gzip, loaded only when a shared image is uploaded; canvas baseline JPEG as the fallback), quality 0.82 stepping down to 0.52 until <= 300 KB); `data/storage.js` `storeImage` stores it at the path derived from the WebP path (no DB column), never fails the upload because of it (`ogPath` / `ogError`), `removeImage` removes it, `publicOgUrl(path)`; `ImageField` shows "Link preview (JPEG 1200 x 630): 68 KB" (ar + en). The folder kind `project-cover` (the migrated name) now maps to the cover profile. **Backfill**: `db/scripts/backfill-og-images.mjs` made the **18** derivatives of the shared images that existed (7 project covers, 2 public-link previews, 5 form covers, 3 landing card covers, 1 landing hero; 1,034,794 B, largest 110 KB); `og_missing_refs()` = 0. **How the future share function resolves the og:image URL** is documented in `web/README.md` ("Link-preview JPEG") and `db/MIGRATION-LOG.md` (m45): source path by kind (`og_source_refs()`), `ogPath = source.replace(/\.webp$/i, '.jpg')` (or `public.og_image_path`), URL `<SUPABASE_URL>/storage/v1/object/public/og-images/<ogPath>` + `og:image:type / width / height` + `twitter:card`, generic Athar card when there is no derivative.
3. **iPhone / Safari diagnostics**. Dev-only page **`/dev/image-check`** (`app/pages/DevImageCheckPage.jsx`, route added behind `import.meta.env.DEV` like `/dev/nodes`; no sign-in; verified absent from `dist`): device facts (user agent parsed: iPhone / iPad / iPad in desktop mode / Android / Windows / Mac, browser + engine, screen, secure context, WebAssembly + SIMD, OffscreenCanvas), four probes measured now (native canvas WebP, WebAssembly WebP fallback, native canvas JPEG, progressive WebAssembly JPEG, with first-run and warm timings), "what the app will use", a developer switch that pretends the browser cannot make WebP (exercises the fallback in Chrome), then "Choose a photo" (profile cover / landing / content / avatar) or a built-in test image: it runs the REAL `convertToWebp` and shows per-stage timings, before / after bytes and dimensions, whether the rotation tag was applied (EXIF read from the file head vs the decoded size: OK / WRONG / not provable), the original, the converted WebP and the link-preview JPEG side by side, which encoder did the work, a PASS / WARN / FAIL verdict (WARN = fallback in use, expected on Safari), and a plain-text English report with "Copy report" (clipboard API, else a selectable box for an `http` page). Arabic RTL and English, 44 px targets. Pure logic in `lib/image/diagnostics.js` (`parseUserAgent`, `readExifOrientation`, `orientationVerdict`, `stageTimings`, `collectEnvironment`, `buildReport`), browser probes in `lib/image/probe.js`, the test picture in `lib/image/testImage.js`. **`web/scripts/lan-dev.ps1`** (`npm run dev:lan`): finds the LAN IPv4 (the adapter with a default gateway, virtual adapters skipped), sets `VITE_SUPABASE_URL=http://<ip>:54321` for that process only (`web/.env.local` untouched), starts Vite with `--host` on port 5400 (`--strictPort`), prints the phone URL and the exact `New-NetFirewallRule` / `Remove-NetFirewallRule` commands (never applied), warns when the Wi-Fi profile is Public; `-DryRun`, `-Ip`, `-Port`, `-SupabasePort`. `lib/env.js` `classifyTarget` treats a private LAN address as local (the banner stays green on the phone, `*.supabase.co` stays remote). The Arabic + English guide is in `web/README.md` ("Testing on an iPhone / Safari").

### Verification (local stack, dev server port 5422, own tab, throwaway data only)
- `npm run lint` clean; `npx vitest run`: **78 files, 834 tests, all green** (new / changed for this work: `lib/image/diagnostics.test.js` 25, `og.test.js` 17, `wasmJpeg.test.js` 4, `app/pages/DevImageCheckPage.test.jsx` 11, plus additions to `canvas.test.js`, `storage.test.js`, `storage.guard.test.js`, `ImageField.test.jsx`, `env.test.js`, `profiles.test.js`); `npm run build` green: entry `index` 12.0 kB (4.55 gzip, unchanged), `routes` 77.8 kB, the pipeline stays lazy: `storage` 3.8 kB (was 3.0), `convert` 9.7 kB (was 7.7; it now also holds the JPEG derivative), `wasmJpeg` 0.8 kB + the `@jsquash/jpeg` encode chunk 19 kB + `mozjpeg_enc.wasm` 252 kB (60 kB gzip), all dynamic imports started only by an upload of a shared image. The dev page is not in `dist` (no `imgcheck` / `built-in-test` string); the mozjpeg `.wasm` (252 KB, 60 KB gzip) is a separate on-demand asset; `db/scripts/lib/plan.test.mjs` 8 tests green (`node --test`).
- DB: `m45` re-applied twice (nothing changes); **v45 52/52**, v41 47/47; the whole suite after cleanup: v01, v02, v03 (133), v10, v11 (182), v24 48, v25 22, v30 23, v31 48, v32 28, v33 62, v34 61, v35 85, v36 73, v37 66, v38 99, v39 52, v40 107, v41 47, v42 120, v43 103, v44 63, v45 52: all green (v01 / v10 only fail while a throwaway user / rows exist, as before).
- **Browser, `/dev/image-check`** (Chrome 152 in the pane; own tab): green in English with every probe "Works" (native WebP, WebAssembly WebP 28 - 112 ms first run / 2 ms warm, native JPEG, progressive WebAssembly JPEG 27 ms / 5 ms); a 3000 x 2000 JPEG with EXIF orientation 6 -> "Orientation correct", decoded 2000 x 3000, WebP 1280 x 1920 50 KB (-78%), JPEG 1200 x 630 38 KB progressive, 537 ms in total; the developer switch ("pretend the browser cannot make WebP") -> verdict yellow, "WebP with: the WebAssembly fallback", a 4000 x 3000 4.2 MB JPEG -> 1920 x 1440 144 KB (-97%), JPEG 66 KB, 956 ms in total, report "Encoder used: WebP wasm x2; JPEG wasm x1"; a text file named `.png` -> "Refused: Only PNG, JPEG, WebP or GIF images are accepted" in the page and the report; Arabic RTL at 375 px (mobile emulation, user agent parsed as Android 14): no horizontal overflow (`scrollWidth` 375), results, previews and report readable; no console errors.
- **Browser, uploads** (throwaway super_admin + program + project + form node, signed in): **program cover** (Settings, 4000 x 3000 JPEG of 4.2 MB) -> field text "Optimised: 4.2 MB -> 144 KB (-97%) / Link preview (JPEG 1200 x 630): 66 KB / Resized to 1920 x 1440 px", after Save: `public-assets/<program>/cover/<uuid>.webp` 147,906 B + `_t.webp` 7,250 B (image/webp) + `og-images/<program>/cover/<uuid>.jpg` 67,604 B (image/jpeg); replacing the previous cover removed its WebP, thumbnail and JPEG (`og_orphans` = 0); **form share image** (Settings -> Preview image, a transparent 1200 x 630 PNG of 16 KB) -> "Optimised: 16 KB -> 8.6 KB (-47%) / Link preview (JPEG 1200 x 630): 14 KB", stored as WebP 8,762 B + thumbnail 3,190 B + JPEG 14,134 B; the node config holds the `.webp` path; an anonymous GET of the og-images URL answered **HTTP 200 `image/jpeg`** (bytes `FF D8 FF E0`); `og_missing_refs()`, `og_orphans` and `storage_dangling_refs()` all 0.
- **`lan-dev.ps1`**: `-DryRun` prints the adapter, the LAN address (reachable Supabase), the phone URLs and the firewall commands; a real start on a spare port served `/dev/image-check` with HTTP 200 and `VITE_SUPABASE_URL` set to the LAN address for that process only, listening on all interfaces; `npm run dev:lan -- -DryRun` passes the flag through. (A first version started Vite through `npx` and failed when PowerShell had no console: it now runs `node node_modules/vite/bin/vite.js`.)
- **Baseline after cleanup**: 12 / 12 / 12 users / profiles / roles, 4 programs, 16 projects, 82 nodes, 53 edges, 141 companies, 3 founders, 0 company_founders, 44 program_members, 0 project_members, 612 list_items, 0 participants / certificates / notifications / opportunities / alumni_invitations / staff_invitations / outbox rows, 384 responses, 30 bookings, 33 public_links, 82 node_revisions: **no delta**. **Intentional delta: 371 storage objects (was 373)**, per bucket in the table above; `app_private.image_conversions` holds the 133 audit rows. The throwaway program, project, 2 nodes, user, profile / role / identity / session, 2 founders and the 9 throwaway storage objects (removed through the Storage API) were deleted and a generic FK orphan scan over `public` / `app_private` found 0 orphan groups; the browser origin's `sb-*` keys were cleared, the dev server and the helper servers stopped, the temporary viewport reset, my tab closed.

### Deviations (and why)
- N1. `--apply` now converts EVERY raster (the owner's decision), not only the referenced ones as m41 did; the sibling keeps the original's base name in the same folder (`<base>.webp`, a collision puts the old extension in the name) instead of a new uuid, so paths stay recognisable. `event-assets` / `migration-archive` get no `_t.webp` (frozen archives, nothing displays them).
- N2. The m41 dry run said "0 referenced by current data": it did not scan `migration.asset_result.archive_path`. The 57 `migration-archive` originals ARE referenced there; m45 rewrites it (exact match only) and proves it resolves. The provenance URLs of the old project are deliberately not rewritten.
- N3. Two files were not images (18 bytes of text and an empty file named `.png`): removed as `undecodable_removed` (sha256 in the manifest), not converted.
- N4. The JPEG derivative exists only for images stored in `public-assets` (shared images); never for the private buckets. A transparent source gets a white background (a JPEG has no alpha).
- N5. The browser makes the progressive JPEG with a 60 KB-gzip WebAssembly module (loaded on the first shared-image upload of a session, in every browser); the canvas encoder (baseline) is only the fallback. A smaller bundle would be the canvas encoder alone: the owner's "progressive, ~82" brief won.
- N6. `lib/env.js` classifies private IPv4 addresses as local (needed for the phone run); a public host stays remote.
- N7. Found and fixed while closing this work: the converter listed the `og-images` JPEGs as rasters (a dry run after the backfill would have proposed converting them, `--delete-originals` would have refused each as "not converted yet"); `isConvertible` in `db/scripts/lib/plan.mjs` now excludes the bucket (unit-tested). The dev page's test picture drew on a canvas inside the page, which the guard test forbids (no canvas encoding outside `lib/image/`): moved to `lib/image/testImage.js`.

### Open questions for the owner
1. **Please run the iPhone / Safari check** (guide in `web/README.md`) and send the copied report: it is the only thing not provable from here (the fallback is proven in Chrome through the developer switch and the unit tests, not on WebKit).
2. The 57 `migration-archive` originals are gone from storage (their WebP copies, <= 1600 px, stay); they can be fetched again from the Google URLs in `migration.asset_map` while those files remain shared. Is the 1600 px WebP enough, or do you want them fetched again at full size before the live run?
3. A cover replaced through a node's draft (the form share image, landing images) is not removed from storage when the draft is saved: the form Settings upload at pick time and keep the old file (pre-existing, m41). Its JPEG derivative follows its source. A small orphan sweeper (sources + derivatives) would close it: wanted?
4. When the live project gets m45, run the converter against a restored copy first and keep the manifest; the originals of `event-assets` are on live, so nothing there is lost by deleting locally.

### Handover
- **A new sharing purpose** (a page whose link preview shows an image): upload it with a kind that maps to `cover` / `landing` (or give a new kind `og: true` profile in `lib/image/profiles.js` `KIND_PROFILE`) and add its reference to `app_private.og_source_refs()` in a new migration, so `og_missing_refs()` and the backfill cover it.
- **The share / OG function**: resolve with the recipe above; never emit the WebP; fall back to a generic card. The bucket `og-images` and `public.og_image_path` exist only after m45 (live: m41, m45, converter, backfill).
- **Converter maintenance**: `db/scripts/lib/plan.mjs` is the one place that knows what a raster is, which buckets hold JPEG on purpose (`JPEG_BUCKETS`), and the policy by folder kind (mirror of `profiles.js`: keep them in step).
- **The diagnostics page** reuses the pipeline only; adding a profile to `PROFILES` in `DevImageCheckPage.jsx` and a key in `devImageCheck.i18n.js` is all a new profile needs. `window.__forceNoNativeWebp` / `__forceNativeJpeg` are dev-only switches.
- **Gotchas found**: `npx` inside a PowerShell started without a console can lose its first argument (use `node node_modules/vite/bin/vite.js`); `node --test <directory>` does not work on this Windows Node (name the file); the shared Browser pane returns "screenshot timed out" while another agent drives it (re-select your tab and retry); a `.wasm?url` import of `@jsquash/jpeg` is emitted by Vite as its own hashed asset (the `locateFile` override of `wasmJpeg.js` points the codec at it); a heredoc through the tool layer eats backslashes in JS strings (write scripts with the Write tool).

## Alumni profile model (m49 + v49, 2026-10-14; owner decisions 08 sections 18.7, 18.8, 19): done, deviations, open questions, handover

**Scope**: the shared DATA MODEL and contract for the missing Alumni pages (founder side + manager side), driven by the owner's 38 Excel columns. No page was built; the Athar Network pages stay postponed. Design: `docs/design/09-alumni-pages-and-profile-model.md` (inventory of the pages old / new / missing, the crosswalk of every Excel column, the national-ID rules, vocabularies, yearly metrics, privacy matrix, RPC reference, bulk-import design, builder contract, open questions). Contract for the builders: `web/src/data/alumni.contract.md`.

### Done
- **DB** (`db/migrations/m49_alumni_profile_model.sql`, `db/verify/v49_alumni_profile_model.sql`; details in `db/MIGRATION-LOG.md`): the national ID is stored ONCE (`founder_identities`, unique, no API grant); birth date / age / age category / gender / governorate of origin are derived by ten IMMUTABLE `app_private.id_*` functions (no checksum) and never stored; the ID wins over typed values (a CHECK forbids a second copy); least-privilege access (founder himself + alumni_manager + super_admin, through RPCs; masked by default, audited reveal and export); 13 typed company columns + the manager-only notes table (Hint, veto, review) + bilingual manager-editable vocabularies (127 seeds) + typed yearly metric rows in `company_snapshots`; Program Scope / Program Name / Support provided and the three founder counts are DERIVED; 25 RPCs incl. the directory (manager, member, public), the export in the owner's exact 38-column order and the analytics; the BEFORE trigger on companies guards every writer; non-destructive backfill (114 governorate codes, 98 sector keys from the existing text; `is_alumni` untouched).
- **Client contract** (no pages): `web/src/data/alumni.js` (one function per RPC + hooks + `alumniKeys`, pinned by `alumni.test.js`: a drift between the SQL and the client fails a test), `web/src/lib/alumni.js` (pure helpers: `digitsOnly` (formatting only), `lookupLabel`, `lookupOptions`, `subsectorOptions`, `parseAlumniError`, `diffPatch`, `completenessTone`, `missingYears`), `web/src/data/alumni.contract.md` (shapes, error tokens, shared UI blocks, file ownership).

### Verification (local stack only; no browser needed for this step)
- `db/verify/v49_alumni_profile_model.sql`: **320/320**; m49 applied several times (re-runs change 0 rows); the whole suite v01 .. v45 re-run green (v01, v02, v10 now accept m49; v03 133, v11 182, v24 48, v25 22, v30 23, v31 48, v32 28, v33 62, v34 61, v35 85, v36 73, v37 66, v38 99, v39 52, v40 107, v41 47, v42 120, v43 103, v44 63, v45 52). One real PostgREST smoke test (anon): `list_alumni_lookups` returns the 14 keys (28 governorates, 18 sectors) and `list_public_alumni_directory` returns an empty public page.
- `npm run lint` clean; `npx vitest run` **80 files, 876 tests, all green** (new: `lib/alumni.test.js` 9, `data/alumni.test.js` 34); `npm run build` ok (the new modules are not imported by any page yet: no bundle change).
- **Baseline after cleanup**: 12 / 12 / 12 users / profiles / roles, 4 programs, 16 projects, 82 nodes, 53 edges, 141 companies, 3 founders, 0 company_founders, 44 program_members, 612 list_items, 0 participants / certificates / notifications / opportunities / alumni_invitations / company_snapshots. **Intended deltas**: +3 public tables (`alumni_lookups` 127 rows, `founder_identities` 0, `company_internal` 0), +2 `app_private` tables (empty), 114 `companies.governorate_code` and 98 `companies.sector_key` filled from existing text, +25 public RPCs / +49 `app_private` helpers (all closed to the API roles). (The first counts I took showed 13 users / 5 programs / 17 projects / 84 nodes: another session's throwaway rows, gone later.)

### Deviations (and why)
- A1. `company_snapshots` becomes typed yearly rows (design 04 kept it as jsonb "to be decided"): aggregation, CHECKs and speed; the old `data jsonb` / `form_response_id` stay for legacy rows. A PM of a participating project no longer reads snapshots (financials are private by default).
- A2. The identity, notes and vocabulary tables have NO API privilege (RPC only) instead of per-role policies: nothing can leak through PostgREST `select=*`.
- A3. Invitations get create / revoke / list RPCs (the table existed, the RPC did not) but NO e-mail is queued: the outbox worker does not exist; the manager copies the link (same as staff invitations).
- A4. "Today" for ages and the "not in the future" rule is the date in Cairo, not UTC.
- A5. Program Scope = the program(s), Program Name = the project(s), Support provided = derived from the project's node types: an INTERPRETATION (open question 1 of design 09); both columns are derived, so changing the meaning is a one-line change in the export.
- A6. A founder cannot enter a national ID for another person (co-founder records carry only the self-declared fallback); only a manager can. Keeps "the ID is readable only by its owner and the managers" true.
- A7. `preview_national_id` and every RPC that takes or returns an ID are VOLATILE on purpose (POST only, never a URL).
- A9. KNOWN LIMITS (independent review, accepted for now, registry is ~141 companies): the per-company payload helpers and the anonymous public directory still materialise all founders / participations per call (restrict by company / skip for non-manager audiences before the registry reaches thousands); a team record linked to two companies is editable by managers only (fixed in review).
- A8. A manager's note alone does not bump `companies.updated_at` (the founders' optimistic-concurrency token).

### Open questions for the owner (the full list is section 15 of design 09)
1. Program Scope / Program Name / Support provided: confirm the interpretation (program / project / derived node kinds), or give the real meaning.
2. Age buckets (under 18, 18-24, 25-34, 35-44, 45+); sector / sub sector / stage / startup type / legal status values: send the distinct values of those Excel columns to replace the seeds; one sector + one sub sector per company enough?
3. Currency (EGP, funding cumulative to the year end); history of yearly edits (who / when / source only).
4. Mark all 141 migrated companies as alumni now? (`set_companies_alumni`, one call.)
5. Prefilling the national ID from the archived legacy values (60 numbers can be tied to an e-mail; none matches an account today): approve, restrict or drop. Not executed.
6. Linking a founder record created from the Excel to the later account (by invitation or a manager merge tool); a public directory at all.

### Handover (the two builders)
- Read `web/src/data/alumni.contract.md` first, then design 09 sections 1.2 and 14. Founder-side builder: F1 - F9, creates `src/shared/alumni/*` first; manager-side builder: M1 - M10. Both: pages never call `rpc()` directly, forms send patches (`diffPatch`), labels come from the lookups, derived values are read only, the national ID is typed in ONE component and never leaves a POST body.
- **Gotchas found**: (1) **a role-switched call of a function the role cannot execute crashes this local Postgres** (the default ACL gives EXECUTE to service_role only, so every new function needs an explicit grant, and tests must call `app_private` helpers as the owner); (2) `String.replace` with a `$$` in the replacement turns it into `$` (I broke a migration once: use split / join or a function); (3) a scalar subquery in the SAME statement as a volatile call does not see its writes (split the check from the call); (4) `jsonb ->>` and `||` have the same precedence (parenthesise `(x->>'a') || '|'`); (5) a check on a boolean through `concat_ws` prints `t` / `f`; (6) heredocs above ~6 KB are cut by the tool: write long test files with the file writer; (7) a CHECK constraint that calls an `app_private` function needs EXECUTE for the WRITING role (it only works because every identity write goes through SECURITY DEFINER code): keep CHECKs on directly writable tables to built-in functions and use triggers for lookups.
- **Not covered** (ask the lead for the next migration number): server-side notification broadcast, e-mail delivery of invitations, `merge_founders`, a server-side Excel import (designed in section 13 of design 09), a `profile_reviewed` history.

## Founder-side Alumni pages (m50 + v50, 2026-10-14; design 09 F1 - F9, owner decisions 18.7 / 18.8 / 19): done, deviations, open questions, handover

**Scope**: the missing FOUNDER pages on top of the m49 contract: profile (with the national ID), company, progress, notifications, opportunities, the open sign-up and the invitation page, the Home cards and the shell badge. The manager pages are another builder's (`alumni-admin/`); nothing of theirs was touched.

### Done
- **DB** (`db/migrations/m50_alumni_founder_pages.sql`, `db/verify/v50_alumni_founder_pages.sql`, **42 checks**, rolled back; re-runnable; run AFTER m49): (1) `validate_invitation` returns three ADDITIVE keys (`inviter_name`, `expires_at`, `company_name_en` / `_ar`: "who invited you"); the old keys and the anon grant are unchanged. (2) `opportunity_applications` BEFORE INSERT guard: the RLS policy only checked ownership, so a founder could insert `status = 'accepted'`, apply to an inactive / expired opportunity or write a huge note; for a non-manager the guard forces `status = 'applied'`, the date and the founder id, refuses a closed / inactive opportunity (`opportunity_closed`, deadline day inclusive, Cairo date) and a note over 1000 characters. (3) `notifications` BEFORE UPDATE guard: a reader can only change `is_read` (a manager's text and `metadata` link cannot be rewritten by its reader). (4) **`alumni_stale` is now raised with SQLSTATE P0001, not 40001** (see deviation B1: PostgREST 14 retries a 40001 for ever).
- **Routes** (`app/routes.jsx`): `/signup`, `/invite/:token`, `/alumni/founder/{profile, company/new, company/:id, progress, notifications, opportunities}` replace their ComingSoon lines; the Login page gets a "Create a founder account" link. The founder shell has 5 bottom tabs (Home, Programs, **Opportunities**, **Notifications with the unread badge**, **Profile**; Progress and the Company page are reached from Home / Profile) and its own tiny data module (`data/founderNotifications.js`: the badge is one HEAD count, refetched on focus and once a minute; a failure hides the badge).
- **Pages** (`app/pages/founder/*`, `SignupPage`, `FounderInvitePage`; all lazy chunks; gzip sizes: Profile 6.5 kB, Company 7.1, Progress 4.0, Opportunities 4.3, Notifications 2.7, Sign-up 1.5, Invite 2.3, the Home cards 2.5 inside the Home chunk, each shared block 3 - 4 kB, one small dictionary per page): **Profile** (photo, names ar / en, e-mail read-only, phone, student / graduate, the national ID, bio, LinkedIn, the founder's companies with the role in each, completeness meter with jump-to-section chips, sectioned form, dirty state, explicit Save bar, Undo, unsaved-changes guard, welcome banners for self-registered and invited founders); **Company** (public-style card, the 6 form sections for every owner column assigned to the founder, team with CEO / records / derived counts, co-founder invitation link, yearly figures table + trend charts, DERIVED programs with support chips; a stale-save banner that re-applies my edits on the fresh values); **Create your company** (`/company/new`); **Notifications** (All / Unread, mark one / all, open the linked item, empty states, show older); **Opportunities** (filters by type / state / search, deadline chips, apply with a company + note, status of each application, `?open=<id>` from a notification); **Progress** (7 milestones from the server's own completeness and lists + programs + certificates + per-company trend; nothing invented); **Home cards** (completeness, the ONE next step, "yearly figures due", unread count, shortcuts); **Sign-up** and **Invitation** (token validation, who invited, sign-up or sign-in, a check of the company link after sign-up, the staff / founder exclusivity message, a "created but not linked" outcome).
- **Shared blocks** `src/shared/alumni/*` (created first, as the contract says; barrel `index.js`): `useDraftForm` (draft + Undo + rebase + uploaded-file tracking), `SaveBar`, `UnsavedGuard` (ConfirmDialog on an in-app navigation, `beforeunload`; a no-op under a plain MemoryRouter), `useAlumniRefresh`, `useAlumniErrorText` / `useAlumniFieldErrors`, `ChipSelect`, `LookupSelect`, `GovernoratePicker`, `SectorPicker`, `useLookupOptions`, **`NationalIdField`**, `DerivedValue`, `ProfileSection`, `CompletenessMeter`, `scrollToSection`, `CompanyBasicsSection` / `Classification` / `Location` / `Online` / `Status`, `DirectoryVisibilitySection`, `TeamEditor`, `ProgramsReadOnlyList`, `MetricsTable` (+ the per-year dialog), `TrendBars` / `TrendChart`, `useMetricFormat`. Not built (the manager builder's): `DirectoryFilters`, `CompanyCard` / `CompanyRow`, `exportToXlsx`.
- **Pure logic** (`src/lib`): `nationalId.js` (instant ID feedback), `alumniForms.js` (payload <-> form, patches, instant validation with the SAME reason vocabulary as the server, the section of every completeness key, refusal -> field), `alumniFounder.js` (notification target, opportunity filters / state), `alumniProgress.js` (milestones, completeness, yearly series / change / bar ratios), `alumniSignup.js`. Data: `data/founderNotifications.js`, `data/alumniFounder.js` (opportunities, applications, photo + logo uploads through `data/storage.js`, the signed photo URL), `signUpFounder` in `data/auth.js`, the alumni error tokens in `data/errors.js` (+ `P0002` -> `not_found`).
- **i18n**: ar + en everywhere, ONE small dictionary per page / block (`alumni.profile.i18n.js`, `.company.`, `.notifications.`, `.opportunities.`, `.progress.`, `.home.`, `alumniAuth.i18n.js`, `shared/alumni/i18n.js` core + `.id.` + `.company.`), `nav.unreadCount` / `nav.profileShort` added to the central dictionaries. A test proves ar / en parity, placeholders, plural categories, that every literal key exists and that EVERY FILE imports the dictionaries of the keys it uses (a page never relies on another page having loaded its texts).

### The national ID, exactly as the owner asked (decision 19)
- Typed in ONE place (`NationalIdField`): masked by default (the browser's text security, not `type=password`, which would offer to autofill / save the account password) with a reveal toggle, digits only (Arabic-Indic digits accepted), `autocomplete=off`, never in a URL, query key, log, toast or storage (checked in the browser: no hit in resource URLs, `location`, `localStorage`, `sessionStorage`; every call is a POST). A browser without `-webkit-text-security` would show the digits to their owner (Chromium, Edge and Safari mask).
- **Instant client validation with one clear ar / en reason per failure** (`lib/nationalId.js`: century at digit 1, the date at digit 7, the governorate at digit 9, the length above 14, the future against the Cairo date), pinned to the database by the appendix-A vectors and v49 U07 - U13 in `nationalId.test.js`. The governorate codes come from the vocabulary payload, not from the client. **No derived value is computed in the browser**: at 14 valid digits `preview_national_id` (the same SQL as the database) answers and the read-only "Derived from your national ID" card shows date of birth, age, gender and governorate of origin, each with a "From your national ID" badge. A saved ID is shown masked (`**********1234`); "Show" reveals the owner's own number.
- The typed date of birth / gender exist ONLY while there is no ID, flagged "Self-declared"; they are never sent together with an ID (the patch builder drops them, the server erases them and reports `replaced`, shown as "you typed X, your ID says Y"). A number that belongs to somebody else = a soft-failure message; every refusal names its reason; "Change number" / "Remove number" are part of the draft (Undo restores the saved one).

### Verification (local stack; dev server port 5424, own tab, throwaway accounts only)
- `npm run lint`: clean for everything of this task. `npx vitest run`: **104 files, 1226 tests, all green** (new: 196 in 9 files: nationalId 14, alumniProgress 11, alumniForms + alumniFounder 42, data/alumniFounder 18, shared components 42, i18n 6, founder pages 45, sign-up / invitation 18). `npm run build` ok. v50 **42/42**; v02, v03, v11, v24 - v45 and v51 green; **v01, v10 and v49 fail ONLY while the other builder's throwaway rows are in the database** (auth.users 16, extra companies / founders / identities; v49 C12 + its fixtures use the same synthetic IDs): they are the baseline-count and "no identity" checks, not code.
- Browser, end to end (`127.0.0.1` and `localhost` give two independent sessions): **invited founder** (an invitation made by the RPC as a super_admin) -> `/invite/:token` shows the company, the inviter and the masked e-mail -> sign-up -> linked to the PRE-EXISTING company (not as primary) -> profile edit: names, phone normalised to E.164, student / graduate, role, the ID -> Save persists (checked in the database; the ID is stored once, in `founder_identities`) -> company edit (sector / sub sector / stage / location / website / app / social link / description / date) -> completeness 30 % -> 100 % -> yearly figures (two years) with a trend -> a team record (counts 2 / 1 / 1, derived) -> a company participation shows the DERIVED program with its support chips and the founder program page still opens. **Second founder** (an invitation made by the first one with the co-founder form) joins the same company and sees the same shared data, none of the first one's private values (the "private" note, no age) and no manager-only key. **Self-registered founder** (`/signup`): welcome banner, "create your company" CTA, a duplicate name refused without naming the owner, creation lands on the company page with the created banner. **Wrong e-mail on an invitation**: the account is created and the page says it is not linked. **Staff session** (a throwaway PM account) on an invitation: the exclusivity explanation and sign-out only. **RLS** (curl with the founder's own token): another company read / edit / snapshot = 403, `founder_identities` = permission denied, other founders' notifications = empty, forging an application for another company = RLS refusal. **Stale save** (a second writer): banner, "Reload and keep my changes", save again keeps both edits. **Uploads**: a logo and a founder photo through the WebP pipeline (6.9 KB -> 2.9 KB, 6 KB -> 2.2 KB), stored under the founder's own folders with their `_t` thumbnails, accepted by the `logo_path` / `photo_path` checks, the photo shown by a signed URL; Undo of an unsaved upload removes the files again. Notifications (badge 2 -> 1 -> none, mark read / all, internal + external links) and Opportunities (filters, apply, status, expired hidden) verified.
- Screenshots (headless Edge through CDP, DPR 2, 375 px phone and desktop, ar and en): `web/design-system/applied/alumni-founder-*.png` (26 files: profile, ID typing + derived card, ID error in Arabic, saved ID, no company, save bar, company card / classification / team / figures / programs, create company, notifications, opportunities, progress, Home, invitation, sign-up, login).
- **Baseline** after cleanup: every row I created is gone (5 accounts + profiles / roles, 2 companies, 5 founder rows incl. 1 record without an account, 4 identities, 4 invitations, 4 snapshots, 4 notifications, 5 opportunities, applications, 1 participation, probe-limiter rows; the 4 storage objects were deleted through the Storage API, `storage.objects` is back to 371). The remaining differences from the baseline I recorded (auth.users 16, companies 146, founders 7, identities 4, list_items 617, nodes 88, programs 5, projects 18) belong to the manager builder's parallel throwaway data. Intended delta of mine: **m50 only** (2 functions + 2 triggers + 2 patched functions, no rows).

### Deviations (and why)
- **B1. `alumni_stale` is raised with P0001, not 40001** (the contract and design 09 section 11 say 40001: they need the one-word update). Found in the browser: **PostgREST 14.7 retries a transaction that fails with 40001 until it succeeds**, so a stale save made the request hang and three PostgREST connections spin in a BEGIN / call / error loop on the database (I had to terminate them). Reproduced with a throwaway function: P0001 / 40002 / 22023 answer at once, 40001 never does. m50 patches `save_my_company_profile` and `save_alumni_company` in place (the hint `alumni_stale` is unchanged: the client keys on the token), v49 E26 / Z03 expect P0001, v50 C04 / S01 - S03 pin it. **Not fixed here (flagged to the lead; a task chip was created): m06b `save_node` raises its revision conflict with 40001 too**, so a stale node save probably hangs the same way.
- B2. **Instant client-side ID validation exists** although the contract says "no JavaScript rule of the ID": the lead's task asked for it. It only gives feedback (same reasons, parity-tested), derives nothing, and the server stays the authority.
- B3. **Opportunities are not "targeted"**: the as-is `opportunities` table has no audience column, so every founder sees the same active list (filters: type, open / applied / all, free text). An application is a plain insert under RLS + the m50 guard (no RPC): a company applies once (unique), the founder cannot withdraw it (managers own the status).
- B4. **Bottom bar: 5 tabs** (Opportunities added; Progress and Company from Home / Profile); the Profile tab says "ملفي" in Arabic (the full word does not fit 75 px in bold).
- B5. The **role in a company** is edited on the Profile page (one Save: it calls `save_company_team_member` with the role only) and in the team editor; a registered co-founder's details are read-only for the others (server rule), records without an account are editable / removable by any member.
- B6. **Uploads happen when the file is picked**, the path is attached on Save; an abandoned or undone upload is removed (best effort). Logo = the `avatar` profile (no SVG: the server path rule is WebP only).
- B7. **Co-founder invitation = a link to copy** (shown once); no e-mail is sent (the outbox worker does not exist).
- B8. **Sign-up**: the CAPTCHA is only a hook (`signUpFounder({ captchaToken })`, no widget: 03 OD-5); e-mail confirmation is OFF on this local stack (a session comes back), the page handles "no session" with a "check your e-mail" screen; on the live project the setting must be ON (design 09 open question 10).
- B9. An invitation links only the INVITED e-mail (the trigger's rule): sign-up with another address creates an unlinked account, and the page says so.

### Open questions for the owner
1. A co-founder typed as a RECORD by the lead and later registered with an invitation is two `founders` rows (the counts double) until a manager merges them (`merge_founders` does not exist: design 09 open question 8).
2. Should a founder be able to withdraw an application, and should opportunities get an audience (sector / stage / program) so they can be targeted?
3. Notification links: the founder page follows `metadata.link` / `metadata.url` (an in-app path or an https address), `metadata.opportunity_id`, `metadata.project_id`. The manager builder should write one of these when a notification is about something (agree before the broadcast page ships).
4. Sector / sub sector / stage values are still the seeds (design 09 open question 3); the pages show whatever the vocabulary holds.
5. The e-mail of an invitation (valid 14 days) and a welcome e-mail need the outbox worker.

### Handover
- **Reuse**: `useDraftForm` + `SaveBar` + `UnsavedGuard` for any long form (the patch is `build*Patch(baseForm, values)` of `lib/alumniForms.js`; both sides of the diff are FORM-shaped); `NationalIdField` takes the founder block's `identity` (own payload: the full number is there; manager payload: `id_masked` only, plus `onReveal`); `useAlumniRefresh()` when a page calls the data functions directly (to show its own error text: `useAlumniMutations` toasts every failure globally); `MetricsTable` shows delete only when `onDelete` is given (managers); the company sections take `{ values, set, setMany, errors, disabled }`.
- **Gotchas found**: (1) **PostgREST 14 retries 40001 for ever** (B1): never raise an application error with 40001 / 40P01. (2) The shared Browser pane stops compositing when the window is minimised (screenshots come back white or time out and timers are throttled): the screenshots here were taken with a headless Edge over CDP (a 60-line Node script using the built-in `WebSocket`, `--remote-debugging-port`, a session written to `localStorage['sb-127-auth-token']` after a GoTrue password sign-in): no extra dependency. (3) The manager builder and I used the same synthetic IDs of design 09 appendix A: while their fixtures were in the database my "valid" ID answered "already registered" (use other valid numbers: any `2` / `3`, a real date, a listed governorate, a serial); v49 fails the same way. (4) Vite HMR does a full reload when another agent edits a shared file (a form with unsaved edits is lost: do click-throughs quickly). (5) `Intl` currency output uses a no-break space (`EGP 260,000`): assert with `\s` or `.`. (6) `aria-labelledby` on a section makes it match `getByLabelText` with the section's title: query a field by role + name. (7) My tool layer collapses doubled backslashes and turns a `\u` escape in file contents into the character: write regexes with single backslashes and character-code ranges.
- **Not covered**: a server-side notification broadcast, the invitation e-mail, `merge_founders`, a founder-side directory page (F10: the RPC exists, no route), the public directory.

## Manager-side Alumni pages (m51 + v51, 2026-10-14; design 09 M1 - M10, owner decisions 18.7 / 18.8 / 19): done, deviations, open questions, handover

**Scope**: the missing MANAGER pages of the Alumni area (`alumni_manager` / `super_admin`) on top of the m49 contract and the m50 founder side: Dashboard, Directory (+ company drawer, company page, Excel import check), Invitations, Notifications, Opportunities, Analytics / Impact, the M&E workspace. Certificates are NOT rebuilt (the existing certificates area keeps its place; the company page links to it). The founder pages and `shared/alumni/*` belong to the other builder: only reused, never changed.

### Done
- **DB** (`db/migrations/m51_alumni_manager_pages.sql`, `db/verify/v51_alumni_manager_pages.sql`, **131 checks**, rolled back; re-runnable; run AFTER m49 + m50; details in `db/MIGRATION-LOG.md`): `list_alumni_programs`, `list_alumni_table` (all 38 owner columns as typed values, server sort + paging, the CEO's ID masked), `get_alumni_overview`, `preview_notification_audience` / `send_alumni_notification` (signature changed: + `p_link`, `p_opportunity`) / `list_alumni_broadcasts` (audience resolved on the server), opportunities (provider, archive, **target** program / sector / stage / location enforced by RLS for founders, closed application-status vocabulary, a manager may apply on behalf), `import_alumni_preview` (dry run), the manager-only filter `education_status`.
- **Routes** (`app/routes.jsx`, all lazy chunks): `/alumni/admin/{dashboard, directory, directory/import, invitations, notifications, opportunities, analytics, impact, workspace, company/:id}` replace the "coming soon" lines. `NetworkShell`: 7 links (Dashboard, Directory, Invitations, Opportunities, Notifications, Analytics, M&E); Impact is a switch inside Analytics.
- **Pages** (`app/pages/alumni-admin/*`, one small `*.i18n.js` dictionary per page, ar + en, loaded with the page; gzip: Dashboard 5.0 kB, Directory 7.7, Invitations 7.4, Notifications 6.9, Opportunities 8.2, Analytics 7.1, Workspace 7.5, Company 7.5, Import 7.3, Excel export dialog 7.2, the manager block of the company page 6.0; the xlsx library (143 kB gzip) is loaded only when somebody exports or imports; the entry chunk is 12.5 kB / 4.7 gzip after the m52 round, see the m52 section for the exact before / after): **Dashboard** (real KPIs, a "needs attention" list that links to the filtered directory, recent invitations / notifications, charts as accessible lists), **Directory** (the owner's 38 columns + 6 extras, a column chooser and saved views kept per viewer, every filter in the URL (incl. **company location vs CEO governorate read from the national ID**), server paging / sort, a company drawer with the hint note, the review stamp and the derived values, "Export to Excel" in the owner's EXACT header order, ar / en labels), **Invitations** (one or several at once from pasted lines / CSV, the link is shown ONCE and copied, resend, revoke with a confirmation, statuses with counts, link to the company), **Notifications** (all / program (+ project) / companies / selected founders, a server-counted audience before sending, plain text in both languages with a live preview, optional link or opportunity, history with read counts and the recipients), **Opportunities** (CRUD, draft / publish / archive / delete, targeting, applications drawer with the status workflow, apply on behalf, Excel export), **Analytics** and **Impact** (every number from `get_alumni_analytics`: filters, year, KPIs, breakdowns, yearly totals; a year nobody reported is a dash, never a zero; Excel export; no invented figure), **M&E workspace** (yearly grid: only the changed cells are saved, one row per company and year, notes, a history drawer, the owner's Excel export), **Import** (the owner's sheet is checked in this browser: headers, vocabulary that matches nothing, a plan per row; nothing is written), **Company page** (reuses the founder's sections and `shared/alumni/*`, the manager block: alumni flag, directory veto, review stamp, private hint, team, snapshots, invitations of the company, merge).
- **Shared edits (additive, minimal)**: `ui/Modal.jsx` `side` prop (a drawer from the end edge); `shared/SiteHeader.jsx` `collapseAt` prop and a compact `.nav-link-inverse` at `xl` in `styles/index.css` (seven links only fit on one line from 1280 px; below that the burger opens); `app/shells/NetworkShell.jsx`; `i18n/dict.*` (`nav.directory`, `nav.workspace`, `nav.impact`); `app/routes.jsx`.
- **Logic / data** (pure and tested): `data/alumniAdmin.js` (every RPC wrapper + the direct table functions + hooks, pinned by `alumniAdmin.test.js`), `alumni-admin/{adminLogic, adminState, columns, exportXlsx}.js`.

### The national ID on the manager side (decision 19)
- The Directory shows the CEO's **governorate, age, gender and age category read from the ID** (a badge says so), the typed company location is its own column and its own filter ("Company location" vs "Governorate (from national ID)"): a company in Assiut whose CEO's ID says Cairo is found by the second filter only (checked in the browser).
- The number itself: masked everywhere (`**********1234`), a reveal button calls the audited `get_alumni_founder` (30 seconds, hidden again on "Hide" / unmount, never cached, never in a URL, query key, log or toast); the Excel export carries it (the dialog says so; the export is logged: who / when / how many rows, never the numbers).

- `npx eslint .` clean; `npx vitest run` **104 files, 1230 tests, all green** (new for this task: `alumni-admin/*.test.{js,jsx}` 14 files + `data/alumniAdmin.test.js`: logic, columns, URL state, a real xlsx round trip that proves the header order, every page through the REAL data layer with a fake client); `npm run build` ok; v51 **131/131**.
