### Deviations (and why)
- M-A1. The Excel header is "Date of company established" (design 09, line 221); the owner's typo "stabilished" is accepted by the import and printed corrected by the export.
- M-A2. **No vocabulary page**: the lookups are managed through the existing `save_alumni_lookup` / `delete_alumni_lookup` RPCs and the import proposes new values; a page for them was not in the M1 - M10 list.
- M-A3. **Notifications**: no scheduling, no attachments (a link or an opportunity only), plain text only, at most 5000 recipients per send, a sent notification cannot be recalled (the page says so before sending).
- M-A4. **Invitation e-mail is not queued** (no outbox worker): the manager copies the link; a link cannot be shown again, "Send a new link" replaces the pending one.
- M-A5. **The import has no commit step** (a visible hook): it waits for the owner's decision on how a founder written in the sheet is linked to his later account (design 09 open question 8).
- M-A6. `send_alumni_notification` changed signature (the 5-argument overload is dropped): nothing else called it. `opportunities` gained `target` (founders now see only the opportunities that target them, or everybody's): this changes B3 of the founder-side notes (no audience) on purpose.
- M-A7. `alumni_filter_ids` (m49) was patched in place for `education_status` (the same technique as m49 / m43): re-run m51 after any re-run of m49.
- M-A8. Shared edits: `Modal` got a `side` prop, `SiteHeader` a `collapseAt` prop (the manager nav leaves the burger at 1280 px instead of 1024 px), `.nav-link-inverse` is compact at `xl`, `NetworkShell` is `max-w-[1360px]`.

### Open questions for the owner
1. The owner's Excel vocabulary: the distinct values of Sector / Sub sector / Stage / Startup type / Legal status (the seeds are still in use; the import check lists what matches nothing).
2. The import commit rule (design 09 question 8): link a founder written in the sheet to the later account by invitation or by a manager merge tool.
3. Program Scope / Program Name / Support provided: the interpretation of m49 (program / project / derived node types) is still unconfirmed.
4. Which of the 141 migrated companies are alumni (`set_companies_alumni` is one call; today the registry shows only what a manager marked).
5. History of yearly edits: who / when / source only is kept; is a full audit of every changed figure needed?
6. Should the notification audience also allow "alumni of a sector / stage" like an opportunity (today: all, a program or project, companies, selected founders)?

### Handover
- **Reuse**: `alumni-admin/ui.jsx` (`AdminPage`, `SectionCard`, `KpiCard`, `Segmented`, `Pagination`, `Query`), `charts.jsx` (accessible lists / columns, no chart library), `CompanyPicker`, `RevealId`, `exportXlsx.js` (`ownerSheetRows` = the owner's 38 headers in order), `adminState.js` (`mergeSearch` keeps the unmanaged URL params such as `lang`), `testDb.js` (a fake supabase client for page tests that go through the real data layer).
- **Gotchas found**: (1) an `sr-only` (absolutely positioned) cell inside a scrolling table widens the whole page on a phone unless the scroll container is `position: relative`; (2) `beforeunload` (the unsaved-changes guard) blocks a scripted navigation in headless Edge until the dialog is accepted (`Page.handleJavaScriptDialog`); (3) a shell script heredoc in the tool halves `\\`: write scripts with a file tool; (4) PostgREST needs an explicit `GRANT EXECUTE` for every new function, and a function in `app_private` is not exposed even when granted (404 PGRST202 is the proof); (5) the company picker selects on `mousedown`, so a scripted `click()` does nothing there; (6) tailwind-merge swallows the custom font-size tokens: keep a size token in a plain `className`, not inside `cn()` with a competing size.
- **Not covered**: scheduled notifications, the invitation e-mail, the import commit, `merge_founders`, a vocabulary page.

## Review fixes (m52 + v52, 2026-10-14): what changed for every finding, deviations, open questions, handover

Independent reviewers examined m44 (certificates survive a withdrawal), m45 (image cleanup / OG / diagnostics) and m49 - m51 (the Alumni model and pages). This round fixed every blocking finding at the root and the cheap low ones. DB: `db/migrations/m52_review_fixes.sql` + `db/verify/v52_review_fixes.sql` (85 checks); details and the baseline in `db/MIGRATION-LOG.md`.

### Blocking findings (what I changed)
1. **HIGH, `save_node` raised 40001 (PostgREST retries it for ever)**: P0001 + hint `revision_conflict` + detail = the current rev (patched in place and in `m06b`); `web/src/data/errors.js` maps the token to the `conflict` code and the `node.conflict` text (`alumni_stale` too). Proved through the real PostgREST: a stale save answers HTTP 400 in 13 ms, no connection keeps spinning. v52 C01 scans every function for `errcode = '40001' | '40P01'`; v03 S04, v11 S04, v36 W12 expect P0001.
2. **MEDIUM, `db/test-from-scratch.sh` failed**: the reviewer saw v02 SEC.5; the real picture was wider (the committed v02 list was already not the stage-1 list, and v03 CE01b / CE01c needed m42 / m43). Fix: v02 SEC.5 is a RANGE check (subset of the designed list, all ten stage-1 functions present; v10 keeps the strict equality) and v03 CE01b / CE01c are stage-aware. `bash db/test-from-scratch.sh` prints FROM-SCRATCH TEST PASSED; db/README updated.
3. **MEDIUM, direct writes bypassed the RPC validation**: `founders` has no client INSERT / UPDATE any more; `companies` has column privileges (no `id` / `created_at` / `deleted_at` / `name_key`, `created_by` and `origin_project_id` insert-only) plus the trigger `companies_biu_write_guard` (urls, links, logo shape + own folder + the file exists, lengths, no soft delete, no foreign origin project, no duplicate name for a founder). Verified through the real PostgREST with a founder token: every scenario of the review is refused (400 / 403 / 409), a normal edit and the RPCs still work. **Deviation**: the reviewer preferred a plain REVOKE on `companies`; that would break the PM Lists-node path (v49 G01: a PM inserts a company directly), so the grants are per column and the trigger checks the rest. No unique index on `name_key`: the Athar team's duplicate-then-merge workflow (the 20 merges of m21) needs duplicates; only a FOUNDER is refused a duplicate name.
4. **MEDIUM, the M&E workspace showed skeletons for ever on an empty or failed table**: the decision order is now overview error, table pending, table error, empty, snapshots error, snapshots pending, grid (`useSnapshotRows` is disabled with no rows, so its `isPending` never ended). Tests: empty state, failed table with a working retry, failed yearly figures; browser: 0 alumni now shows "No company matches." (was six grey bars).
5. **MEDIUM, green text failed AA**: new token `--success-text` (152 65% 26%); Badge green, KPI "good", the valid-line count and the staff invitation "accepted" tone use it. Measured in the browser: 5.60:1 on the badge tint (was 3.80) and 6.34:1 on white (was 4.30).
6. **MEDIUM, docs**: the two status rows, the migrations table (m49 - m52), the stale README sentence and the chunk sizes are corrected (below).
7. **MEDIUM, a national ID inside a PM-readable table**: the overlay of one migrated selection item (a pasted text with two IDs) is redacted (`[ID removed]`, 1 row, one list node's revision +1); `app_private.scan_id_shaped_values()` scans every text / jsonb column of `public` and v52 C07 asserts nothing is found on the real data (the scan handles JSON line-break escapes: a number right after a line break is found). The design text (09 section 5.8) still has to say where the legacy answers really are: see "Not edited" below.

### Low findings
- **Fixed (DB)**: no national ID in a URL (`alumni_post_only`, HTTP 405 on GET / HEAD for the seven ID functions, `import_alumni_preview` VOLATILE; the m49 comment and `data/alumni.js` corrected); verify-suite brittleness (v01 compares only baseline-era accounts, v10 V10.4 tolerates later rows, v49 runs its totals on a rolled-back sandbox and C12 no longer demands the absence of alumni data: re-run green with a throwaway alumni company / snapshot / identity / account present); legacy mirrors (`companies_biu_mirror`: the key wins while set, merge_companies included); unknown founder counts are NULL (`alumni_team_stats` + export, manager table, company payload, directory page; UI shows a dash, Excel a blank cell).
- **Fixed (web)**: plural-aware counts (9 keys now plural objects used through `tn()`: "1 company", Arabic one / two / few / many; test `plurals.test.js`), Western digits in the manager Arabic dictionaries (test), destructive confirmations focus the safe button first, 44 px targets (chips, segmented controls, selects, icon buttons, "See all", the invitation company link, grid cells), the company picker closes on Tab / blur, forms (invalid e-mail message on blur, `aria-invalid` + `aria-describedby` + `role="alert"` in `Field`, `BilingualField` and the grid cells, `focusFirstInvalidSoon` on the sign-up and opportunity forms, `SkeletonList` with a status text), a title per page (`RouteTitle`: the first `<h1`, left alone when a page sets its own, e.g. public pages), translated nav labels + the Latin comma in English lists (`joinList`), the boot-path chunk (`badgeCount` moved to `lib/badge.js`: `lib/alumniFounder.js` and `lib/nationalId.js` are no longer in `routes`, checked with a source-map build), duplicated code (one `copyText` in `lib/clipboard.js` used by the Landing share, the alumni pages and the diagnostics page; `ChipGroup` replaced by `ChipSelect`; one RTL arrow pattern), dead exports removed (`targetSize`, `useAlumniDirectory`, `APPLICATION_STATUSES`, `createTestRouter`, `SOCIAL_KINDS` no longer exported), the manager header fits at 320 px (logo 96 px below 360 px; measured: button right edge 308 of 320, LTR and RTL), the inert invitation language control is gone (and Resend sends the UI language instead of a hard-coded `ar`), a notification is shown in the language of the page (a broadcast keeps both languages in its metadata), the founder Home shows a skeleton and a retry line instead of vanishing, the Excel export writes blank cells (`blanks: true`, proved with a real SheetJS round trip), the stray `web/alumni-2026-10-14.xlsx` deleted, `alumni.contract.md` says P0001 and the two new rules.
- **Not fixed, with the reason**: *invitation acceptance without a verified e-mail* (needs e-mail confirmation ON, which is a setting of the live project, and a deferred link at confirmation time: a feature, not a fix; pre-launch item below); *open sign-up weakens the "alumni" tier and the ID probe limiter* (CAPTCHA / confirmation are launch settings; a per-IP limit needs the edge layer); *founder overwrites of manager-reviewed figures have no history* (a design question for the owner); *design 09 says "no JavaScript ID rules"* (the client rules exist for instant feedback and are pinned by the appendix-A vectors; recorded as deviation B2 of the founder section, the design text still has to say so); *a second chart component and the generic Pagination / KpiCard / SectionCard / CheckRow living in `alumni-admin/ui.jsx`* (moving them is churn without a user benefit now); *Postgres segfault on a role-switched call of an ungranted function* (info only: the verify scripts already use catalog checks; consider a newer image); `resetEncoderStats` stays (a test hook of `lib/image/canvas.test.js`); the 141 migrated companies still have no CEO / age / gender data (the owner's import commit is not built: their counts are now blank, not 0).

- **Info only, recorded (no action)**: re-run m43 and m44 TOGETHER (m43 alone puts the attended condition back into the verifier); `notifications.body` is stored verbatim (the founder UI renders text; a future e-mail outbox must escape it); `opportunities.image_url` has no CHECK (the UI uses `safeHttpUrl`); `og-images` and `public-assets` are world-listable, so `alumni/<auth uid>/logo` reveals account UUIDs; the certificate verifier limit is not exploitable by one IP (blocked calls roll back their own counter).

### Handover
- **Rules**: see the "Review fixes" section of `web/README.md` (no 40001 / 40P01, POST-only ID functions, one write path, unknown is not zero, the accessibility helpers).
- **Gotchas found**: (1) PostgREST 14.7 sets `request.method` and runs a GET in a READ-ONLY transaction, a custom SQLSTATE `PTnnn` becomes HTTP nnn; (2) a column-level REVOKE does nothing while the role has the table-level grant: revoke the table privilege, then grant the column list (m52 builds it from `information_schema` at apply time, so a column added later is NOT client-writable until a migration grants it); (3) `jsonb::text` escapes a line break as backslash + n, which hides a following number from a "no letter next to it" regex: flatten first (`app_private.json_scan_text`); (4) a BEFORE trigger on `companies` also fires for the definer RPCs: the checks must accept what the RPCs write; (5) `regexp_replace` on `pg_get_functiondef` needs every anchor asserted, and a re-run of m49 / m50 / m51 undoes the in-place patches (re-run m52 after them); (6) stage-1 gates (v01 .. v03) now run on every build: keep new assertions in them stage-aware (`to_regclass` of a later table) or put them in a later verify file; (7) my tool layer collapses doubled backslashes in file contents and a heredoc over a few KB is cut: edit scripts were written with the file writer and used `String.fromCharCode(92)` for a backslash; Python is not installed on this machine (Node is); (8) `role="alert"` on every field error means `getByRole('alert')` can match several nodes in a test: query by text.
